2.55.20	hataṃ tvayā rājyam idaṃ sarāṣṭraṃ hatas tathātmā saha mantribhiś ca hatā saputrāsmi hatāś ca paurāḥ sutaś ca bhāryā ca tava prahṛṣṭau
2.55.21	imāṃ giraṃ dāruṇaśabdasaṃśritāṃ niśamya rājāpi mumoha duḥkhitaḥ tataḥ sa śokaṃ praviveśa pārthivaḥ svaduṣkṛtaṃ cāpi punas tadāsmarat
2.56.1	evaṃ tu kruddhayā rājā rāmamātrā saśokayā śrāvitaḥ paruṣaṃ vākyaṃ cintayām āsa duḥkhitaḥ
2.56.2	tasya cintayamānasya pratyabhāt karma duṣkṛtam yad anena kṛtaṃ pūrvam ajñānāc chabdavedhinā
2.56.3	amanās tena śokena rāmaśokena ca prabhuḥ dahyamānas tu śokābhyāṃ kausalyām āha bhūpatiḥ
2.56.4	prasādaye tvāṃ kausalye racito 'yaṃ mayāñjaliḥ vatsalā cānṛśaṃsā ca tvaṃ hi nityaṃ pareṣv api
2.56.5	bhartā tu khalu nārīṇāṃ guṇavān nirguṇo 'pi vā dharmaṃ vimṛśamānānāṃ pratyakṣaṃ devi daivatam
2.56.6	sā tvaṃ dharmaparā nityaṃ dṛṣṭalokaparāvara nārhase vipriyaṃ vaktuṃ duḥkhitāpi suduḥkhitam
2.56.7	tad vākyaṃ karuṇaṃ rājñaḥ śrutvā dīnasya bhāṣitam kausalyā vyasṛjad bāṣpaṃ praṇālīva navodakam
2.56.8	sa mūdrhṇi baddhvā rudatī rājñaḥ padmam ivāñjalim saṃbhramād abravīt trastā tvaramāṇākṣaraṃ vacaḥ
2.56.9	prasīda śirasā yāce bhūmau nitatitāsmi te yācitāsmi hatā deva hantavyāhaṃ na hi tvayā
2.56.10	naiṣā hi sā strī bhavati ślāghanīyena dhīmatā ubhayor lokayor vīra patyā yā saṃprasādyate
2.56.11	jānāmi dharmaṃ dharmajña tvāṃ jāne satyavādinam putraśokārtayā tat tu mayā kim api bhāṣitam
2.56.12	śoko nāśayate dhairyaṃ śoko nāśayate śrutam śoko nāśayate sarvaṃ nāsti śokasamo ripuḥ
2.56.13	śayam āpatitaḥ soḍhuṃ praharo ripuhastataḥ soḍhum āpatitaḥ śokaḥ susūkṣmo 'pi na śakyate
2.56.14	vanavāsāya rāmasya pañcarātro 'dya gaṇyate yaḥ śokahataharṣāyāḥ pañcavarṣopamo mama
2.56.15	taṃ hi cintayamānāyāḥ śoko 'yaṃ hṛdi vardhate adīnām iva vegena samudrasalilaṃ mahat
2.56.16	evaṃ hi kathayantyās tu kausalyāyāḥ śubhaṃ vacaḥ mandaraśmir abhūt suryo rajanī cābhyavartata
2.56.17	atha prahlādito vākyair devyā kausalyayā nṛpaḥ śokena ca samākrānto nidrāyā vaśam eyivān
2.57.1	pratibuddho muhur tena śokopahatacetanaḥ atha rājā daśarathaḥ sa cintām abhyapadyata
2.57.2	rāmalakṣmaṇayoś caiva vivāsād vāsavopamam āviveśopasargas taṃ tamaḥ sūryam ivāsuram
2.57.3	sa rājā rajanīṃ ṣaṣṭhīṃ rāme pravrajite vanam ardharātre daśarathaḥ saṃsmaran duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam kausalyāṃ putraśokārtām idaṃ vacanam abravīt
2.57.4	yad ācarati kalyāṇi śubhaṃ vā yadi vāśubham tad eva labhate bhadre kartā karmajam ātmanaḥ
2.57.5	guru lāghavam arthānām ārambhe karmaṇāṃ phalam doṣaṃ vā yo na jānāti sa bāla iti hocyate
2.57.6	kaś cid āmravaṇaṃ chittvā palāśāṃś ca niṣiñcati puṣpaṃ dṛṣṭvā phale gṛdhnuḥ sa śocati phalāgame
2.57.7	so 'ham āmravaṇaṃ chittvā palāśāṃś ca nyaṣecayam rāmaṃ phalāgame tyaktvā paścāc chocāmi durmatiḥ
2.57.8	labdhaśabdena kausalye kumāreṇa dhanuṣmatā kumāraḥ śabdavedhīti mayā pāpam idaṃ kṛtam tad idaṃ me 'nusaṃprāptaṃ devi duḥkhaṃ svayaṃ kṛtam
2.57.9	saṃmohād iha bālena yathā syād bhakṣitaṃ viṣam evaṃ mamāpy avijñātaṃ śabdavedhyamayaṃ phalam
2.57.10	devy anūḍhā tvam abhavo yuvarājo bhavāmy aham tataḥ prāvṛḍ anuprāptā madakāmavivardhinī
2.57.11	upāsyahi rasān bhaumāṃs taptvā ca jagad aṃśubhiḥ paretācaritāṃ bhīmāṃ ravir āviśate diśam
2.57.12	uṣṇam antardadhe sadyaḥ snigdhā dadṛśire ghanāḥ tato jahṛṣire sarve bhekasāraṅgabarhiṇaḥ
2.57.13	patitenāmbhasā channaḥ patamānena cāsakṛt ābabhau mattasāraṅgas toyarāśir ivācalaḥ
2.57.14	tasminn atisukhe kāle dhanuṣmān iṣumān rathī vyāyāma kṛtasaṃkalpaḥ sarayūm anvagāṃ nadīm
2.57.15	nipāne mahiṣaṃ rātrau gajaṃ vābhyāgataṃ nadīm anyaṃ vā śvāpadaṃ kaṃ cij jighāṃsur ajitendriyaḥ
2.57.16	athāndhakāre tv aśrauṣaṃ jale kumbhasya paryataḥ acakṣur viṣaye ghoṣaṃ vāraṇasyeva nardataḥ
2.57.17	tato 'haṃ śaram uddhṛtya dīptam āśīviṣopamam amuñcaṃ niśitaṃ bāṇam aham āśīviṣopamam
2.57.18	tatra vāg uṣasi vyaktā prādurāsīd vanaukasaḥ hā heti patatas toye vāg abhūt tatra mānuṣī katham asmadvidhe śastraṃ nipatet tu tapasvini
2.57.19	praviviktāṃ nadīṃ rātrāv udāhāro 'ham āgataḥ iṣuṇābhihataḥ kena kasya vā kiṃ kṛtaṃ mayā
2.57.20	ṛṣer hi nyasta daṇḍasya vane vanyena jīvataḥ kathaṃ nu śastreṇa vadho madvidhasya vidhīyate
2.57.21	jaṭābhāradharasyaiva valkalājinavāsasaḥ ko vadhena mamārthī syāt kiṃ vāsyāpakṛtaṃ mayā
2.57.22	evaṃ niṣphalam ārabdhaṃ kevalānarthasaṃhitam na kaś cit sādhu manyeta yathaiva gurutalpagam
2.57.23	nemaṃ tathānuśocāmi jīvitakṣayam ātmanaḥ mātaraṃ pitaraṃ cobhāv anuśocāmi madvidhe
2.57.24	tad etān mithunaṃ vṛddhaṃ cirakālabhṛtaṃ mayā mayi pañcatvam āpanne kāṃ vṛttiṃ vartayiṣyati
2.57.25	vṛddhau ca mātāpitarāv ahaṃ caikeṣuṇā hataḥ kena sma nihatāḥ sarve subālenākṛtātmanā
2.57.26	taṃ giraṃ karuṇāṃ śrutvā mama dharmānukāṅkṣiṇaḥ karābhyāṃ saśaraṃ cāpaṃ vyathitasyāpatad bhuvi
2.57.27	taṃ deśam aham āgamya dīnasattvaḥ sudurmanāḥ apaśyam iṣuṇā tīre sarayvās tāpasaṃ hatam
2.57.28	sa mām udvīkṣya netrābhyāṃ trastam asvasthacetasaṃ ity uvāca vacaḥ krūraṃ didhakṣann iva tejasā
2.57.29	kiṃ tavāpakṛtaṃ rājan vane nivasatā mayā jihīrṣur ambho gurvarthaṃ yad ahaṃ tāḍitas tvayā
2.57.30	ekena khalu bāṇena marmaṇy abhihate mayi dvāv andhau nihatau vṛddhau mātā janayitā ca me
2.57.31	tau nūnaṃ durbalāv andhau matpratīkṣau pipāsitau ciram āśākṛtāṃ tṛṣṇāṃ kaṣṭāṃ saṃdhārayiṣyataḥ
2.57.32	na nūnaṃ tapaso vāsti phalayogaḥ śrutasya vā pitā yan māṃ na jānāti śayānaṃ patitaṃ bhuvi
2.57.33	jānann api ca kiṃ kuryād aśaktir aparikramaḥ bhidyamānam ivāśaktas trātum anyo nago nagam
2.57.34	pitus tvam eva me gatvā śīghram ācakṣva rāghava na tvām anudahet kruddho vanaṃ vahnir ivaidhitaḥ
2.57.35	iyam ekapadī rājan yato me pitur āśramaḥ taṃ prasādaya gatvā tvaṃ na tvāṃ sa kupitaḥ śapet
2.57.36	viśalyaṃ kuru māṃ rājan marma me niśitaḥ śaraḥ ruṇaddhi mṛdu sotsedhaṃ tīram amburayo yathā
2.57.37	na dvijātir ahaṃ rājan mā bhūt te manaso vyathā śūdrāyām asmi vaiśyena jāto janapadādhipa
2.57.38	itīva vadataḥ kṛcchrād bāṇābhihatamarmaṇaḥ tasya tv ānamyamānasya taṃ bāṇam aham uddharam
2.57.39	jalārdragātraṃ tu vilapya kṛcchān marmavraṇaṃ saṃtatam ucchasantam tataḥ sarayvāṃ tam ahaṃ śayānaṃ samīkṣya bhadre subhṛśaṃ viṣaṇṇaḥ
2.58.1	tad ajñānān mahat pāpaṃ kṛtvā saṃkulitendriyaḥ ekas tv acintayaṃ buddhyā kathaṃ nu sukṛtaṃ bhavet
2.58.2	tatas taṃ ghaṭam ādaya pūrṇaṃ paramavāriṇā āśramaṃ tam ahaṃ prāpya yathākhyātapathaṃ gataḥ
2.58.3	tatrāhaṃ durbalāv andhau vṛddhāv apariṇāyakau apaśyaṃ tasya pitarau lūnapakṣāv iva dvijau
2.58.4	tannimittābhir āsīnau kathābhir aparikramau tām āśāṃ matkṛte hīnāv udāsīnāv anāthavat
2.58.5	padaśabdaṃ tu me śrutvā munir vākyam abhāṣata kiṃ cirāyasi me putra pānīyaṃ kṣipram ānaya
2.58.6	yannimittam idaṃ tāta salile krīḍitaṃ tvayā utkaṇṭhitā te māteyaṃ praviśa kṣipram āśramam
2.58.7	yad vyalīkaṃ kṛtaṃ putra mātrā te yadi vā mayā na tan manasi kartavyaṃ tvayā tāta tapasvinā
2.58.8	tvaṃ gatis tv agatīnāṃ ca cakṣus tvaṃ hīnacakṣuṣām samāsaktās tvayi prāṇāḥ kiṃ cin nau nābhibhāṣase
2.58.9	munim avyaktayā vācā tam ahaṃ sajjamānayā hīnavyañjanayā prekṣya bhīto bhīta ivābruvam
2.58.10	manasaḥ karma ceṣṭābhir abhisaṃstabhya vāgbalam ācacakṣe tv ahaṃ tasmai putravyasanajaṃ bhayam
2.58.11	kṣatriyo 'haṃ daśaratho nāhaṃ putro mahātmanaḥ sajjanāvamataṃ duḥkham idaṃ prāptaṃ svakarmajam
2.58.12	bhagavaṃś cāpahasto 'haṃ sarayūtīram āgataḥ jighāṃsuḥ śvāpadaṃ kiṃ cin nipāne vāgataṃ gajam
2.58.13	tatra śruto mayā śabdo jale kumbhasya pūryataḥ dvipo 'yam iti matvā hi bāṇenābhihato mayā
2.58.14	gatvā nadyās tatas tīram apaśyam iṣuṇā hṛdi vinirbhinnaṃ gataprāṇaṃ śayānaṃ bhuvi tāpasaṃ
2.58.15	bhagavañ śabdam ālakṣya mayā gajajighāṃsunā visṛṣṭo 'mbhasi nārācas tena te nihataḥ sutaḥ
2.58.16	sa coddhṛtena bāṇena tatraiva svargam āsthitaḥ bhagavantāv ubhau śocann andhāv iti vilapya ca
2.58.17	ajñānād bhavataḥ putraḥ sahasābhihato mayā śeṣam evaṃgate yat syāt tat prasīdatu me muniḥ
2.58.18	sa tac chrutvā vacaḥ krūraṃ niḥśvasañ śokakarśitaḥ mām uvāca mahātejāḥ kṛtāñjalim upasthitam
2.58.19	yady etad aśubhaṃ karma na sma me kathayeḥ svayam phalen mūrdhā sma te rājan sadyaḥ śatasahasradhā
2.58.20	kṣatriyeṇa vadho rājan vānaprasthe viśeṣataḥ jñānapūrvaṃ kṛtaḥ sthānāc cyāvayed api vajriṇam
2.58.21	ajñānād dhi kṛtaṃ yasmād idaṃ tenaiva jīvasi api hy adya kulaṃ nasyād rāghavāṇāṃ kuto bhavān
2.58.22	naya nau nṛpa taṃ deśam iti māṃ cābhyabhāṣata adya taṃ draṣṭum icchāvaḥ putraṃ paścimadarśanam
2.58.23	rudhireṇāvasitāṅgaṃ prakīrṇājinavāsasaṃ śayānaṃ bhuvi niḥsaṃjñaṃ dharmarājavaśaṃ gatam
2.58.24	athāham ekas taṃ deśaṃ nītvā tau bhṛśaduḥkhitau asparśayam ahaṃ putraṃ taṃ muniṃ saha bhāryayā
2.58.25	tau putram ātmanaḥ spṛṣṭvā tam āsādya tapasvinau nipetatuḥ śarīre 'sya pitā cāsyedam abravīt
2.58.26	na nv ahaṃ te priyaḥ putra mātaraṃ paśya dhārmika kiṃ nu nāliṅgase putra sukumāra vaco vada
2.58.27	kasya vāpararātre 'haṃ śroṣyāmi hṛdayaṃgamam adhīyānasya madhuraṃ śāstraṃ vānyad viśeṣataḥ
2.58.28	ko māṃ saṃdhyām upāsyaiva snātvā hutahutāśanaḥ ślāghayiṣyaty upāsīnaḥ putraśokabhayārditam
2.58.29	kandamūlaphalaṃ hṛtvā ko māṃ priyam ivātithim bhojayiṣyaty akarmaṇyam apragraham anāyakam
2.58.30	imām andhāṃ ca vṛddhāṃ ca mātaraṃ te tapasvinīm kathaṃ putra bhariṣyāmi kṛpaṇāṃ putragardhinīm
2.58.31	tiṣṭha mā mā gamaḥ putra yamasya sadanaṃ prati śvo mayā saha gantāsi jananyā ca samedhitaḥ
2.58.32	ubhāv api ca śokārtāv anāthau kṛpaṇau vane kṣipram eva gamiṣyāvas tvayā hīnau yamakṣayam
2.58.33	tato vaivasvataṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ pravakṣyāmi bhāratīm kṣamatāṃ dharmarājo me bibhṛyāt pitarāv ayam
2.58.34	apāpo 'si yathā putra nihataḥ pāpakarmaṇā tena satyena gacchāśu ye lokāḥ śastrayodhinām
2.58.35	yānti śūrā gatiṃ yāṃ ca saṃgrāmeṣv anivartinaḥ hatās tv abhimukhāḥ putra gatiṃ tāṃ paramāṃ vraja
2.58.36	yāṃ gatiṃ sagaraḥ śaibyo dilīpo janamejayaḥ nahuṣo dhundhumāraś ca prāptās tāṃ gaccha putraka
2.58.37	yā gatiḥ sarvasādhūnāṃ svādhyāyāt patasaś ca yā bhūmidasyāhitāgneś ca ekapatnīvratasya ca
2.58.38	gosahasrapradātṝṇāṃ yā yā gurubhṛtām api dehanyāsakṛtāṃ yā ca tāṃ gatiṃ gaccha putraka na hi tv asmin kule jāto gacchaty akuśalāṃ gatim
2.58.39	evaṃ sa kṛpaṇaṃ tatra paryadevayatāsakṛt tato 'smai kartum udakaṃ pravṛttaḥ saha bhāryayā
2.58.40	sa tu divyena rūpeṇa muniputraḥ svakarmabhiḥ āśvāsya ca muhūrtaṃ tu pitarau vākyam abravīt
2.58.41	sthānam asmi mahat prāpto bhavatoḥ paricāraṇāt bhavantāv api ca kṣipraṃ mama mūlam upaiṣyataḥ
2.58.42	evam uktvā tu divyena vimānena vapuṣmatā āruroha divaṃ kṣipraṃ muniputro jitendriyaḥ
2.58.43	sa kṛtvā tūdakaṃ tūrṇaṃ tāpasaḥ saha bhāryayā mām uvāca mahātejāḥ kṛtāñjalim upasthitam
2.58.44	adyaiva jahi māṃ rājan maraṇe nāsti me vyathā yac chareṇaikaputraṃ māṃ tvam akārṣīr aputrakam
2.58.45	tvayā tu yad avijñānān nihato me sutaḥ śuciḥ tena tvām abhiśapsyāmi suduḥkham atidāruṇam
2.58.46	putravyasanajaṃ duḥkhaṃ yad etan mama sāmpratam evaṃ tvaṃ putraśokena rājan kālaṃ kariṣyasi
2.58.47	tasmān mām āgataṃ bhadre tasyodārasya tad vacaḥ yad ahaṃ putraśokena saṃtyakṣyāmy adya jīvitam
2.58.48	yadi māṃ saṃspṛśed rāmaḥ sakṛd adyālabheta vā na tan me sadṛśaṃ devi yan mayā rāghave kṛtam
2.58.49	cakṣuṣā tvāṃ na paśyāmi smṛtir mama vilupyate dūtā vaivasvatasyaite kausalye tvarayanti mām
2.58.50	atas tu kiṃ duḥkhataraṃ yad ahaṃ jīvitakṣaye na hi paśyāmi dharmajñaṃ rāmaṃ satyaparākyamam
2.58.51	na te manuṣyā devās te ye cāruśubhakuṇḍalam mukhaṃ drakṣyanti rāmasya varṣe pañcadaśe punaḥ
2.58.52	padmapatrekṣaṇaṃ subhru sudaṃṣṭraṃ cārunāsikam dhanyā drakṣyanti rāmasya tārādhipanibhaṃ mukham
2.58.53	sadṛśaṃ śāradasyendoḥ phullasya kamalasya ca sugandhi mama nāthasya dhanyā drakṣyanti tanmukham
2.58.54	nivṛttavanavāsaṃ tam ayodhyāṃ punar āgatam drakṣyanti sukhino rāmaṃ śukraṃ mārgagataṃ yathā
2.58.55	ayam ātmabhavaḥ śoko mām anātham acetanam saṃsādayati vegena yathā kūlaṃ nadīrayaḥ
2.58.56	hā rāghava mahābāho hā mamāyāsa nāśana rājā daśarathaḥ śocañ jīvitāntam upāgamat
2.58.57	tathā tu dīnaṃ kathayan narādhipaḥ priyasya putrasya vivāsanāturaḥ gate 'rdharātre bhṛśaduḥkhapīḍitas tadā jahau prāṇam udāradarśanaḥ
2.59.1	atha rātryāṃ vyatītāyāṃ prātar evāpare 'hani bandinaḥ paryupātiṣṭhaṃs tat pārthivaniveśanam
2.59.2	tataḥ śucisamācārāḥ paryupasthāna kovidaḥ strīvarṣavarabhūyiṣṭhā upatasthur yathāpuram
2.59.3	haricandanasaṃpṛktam udakaṃ kāñcanair ghaṭaiḥ āninyuḥ snānaśikṣājñā yathākālaṃ yathāvidhi
2.59.4	maṅgalālambhanīyāni prāśanīyān upaskarān upaninyus tathāpy anyāḥ kumārī bahulāḥ striyaḥ
2.59.5	atha yāḥ kosalendrasya śayanaṃ pratyanantarāḥ tāḥ striyas tu samāgamya bhartāraṃ pratyabodhayan
2.59.6	tā vepathuparītāś ca rājñaḥ prāṇeṣu śaṅkitāḥ pratisrotas tṛṇāgrāṇāṃ sadṛśaṃ saṃcakampire
2.59.7	atha saṃvepamanānāṃ strīṇāṃ dṛṣṭvā ca pārthivam yat tad āśaṅkitaṃ pāpaṃ tasya jajñe viniścayaḥ
2.59.8	tataḥ pracukruśur dīnāḥ sasvaraṃ tā varāṅganāḥ kareṇava ivāraṇye sthānapracyutayūthapāḥ
2.59.9	tāsām ākranda śabdena sahasodgatacetane kausalyā ca sumitrāca tyaktanidre babhūvatuḥ
2.59.10	kausalyā ca sumitrā ca dṛṣṭvā spṛṣṭvā ca pārthivam hā nātheti parikruśya petatur dharaṇītale
2.59.11	sā kosalendraduhitā veṣṭamānā mahītale na babhrāja rajodhvastā tāreva gaganacyutā
2.59.12	tat samuttrastasaṃbhrāntaṃ paryutsukajanākulam sarvatas tumulākrandaṃ paritāpārtabāndhavam
2.59.13	sadyo nipatitānandaṃ dīnaviklavadarśanam babhūva naradevasya sadma diṣṭāntam īyuṣaḥ
2.59.14	atītam ājñāya tu pārthivarṣabhaṃ yaśasvinaṃ saṃparivārya patnayaḥ bhṛśaṃ rudantyaḥ karuṇaṃ suduḥkhitāḥ pragṛhya bāhū vyalapann anāthavat
2.60.1	tam agnim iva saṃśāntam ambuhīnam ivārṇavam hataprabham ivādityaṃ svargathaṃ prekṣya bhūmipam
2.60.2	kausalyā bāṣpapūrṇākṣī vividhaṃ śokakarśitā upagṛhya śiro rājñaḥ kaikeyīṃ pratyabhāṣata
2.60.3	sakāmā bhava kaikeyi bhuṅkṣva rājyam akaṇṭakam tyaktvā rājānam ekāgrā nṛśaṃse duṣṭacāriṇi
2.60.4	vihāya māṃ gato rāmo bhartā ca svargato mama vipathe sārthahīneva nāhaṃ jīvitum utsahe
2.60.5	bhartāraṃ taṃ parityajya kā strī daivatam ātmanaḥ icchej jīvitum anyatra kaikeyyās tyaktadharmaṇaḥ
2.60.6	na lubdho budhyate doṣān kiṃ pākam iva bhakṣayan kubjānimittaṃ kaikeyyā rāghavāṇān kulaṃ hatam
2.60.7	aniyoge niyuktena rājñā rāmaṃ vivāsitam sabhāryaṃ janakaḥ śrutvā paritapsyaty ahaṃ yathā
2.60.8	rāmaḥ kamalapatrākṣo jīvanāśam ito gataḥ videharājasya sutā tahā sītā tapasvinī duḥkhasyānucitā duḥkhaṃ vane paryudvijiṣyati
2.60.9	nadatāṃ bhīmaghoṣāṇāṃ niśāsu mṛgapakṣiṇām niśamya nūnaṃ saṃstrastā rāghavaṃ saṃśrayiṣyati
2.60.10	vṛddhaś caivālpaputraś ca vaidehīm anicintayan so 'pi śokasamāviṣṭo nanu tyakṣyati jīvitam
2.60.11	tāṃ tataḥ saṃpariṣvajya vilapantīṃ tapasvinīm vyapaninyuḥ suduḥkhārtāṃ kausalyāṃ vyāvahārikāḥ
2.60.12	tailadroṇyām athāmātyāḥ saṃveśya jagatīpatim rājñaḥ sarvāṇy athādiṣṭāś cakruḥ karmāṇy anantaram
2.60.13	na tu saṃkalanaṃ rājño vinā putreṇa mantriṇaḥ sarvajñāḥ kartum īṣus te tato rakṣanti bhūmipam
2.60.14	tailadroṇyāṃ tu sacivaiḥ śāyitaṃ taṃ narādhipam hā mṛto 'yam iti jñātvā striyas tāḥ paryadevayan
2.60.15	bāhūn udyamya kṛpaṇā netraprasravaṇair mukhaiḥ rudantyaḥ śokasaṃtaptāḥ kṛpaṇaṃ paryadevayan
2.60.16	niśānakṣatrahīneva strīva bhartṛvivarjitā purī nārājatāyodhyā hīnā rājñā mahātmanā
2.60.17	bāṣpaparyākulajanā hāhābhūtakulāṅganā śūnyacatvaraveśmāntā na babhrāja yathāpuram
2.60.18	gataprabhā dyaur iva bhāskaraṃ vinā vyapetanakṣatragaṇeva śarvarī purī babhāse rahitā mahātmanā na cāsrakaṇṭhākulamārgacatvarā
2.60.19	narāś ca nāryaś ca sametya saṃghaśo vigarhamāṇā bharatasya mātaram tadā nagaryāṃ naradevasaṃkṣaye babhūvur ārtā na ca śarma lebhire
2.61.1	vyatītāyāṃ tu śarvaryām ādityasyodaye tataḥ sametya rājakartāraḥ sabhām īyur dvijātayaḥ
2.61.2	mārkaṇḍeyo 'tha maudgalyo vāmadevaś ca kāśyapaḥ kātyayano gautamaś ca jābāliś ca mahāyaśāḥ
2.61.3	ete dvijāḥ sahāmātyaiḥ pṛthag vācam udīrayan vasiṣṭham evābhimukhāḥ śreṣṭho rājapurohitam
2.61.4	atītā śarvarī duḥkhaṃ yā no varṣaśatopamā asmin pañcatvam āpanne putraśokena pārthive
2.61.5	svargataś ca mahārājo rāmaś cāraṇyam āśritaḥ lakṣmaṇaś cāpi tejasvī rāmeṇaiva gataḥ saha
2.61.6	ubhau bharataśatrughnau kkekayeṣu paraṃtapau pure rājagṛhe ramye mātāmahaniveśane
2.61.7	ikṣvākūṇām ihādyaiva kaś cid rājā vidhīyatām arājakaṃ hi no rāṣṭraṃ na vināśam avāpnuyāt
2.61.8	nārājale janapade vidyunmālī mahāsvanaḥ abhivarṣati parjanyo mahīṃ divyena vāriṇā
2.61.9	nārājake janapade bījamuṣṭiḥ prakīryate nārākake pituḥ putro bhāryā vā vartate vaśe
2.61.10	arājake dhanaṃ nāsti nāsti bhāryāpy arājake idam atyāhitaṃ cānyat kutaḥ satyam arājake
2.61.11	nārājake janapade kārayanti sabhāṃ narāḥ udyānāni ca ramyāṇi hṛṣṭāḥ puṇyagṛhāṇi ca
2.61.12	nārājake janapade yajñaśīlā dvijātayaḥ satrāṇy anvāsate dāntā brāhmaṇāḥ saṃśitavratāḥ
2.61.13	nārājake janapade prabhūtanaṭanartakāḥ utsavāś ca samājāś ca vardhante rāṣṭravardhanāḥ
2.61.14	nārajake janapade siddhārthā vyavahāriṇaḥ kathābhir anurajyante kathāśīlāḥ kathāpriyaiḥ
2.61.15	nārājake janapade vāhanaiḥ śīghragāmibhiḥ narā niryānty araṇyāni nārībhiḥ saha kāminaḥ
2.61.16	nārākaje janapade dhanavantaḥ surakṣitāḥ śerate vivṛta dvārāḥ kṛṣigorakṣajīvinaḥ
2.61.17	nārājake janapade vaṇijo dūragāminaḥ gacchanti kṣemam adhvānaṃ bahupuṇyasamācitāḥ
2.61.18	nārājake janapade caraty ekacaro vaśī bhāvayann ātmanātmānaṃ yatrasāyaṃgṛho muniḥ
2.61.19	nārājake janapade yogakṣemaṃ pravartate na cāpy arājake senā śatrūn viṣahate yudhi
2.61.20	yathā hy anudakā nadyo yathā vāpy atṛṇaṃ vanam agopālā yathā gāvas tathā rāṣṭram arājakam
2.61.21	nārājake janapade svakaṃ bhavati kasya cit matsyā iva narā nityaṃ bhakṣayanti parasparam
2.61.22	yehi saṃbhinnamaryādā nāstikāś chinnasaṃśayāḥ te 'pi bhāvāya kalpante rājadaṇḍanipīḍitāḥ
2.61.23	aho tama ivedaṃ syān na prajñāyeta kiṃ cana rājā cen na bhaveṃl loke vibhajan sādhvasādhunī
2.61.24	jīvaty api mahārāje tavaiva vacanaṃ vayam nātikramāmahe sarve velāṃ prāpyeva sāgaraḥ
2.61.25	sa naḥ samīkṣya dvijavaryavṛttaṃ nṛpaṃ vinā rājyam araṇyabhūtam kumāram ikṣvākusutaṃ vadānyaṃ tvam eva rājānam ihābhiṣiñcaya
2.62.1	teṣāṃ tad vacanaṃ śrutvā vasiṣṭhaḥ pratyuvāca ha mitrāmātyagaṇān sarvān brāhmaṇāṃs tān idaṃ vacaḥ
2.62.2	yad asau mātulakule pure rājagṛhe sukhī bharato vasati bhrātrā śatrughnena samanvitaḥ
2.62.3	tac chīghraṃ javanā dūtā gacchantu tvaritair hayaiḥ ānetuṃ bhrātarau vīrau kiṃ samīkṣāmahe vayam
2.62.4	gacchantv iti tataḥ sarve vasiṣṭhaṃ vākyam abruvan teṣāṃ tad vacanaṃ śrutvā vasiṣṭho vākyam abravīt
2.62.5	ehi siddhārtha vijaya jayantāśokanandana śrūyatām itikartavyaṃ sarvān eva bravīmi vaḥ
2.62.6	puraṃ rājagṛhaṃ gatvā śīghraṃ śīghrajavair hayaiḥ tyaktaśokair idaṃ vācyaḥ śāsanād bharato mama
2.62.7	purohitas tvāṃ kuśalaṃ prāha sarve ca mantriṇaḥ tvaramāṇaś ca niryāhi kṛtyam ātyayikaṃ tvayā
2.62.8	mā cāsmai proṣitaṃ rāmaṃ mā cāsmai pitaraṃ mṛtam bhavantaḥ śaṃsiṣur gatvā rāghavāṇām imaṃ kṣayam
2.62.9	kauśeyāni ca vastrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni varāṇi ca kṣipram ādāya rājñaś ca bharatasya ca gacchata vasiṣṭhenābhyanujñātā dūtāḥ saṃtvaritā yayuḥ
2.62.10	te hastina pure gaṅgāṃ tīrtvā pratyaṅmukhā yayuḥ pāñcāladeśam āsādya madhyena kurujāṅgalam
2.62.11	te prasannodakāṃ divyāṃ nānāvihagasevitām upātijagmur vegena śaradaṇḍāṃ janākulām
2.62.12	nikūlavṛkṣam āsādya divyaṃ satyopayācanam abhigamyābhivādyaṃ taṃ kuliṅgāṃ prāviśan purīm
2.62.13	abhikālaṃ tataḥ prāpya tejo'bhibhavanāc cyutāḥ yayur madhyena bāhlīkān sudāmānaṃ ca parvatam viṣṇoḥ padaṃ prekṣamāṇā vipāśāṃ cāpi śālmalīm
2.62.14	te śrāntavāhanā dūtā vikṛṣṭena satā pathā giri vrajaṃ pura varaṃ śīghram āsedur añjasā
2.62.15	bhartuḥ priyārthaṃ kularakṣaṇārthaṃ bhartuś ca vaṃśasya parigrahārtham aheḍamānās tvarayā sma dūtā rātryāṃ tu te tat puram eva yātāḥ
2.63.1	yām eva rātriṃ te dūtāḥ praviśanti sma tāṃ purīm bharatenāpi tāṃ rātriṃ svapno dṛṣṭo 'yam apriyaḥ
2.63.2	vyuṣṭām eva tu tāṃ rātriṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ svapnam apriyam putro rājādhirājasya subhṛśaṃ paryatapyata
2.63.3	tapyamānaṃ samājñāya vayasyāḥ priyavādinaḥ āyāsaṃ hi vineṣyantaḥ sabhāyāṃ cakrire kathāḥ
2.63.4	vādayanti tathā śāntiṃ lāsayanty api cāpare nāṭakāny apare prāhur hāsyāni vividhāni ca
2.63.5	sa tair mahātmā bharataḥ sakhibhiḥ priya vādibhiḥ goṣṭhīhāsyāni kurvadbhir na prāhṛṣyata rāghavaḥ
2.63.6	tam abravīt priyasakho bharataṃ sakhibhir vṛtam suhṛdbhiḥ paryupāsīnaḥ kiṃ sakhe nānumodase
2.63.7	evaṃ bruvāṇaṃ suhṛdaṃ bharataḥ pratyuvāca ha śṛṇu tvaṃ yan nimittaṃme dainyam etad upāgatam
2.63.8	svapne pitaram adrākṣaṃ malinaṃ muktamūrdhajam patantam adriśikharāt kaluṣe gomaye hrade
2.63.9	plavamānaś ca me dṛṣṭaḥ sa tasmin gomayahrade pibann añjalinā tailaṃ hasann iva muhur muhuḥ
2.63.10	tatas tilodanaṃ bhuktvā punaḥ punar adhaḥśirāḥ tailenābhyaktasarvāṅgas tailam evāvagāhata
2.63.11	svapne 'pi sāgaraṃ śuṣkaṃ candraṃ ca patitaṃ bhuvi sahasā cāpi saṃśantaṃ jvalitaṃ jātavedasaṃ
2.63.12	avadīrṇāṃ ca pṛthivīṃ śuṣkāṃś ca vividhān drumān ahaṃ paśyāmi vidhvastān sadhūmāṃś caiva pārvatān
2.63.13	pīṭhe kārṣṇāyase cainaṃ niṣaṇṇaṃ kṛṣṇavāsasaṃ prahasanti sma rājānaṃ pramadāḥ kṛṣṇapiṅgalāḥ
2.63.14	tvaramāṇaś ca dharmātmā raktamālyānulepanaḥ rathena kharayuktena prayāto dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ
2.63.15	evam etan mayā dṛṣṭam imāṃ rātriṃ bhayāvahām ahaṃ rāmo 'tha vā rājā lakṣmaṇo vā mariṣyati
2.63.16	naro yānena yaḥ svapne kharayuktena yāti hi acirāt tasya dhūmāgraṃ citāyāṃ saṃpradṛśyate etannimittaṃ dīno 'haṃ tan na vaḥ pratipūjaye
2.63.17	śuṣyatīva ca me kaṇṭho na svastham iva me manaḥ jugupsann iva cātmānaṃ na ca paśyāmi kāraṇam
2.63.18	imāṃ hi duḥsvapnagatiṃ niśāmya tām anekarūpām avitarkitāṃ purā bhayaṃ mahat tad dhṛdayān na yāti me vicintya rājānam acintyadarśanam
2.64.1	bharate bruvati svapnaṃ dūtās te klāntavāhanāḥ praviśyāsahyaparikhaṃ ramyaṃ rājagṛhaṃ puram
2.64.2	samāgamya tu rājñā ca rājaputreṇa cārcitāḥ rājñaḥ pādau gṛhītvā tu tam ūcur bharataṃ vacaḥ
2.64.3	purohitas tvā kuśalaṃ prāha sarve ca mantriṇaḥ tvaramāṇaś ca niryāhi kṛtyam ātyayikaṃ tvayā
2.64.4	atra viṃśatikoṭyas tu nṛpater mātulasya te daśakoṭyas tu saṃpūrṇās tathaiva ca nṛpātmaja
2.64.5	pratigṛhya ca tat sarvaṃ svanuraktaḥ suhṛjjane dūtān uvāca bharataḥ kāmaiḥ saṃpratipūjya tān
2.64.6	kaccit sukuśalī rājā pitā daśaratho mama kaccic cārāgatā rāme lakṣmaṇe vā mahātmani
2.64.7	āryā ca dharmaniratā dharmajñā dharmadarśinī arogā cāpi kausalyā mātā rāmasya dhīmataḥ
2.64.8	kaccit sumitrā dharmajñā jananī lakṣmaṇasya yā śatrughnasya ca vīrasya sārogā cāpi madhyamā
2.64.9	ātmakāmā sadā caṇḍī krodhanā prājñamāninī arogā cāpi kaikeyī mātā me kim uvāca ha
2.64.10	evam uktās tu te dūtā bharatena mahātmanā ūcuḥ saṃpraśritaṃ vākyam idaṃ taṃ bharataṃ tadā kuśalās te naravyāghra yeṣāṃ kuśalam icchasi
2.64.11	bharataś cāpi tān dūtān evam ukto 'bhyabhāṣata āpṛcche 'haṃ mahārājaṃ dūtāḥ saṃtvarayanti mām
2.64.12	evam uktvā tu tān dūtān bharataḥ pārthivātmajaḥ dūtaiḥ saṃcodito vākyaṃ mātāmaham uvāca ha
2.64.13	rājan pitur gamiṣyāmi sakāśaṃ dūtacoditaḥ punar apy aham eṣyāmi yadā me tvaṃ smariṣyasi
2.64.14	bharatenaivam uktas tu nṛpo mātāmahas tadā tam uvāca śubhaṃ vākyaṃ śirasy āghrāya rāghavam
2.64.15	gaccha tātānujāne tvāṃ kaikeyī suprajās tvayā mātaraṃ kuśalaṃ brūyāḥ pitaraṃ ca paraṃtapa
2.64.16	purohitaṃ ca kuśalaṃ ye cānye dvijasattamāḥ tau ca tāta maheṣvāsau bhrātaru rāmalakṣmaṇau
2.64.17	tasmai hastyuttamāṃś citrān kambalān ajināni ca abhisatkṛtya kaikeyo bharatāya dhanaṃ dadau
2.64.18	rukma niṣkasahasre dve ṣoḍaśāśvaśatāni ca satkṛtya kaikeyī putraṃ kekayo dhanam ādiśat
2.64.19	tathāmātyān abhipretān viśvāsyāṃś ca guṇānvitān dadāv aśvapatiḥ śīghraṃ bharatāyānuyāyinaḥ
2.64.20	airāvatān aindraśirān nāgān vai priyadarśanān kharāñ śīghrān susaṃyuktān mātulo 'smai dhanaṃ dadau
2.64.21	antaḥpure 'tisaṃvṛddhān vyāghravīryabalānvitān daṃṣṭrāyudhān mahākāyāñ śunaś copāyanaṃ dadau
2.64.22	sa mātāmaham āpṛcchya mātulaṃ ca yudhājitam ratham āruhya bharataḥ śatrughnasahito yayau
2.64.23	rathān maṇḍalacakrāṃś ca yojayitvā paraḥśatam uṣṭrago'śvakharair bhṛtyā bharataṃ yāntam anvayuḥ
2.64.24	balena gupto bharato mahātmā sahāryakasyātmasamair amātyaiḥ ādāya śatrughnam apetaśatrur gṛhād yayau siddha ivendralokāt
2.65.1	sa prāṅmukho rājagṛhād abhiniryāya vīryavān hrādinīṃ dūrapārāṃ ca pratyaksrotas taraṅgiṇīm śatadrūm atarac chrīmān nadīm ikṣvākunandanaḥ
2.65.2	eladhāne nadīṃ tīrtvā prāpya cāparaparpaṭān śilām ākurvatīṃ tīrtvā āgneyaṃ śalyakartanam
2.65.3	satyasaṃdhaḥ śuciḥ śrīmān prekṣamāṇaḥ śilāvahām atyayāt sa mahāśailān vanaṃ caitrarathaṃ prati
2.65.4	veginīṃ ca kuliṅgākhyāṃ hrādinīṃ parvatāvṛtām yamunāṃ prāpya saṃtīrṇo balam āśvāsayat tadā
2.65.5	śītīkṛtya tu gātrāṇi klāntān āśvāsya vājinaḥ tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca prāyād ādāya codakam
2.65.6	rājaputro mahāraṇyam anabhīkṣṇopasevitam bhadro bhadreṇa yānena mārutaḥ kham ivātyayāt
2.65.7	toraṇaṃ dakṣiṇārdhena jambūprastham upāgamat varūthaṃ ca yayau ramyaṃ grāmaṃ daśarathātmajaḥ
2.65.8	tatra ramye vane vāsaṃ kṛtvāsau prāṅmukho yayau udyānam ujjihānāyāḥ priyakā yatra pādapāḥ
2.65.9	sālāṃs tu priyakān prāpya śīghrān āsthāya vājinaḥ anujñāpyātha bharato vāhinīṃ tvarito yayau
2.65.10	vāsaṃ kṛtvā sarvatīrthe tīrtvā cottānakāṃ nadīm anyā nadīś ca vividhāḥ pārvatīyais turaṃgamaiḥ
2.65.11	hastipṛṣṭhakam āsādya kuṭikām atyavartata tatāra ca naravyāghro lauhitye sa kapīvatīm ekasāle sthāṇumatīṃ vinate gomatīṃ nadīm
2.65.12	kaliṅga nagare cāpi prāpya sālavanaṃ tadā bharataḥ kṣipram āgacchat supariśrāntavāhanaḥ
2.65.13	vanaṃ ca samatītyāśu śarvaryām aruṇodaye ayodhyāṃ manunā rājñā nirmitāṃ sa dadarśa ha
2.65.14	tāṃ purīṃ puruṣavyāghraḥ saptarātroṣiṭaḥ pathi ayodhyām agrato dṛṣṭvā rathe sārathim abravīt
2.65.15	eṣā nātipratītā me puṇyodyānā yaśasvinī ayodhyā dṛśyate dūrāt sārathe pāṇḍumṛttikā
2.65.16	yajvabhir guṇasaṃpannair brāhmaṇair vedapāragaiḥ bhūyiṣṭham ṛddhair ākīrṇā rājarṣivarapālitā
2.65.17	ayodhyāyāṃ purāśabdaḥ śrūyate tumulo mahān samantān naranārīṇāṃ tam adya na śṛṇomy aham
2.65.18	udyānāni hi sāyāhne krīḍitvoparatair naraiḥ samantād vipradhāvadbhiḥ prakāśante mamānyadā
2.65.19	tāny adyānurudantīva parityaktāni kāmibhiḥ araṇyabhūteva purī sārathe pratibhāti me
2.65.20	na hy atra yānair dṛśyante na gajair na ca vājibhiḥ niryānto vābhiyānto vā naramukhyā yathāpuram
2.65.21	aniṣṭāni ca pāpāni paśyāmi vividhāni ca nimittāny amanojñāni tena sīdati te manaḥ
2.65.22	dvāreṇa vaijayantena prāviśac chrāntavāhanaḥ dvāḥsthair utthāya vijayaṃ pṛṣṭas taiḥ sahito yayau
2.65.23	sa tv anekāgrahṛdayo dvāḥsthaṃ pratyarcya taṃ janam sūtam aśvapateḥ klāntam abravīt tatra rāghavaḥ
2.65.24	śrutā no yādṛśāḥ pūrvaṃ nṛpatīnāṃ vināśane ākārās tān ahaṃ sarvān iha paśyāmi sārathe
2.65.25	malinaṃ cāśrupūrṇākṣaṃ dīnaṃ dhyānaparaṃ kṛśam sastrī puṃsaṃ ca paśyāmi janam utkaṇṭhitaṃ pure
2.65.26	ity evam uktvā bharataḥ sūtaṃ taṃ dīnamānasaḥ tāny aniṣṭāny ayodhyāyāṃ prekṣya rājagṛhaṃ yayau
2.65.27	tāṃ śūnyaśṛṅgāṭakaveśmarathyāṃ rajo'ruṇadvārakapāṭayantrām dṛṣṭvā purīm indrapurī prakāśāṃ duḥkhena saṃpūrṇataro babhūva
2.65.28	bahūni paśyan manaso 'priyāṇi yāny annyadā nāsya pure babhūvuḥ avākśirā dīnamanā nahṛṣṭaḥ pitur mahātmā praviveśa veśma
2.66.1	apaśyaṃs tu tatas tatra pitaraṃ pitur ālaye jagāma bharato draṣṭuṃ mātaraṃ mātur ālaye
2.66.2	anuprāptaṃ tu taṃ dṛṣṭvā kaikeyī proṣitaṃ sutam utpapāta tadā hṛṣṭā tyaktvā sauvarṇamānasaṃ
2.66.3	sa praviśyaiva dharmātmā svagṛhaṃ śrīvivarjitam bharataḥ prekṣya jagrāha jananyāś caraṇau śubhau
2.66.4	taṃ mūrdhni samupāghrāya pariṣvajya yaśasvinam aṅke bharatam āropya praṣṭuṃ samupacakrame
2.66.5	adya te kati cid rātryaś cyutasyāryakaveśmanaḥ api nādhvaśramaḥ śīghraṃ rathenāpatatas tava
2.66.6	āryakas te sukuśalo yudhājin mātulas tava pravāsāc ca sukhaṃ putra sarvaṃ me vaktum arhasi
2.66.7	evaṃ pṛṣṭhas tu kaikeyyā priyaṃ pārthivanandanaḥ ācaṣṭa bharataḥ sarvaṃ mātre rājīvalocanaḥ
2.66.8	adya me saptamī rātriś cyutasyāryakaveśmanaḥ ambāyāḥ kuśalī tāto yudhājin mātulaś ca me
2.66.9	yan me dhanaṃ ca ratnaṃ ca dadau rājā paraṃtapaḥ pariśrāntaṃ pathy abhavat tato 'haṃ pūrvam āgataḥ
2.66.10	rājavākyaharair dūtais tvaryamāṇo 'ham āgataḥ yad ahaṃ praṣṭum icchāmi tad ambā vaktum arhasi
2.66.11	śūnyo 'yaṃ śayanīyas te paryaṅko hemabhūṣitaḥ na cāyam ikṣvākujanaḥ prahṛṣṭaḥ pratibhāti me
2.66.12	rājā bhavati bhūyiṣṭham ihāmbāyā niveśane tam ahaṃ nādya paśyāmi draṣṭum icchann ihāgataḥ
2.66.13	pitur grahīṣye caraṇau taṃ mamākhyāhi pṛcchataḥ āhosvid amba jyeṣṭhāyāḥ kausalyāyā niveśane
2.66.14	taṃ pratyuvāca kaikeyī priyavad ghoram apriyam ajānantaṃ prajānantī rājyalobhena mohitā yā gatiḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ tāṃ gatiṃ te pitā gataḥ
2.66.15	tac chrutvā bharato vākyaṃ dharmābhijanavāñ śuciḥ papāta sahasā bhūmau pitṛśokabalārditaḥ
2.66.16	tataḥ śokena saṃvītaḥ pitur maraṇaduḥkhitaḥ vilalāpa mahātejā bhrāntākulitacetanaḥ
2.66.17	etat suruciraṃ bhāti pitur me śayanaṃ purā tad idaṃ na vibhāty adya vihīnaṃ tena dhīmatā
2.66.18	tam ārtaṃ devasaṃkāśaṃ samīkṣya patitaṃ bhuvi utthāpayitvā śokārtaṃ vacanaṃ cedam abravīt
2.66.19	uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha kiṃ śeṣe rājaputra mahāyaśaḥ tvadvidhā na hi śocanti santaḥ sadasi saṃmatāḥ
2.66.20	sa rudatyā ciraṃ kālaṃ bhūmau viparivṛtya ca jananīṃ pratyuvācedaṃ śokair bahubhir āvṛtaḥ
2.66.21	abhiṣekṣyati rāmaṃ tu rājā yajñaṃ nu yakṣyati ity ahaṃ kṛtasaṃkalpo hṛṣṭo yātrām ayāsiṣam
2.66.22	tad idaṃ hy anyathā bhūtaṃ vyavadīrṇaṃ mano mama pitaraṃ yo na paśyāmi nityaṃ priyahite ratam
2.66.23	amba kenātyagād rājā vyādhinā mayy anāgate dhanyā rāmādayaḥ sarve yaiḥ pitā saṃskṛtaḥ svayam
2.66.24	na nūnaṃ māṃ mahārājaḥ prāptaṃ jānāti kīrtimān upajighred dhi māṃ mūrdhni tātaḥ saṃnamya satvaram
2.66.25	kva sa pāṇiḥ sukhasparśas tātasyākliṣṭakarmaṇaḥ yena māṃ rajasā dhvastam abhīkṣṇaṃ parimārjati
2.66.26	yo me bhrātā pitā bandhur yasya dāso 'smi dhīmataḥ tasya māṃ śīghram ākhyāhi rāmasyākliṣṭa karmaṇaḥ
2.66.27	pitā hi bhavati jyeṣṭho dharmam āryasya jānataḥ tasya pādau grahīṣyāmi sa hīdānīṃ gatir mama
2.66.28	ārye kim abravīd rājā pitā me satyavikramaḥ paścimaṃ sādhu saṃdeśam icchāmi śrotum ātmanaḥ
2.66.29	iti pṛṣṭā yathātattvaṃ kaikeyī vākyam abravīt rāmeti rājā vilapan hā sīte lakṣmaṇeti ca sa mahātmā paraṃ lokaṃ gato gatimatāṃ varaḥ
2.66.30	imāṃ tu paścimāṃ vācaṃ vyājahāra pitā tava kāla dharmaparikṣiptaḥ pāśair iva mahāgajaḥ
2.66.31	siddhārthās tu narā rāmam āgataṃ sītayā saha lakṣmaṇaṃ ca mahābāhuṃ drakṣyanti punar āgatam
2.66.32	tac chrutvā viṣasādaiva dvitīyā priyaśaṃsanāt viṣaṇṇavadano bhūtvā bhūyaḥ papraccha mātaram
2.66.33	kva cedānīṃ sa dharmātmā kausalyānandavardhanaḥ lakṣmaṇena saha bhrātrā sītayā ca samaṃ gataḥ
2.66.34	tathā pṛṣṭā yathātattvam ākhyātum upacakrame mātāsya yugapad vākyaṃ vipriyaṃ priyaśaṅkayā
2.66.35	sa hi rājasutaḥ putra cīravāsā mahāvanam daṇḍakān saha vaidehyā lakṣmaṇānucaro gataḥ
2.66.36	tac chrutvā bharatas trasto bhrātuś cāritraśaṅkayā svasya vaṃśasya māhātmyāt praṣṭuṃ samupacakrame
2.66.37	kaccin na brāhmaṇavadhaṃ hṛtaṃ rāmeṇa kasya cit kaccin nāḍhyo daridro vā tenāpāpo vihiṃsitaḥ
2.66.38	kaccin na paradārān vā rājaputro 'bhimanyate kasmāt sa daṇḍakāraṇye bhrūṇaheva vivāsitaḥ
2.66.39	athāsya capalā mātā tat svakarma yathātatham tenaiva strīsvabhāvena vyāhartum upacakrame
2.66.40	na brāhmaṇadhanaṃ kiṃ cid dhṛtaṃ rāmeṇa kasya cit kaś cin nāḍhyo daridro vā tenāpāpo vihiṃsitaḥ na rāmaḥ paradārāṃś ca cakṣurbhyām api paśyati
2.66.41	mayā tu putra śrutvaiva rāmasyaivābhiṣecanam yācitas te pitā rājyaṃ rāmasya ca vivāsanam
2.66.42	sa svavṛttiṃ samāsthāya pitā te tat tathākarot rāmaś ca sahasaumitriḥ preṣitaḥ saha sītayā
2.66.43	tam apaśyan priyaṃ putraṃ mahīpālo mahāyaśāḥ putraśokaparidyūnaḥ pañcatvam upapedivān
2.66.44	tvayā tv idānīṃ dharmajña rājatvam avalambyatām tvatkṛte hi mayā sarvam idam evaṃvidhaṃ kṛtam
2.66.45	tat putra śīghraṃ vidhinā vidhijñair vasiṣṭhamukhyaiḥ sahito dvijendraiḥ saṃkālya rājānam adīnasattvam ātmānam urvyām abhiṣecayasva
2.67.1	śrutvā tu pitaraṃ vṛttaṃ bhrātaru ca vivāsitau bharato duḥkhasaṃtapta idaṃ vacanam abravīt
2.67.2	kiṃ nuṇkāryaṃ hatasyeha mama rājyena śocataḥ vihīnasyātha pitrā ca bhrātrā pitṛsamena ca
2.67.3	duḥkhe me duḥkham akaror vraṇe kṣāram ivādadhāḥ rājānaṃ pretabhāvasthaṃ kṛtvā rāmaṃ ca tāpasaṃ
2.67.4	kulasya tvam abhāvāya kālarātrir ivāgatā aṅgāram upagūhya sma pitā me nāvabuddhavān
2.67.5	kausalyā ca sumitrā ca putraśokābhipīḍite duṣkaraṃ yadi jīvetāṃ prāpya tvāṃ jananīṃ mama
2.67.6	nanu tv āryo 'pi dharmātmā tvayi vṛttim anuttamām vartate guruvṛttijño yathā mātari vartate
2.67.7	tathā jyeṣṭhā hi me mātā kausalyā dīrghadarśinī tvayi dharmaṃ samāsthāya bhaginyām iva vartate
2.67.8	tasyāḥ putraṃ kṛtātmānaṃ cīravalkalavāsasaṃ prasthāpya vanavāsāya kathaṃ pāpe na śocasi
2.67.9	apāpadarśinaṃ śūraṃ kṛtātmānaṃ yaśasvinam pravrājya cīravasanaṃ kiṃ nu paśyasi kāraṇam
2.67.10	lubdhāyā vidito manye na te 'haṃ rāghavaṃ prati tathā hy anartho rājyārthaṃ tvayā nīto mahān ayam
2.67.11	ahaṃ hi puruṣavyāghrāv apaśyan rāmalakṣmaṇau kena śaktiprabhāvena rājyaṃ rakṣitum utsahe
2.67.12	taṃ hi nityaṃ mahārājo balavantaṃ mahābalaḥ apāśrito 'bhūd dharmātmā merur meruvanaṃ yathā
2.67.13	so 'haṃ katham imaṃ bhāraṃ mahādhuryasamudyatam damyo dhuram ivāsādya saheyaṃ kena caujasā
2.67.14	atha vā me bhavec chaktir yogair buddhibalena vā sakāmāṃ na kariṣyāmi tvām ahaṃ putragardhinīm nivartayiṣyāmi vanād bhrātaraṃ svajanapriyam
2.67.15	ity evam uktvā bharato mahātmā priyetarair vākyagaṇais tudaṃs tām śokāturaś cāpi nanāda bhūyaḥ siṃho yathā parvatagahvarasthaḥ
2.68.1	tāṃ tathā garhayitvā tu mātaraṃ bharatas tadā roṣeṇa mahatāviṣṭaḥ punar evābravīd vacaḥ
2.68.2	rājyād bhraṃśasva kaikeyi nṛśaṃse duṣṭacāriṇi parityaktā ca dharmeṇa mā mṛtaṃ rudatī bhava
2.68.3	kiṃ nu te 'dūṣayad rājā rāmo vā bhṛśadhārmikaḥ yayor mṛtyur vivāsaś ca tvatkṛte tulyam āgatau
2.68.4	bhrūṇahatyām asi prāptā kulasyāsya vināśanāt kaikeyi narakaṃ gaccha mā ca bhartuḥ salokatām
2.68.5	yat tvayā hīdṛśaṃ pāpaṃ kṛtaṃ ghoreṇa karmaṇā sarvalokapriyaṃ hitvā mamāpy āpāditaṃ bhayam
2.68.6	tvatkṛte me pitā vṛtto rāmaś cāraṇyam āśritaḥ ayaśo jīvaloke ca tvayāhaṃ pratipāditaḥ
2.68.7	mātṛrūpe mamāmitre nṛśaṃse rājyakāmuke na te 'ham abhibhāṣyo 'smi durvṛtte patighātini
2.68.8	kausalyā ca sumitrā ca yāś cānyā mama mātaraḥ duḥkhena mahatāviṣṭās tvāṃ prāpya kuladūṣiṇīm
2.68.9	na tvam aśvapateḥ kanyā dharmarājasya dhīmataḥ rākṣasī tatra jātāsi kulapradhvaṃsinī pituḥ
2.68.10	yat tvayā dhārmiko rāmo nityaṃ satyaparāyaṇaḥ vanaṃ prasthāpito duḥkhāt pitā ca tridivaṃ gataḥ
2.68.11	yat pradhānāsi tat pāpaṃ mayi pitrā vinākṛte bhrātṛbhyāṃ ca parityakte sarvalokasya cāpriye
2.68.12	kausalyāṃ dharmasaṃyuktāṃ viyuktāṃ pāpaniścaye kṛtvā kaṃ prāpsyase tv adya lokaṃ nirayagāminī
2.68.13	kiṃ nāvabudhyase krūre niyataṃ bandhusaṃśrayam jyeṣṭhaṃ pitṛsamaṃ rāmaṃ kausalyāyātmasaṃbhavam
2.68.14	aṅgapratyaṅgajaḥ putro hṛdayāc cāpi jāyate tasmāt priyataro mātuḥ priyatvān na tu bāndhavaḥ
2.68.15	anyadā kila dharmajñā surabhiḥ surasaṃmatā vahamānau dadarśorvyāṃ putrau vigatacetasau
2.68.16	tāv ardhadivase śrāntau dṛṣṭvā putrau mahītale ruroda putra śokena bāṣpaparyākulekṣaṇā
2.68.17	adhastād vrajatas tasyāḥ surarājño mahātmanaḥ bindavaḥ patitā gātre sūkṣmāḥ surabhigandhinaḥ
2.68.18	tāṃ dṛṣṭvā śokasaṃtaptāṃ vajrapāṇir yaśasvinīm indraḥ prāñjalir udvignaḥ surarājo 'bravīd vacaḥ
2.68.19	bhayaṃ kaccin na cāsmāsu kutaś cid vidyate mahat kuto nimittaḥ śokas te brūhi sarvahitaiṣiṇi
2.68.20	evam uktā tu surabhiḥ surarājena dhīmatā patyuvāca tato dhīrā vākyaṃ vākyaviśāradā
2.68.21	śāntaṃ pātaṃ na vaḥ kiṃ cit kutaś cid amarādhipa ahaṃ tu magnau śocāmi svaputrau viṣame sthitau
2.68.22	etau dṛṣṭvā kṛṣau dīnau sūryaraśmipratāpinau vadhyamānau balīvardau karṣakeṇa surādhipa
2.68.23	mama kāyāt prasūtau hi duḥkhitau bhāra pīḍitau yau dṛṣṭvā paritapye 'haṃ nāsti putrasamaḥ priyaḥ
2.68.24	yasyāḥ putra sahasrāṇi sāpi śocati kāmadhuk kiṃ punar yā vinā rāmaṃ kausalyā vartayiṣyati
2.68.25	ekaputrā ca sādhvī ca vivatseyaṃ tvayā kṛtā tasmāt tvaṃ satataṃ duḥkhaṃ pretya ceha ca lapsyase
2.68.26	ahaṃ hy apacitiṃ bhrātuḥ pituś ca sakalām imām vardhanaṃ yaśasaś cāpi kariṣyāmi na saṃśayaḥ
2.68.27	ānāyayitvā tanayaṃ kausalyāyā mahādyutim svayam eva pravekṣyāmi vanaṃ muniniṣevitam
2.68.28	iti nāga ivāraṇye tomarāṅkuśacoditaḥ papāta bhuvi saṃkruddho niḥśvasann iva pannagaḥ
2.68.29	saṃraktanetraḥ śithilāmbaras tadā vidhūtasarvābharaṇaḥ paraṃtapaḥ babhūva bhūmau patito nṛpātmajaḥ śacīpateḥ ketur ivotsavakṣaye
2.69.1	tathaiva krośatas tasya bharatasya mahātmanaḥ kausalyā śabdam ājñāya sumitrām idam abravīt
2.69.2	āgataḥ krūrakāryāyāḥ kaikeyyā bharataḥ sutaḥ tam ahaṃ draṣṭum icchāmi bharataṃ dīrghadarśinam
2.69.3	evam uktvā sumitrāṃ sā vivarṇā malināmbarā pratasthe bharato yatra vepamānā vicetanā
2.69.4	sa tu rāmānujaś cāpi śatrughnasahitas tadā pratasthe bharato yatra kausalyāyā niveśanam
2.69.5	tataḥ śatrughnabharatau kausalyāṃ prekṣya duḥkhitau paryaṣvajetāṃ duḥkhārtāṃ patitāṃ naṣṭacetanām
2.69.6	bharataṃ pratyuvācedaṃ kausalyā bhṛśaduḥkhitā idaṃ te rājyakāmasya rājyaṃ prāptam akaṇṭakam saṃprāptaṃ bata kaikeyyā śīghraṃ krūreṇa karmaṇā
2.69.7	prasthāpya cīravasanaṃ putraṃ me vanavāsinam kaikeyī kaṃ guṇaṃ tatra paśyati krūradarśinī
2.69.8	kṣipraṃ mām api kaikeyī prasthāpayitum arhati hiraṇyanābho yatrāste suto me sumahāyaśāḥ
2.69.9	atha vā svayam evāhaṃ sumitrānucarā sukham agnihotraṃ puraskṛtya prasthāsye yatra rāghavaḥ
2.69.10	kāmaṃ vā svayam evādya tatra māṃ netum arhasi yatrāsau puruṣavyāghras tapyate me tapaḥ sutaḥ
2.69.11	idaṃ hi tava vistīrṇaṃ dhanadhānyasamācitam hastyaśvarathasaṃpūrṇaṃ rājyaṃ niryātitaṃ tayā
2.69.12	evaṃ vilapamānāṃ tāṃ bharataḥ prāñjalis tadā kausalyāṃ pratyuvācedaṃ śokair bahubhir āvṛtām
2.69.13	ārye kasmād ajānantaṃ garhase mām akilbiṣam vipulāṃ ca mama prītiṃ sthirāṃ jānāsi rāghave
2.69.14	kṛtā śāstrānugā buddhir mā bhūt tasya kadā cana satyasaṃdhaḥ satāṃ śreṣṭho yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.15	praiṣyaṃ pāpīyasāṃ yātu sūryaṃ ca prati mehatu hantu pādena gāṃ suptāṃ yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.16	kārayitvā mahat karma bhartā bhṛtyam anarthakam adharmo yo 'sya so 'syās tu yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.17	paripālayamānasya rājño bhūtāni putravat tatas tu druhyatāṃ pāpaṃ yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.18	baliṣaḍbhāgam uddhṛtya nṛpasyārakṣataḥ prajāḥ adharmo yo 'sya so 'syāstu yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.19	saṃśrutya ca tapasvibhyaḥ satre vai yajñadakṣiṇām tāṃ vipralapatāṃ pāpaṃ yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.20	hastyaśvarathasaṃbādhe yuddhe śastrasamākule mā sma kārṣīt satāṃ dharmaṃ yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.21	upadiṣṭaṃ susūkṣmārthaṃ śāstraṃ yatnena dhīmatā sa nāśayatu duṣṭātmā yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.22	pāyasaṃ kṛsaraṃ chāgaṃ vṛthā so 'śnātu nirghṛṇaḥ gurūṃś cāpy avajānātu yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.23	putrair dāraiś ca bhṛtyaiś ca svagṛhe parivāritaḥ sa eko mṛṣṭam aśnātu yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.24	rājastrībālavṛddhānāṃ vadhe yat pāpam ucyate bhṛtyatyāge ca yat pāpaṃ tat pāpaṃ pratipadyatām
2.69.25	ubhe saṃdhye śayānasya yat pāpaṃ parikalpyate tac ca pāpaṃ bhavet tasya yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.26	yad agnidāyake pāpaṃ yat pāpaṃ gurutalpage mitradrohe ca yat pāpaṃ tat pāpaṃ pratipadyatām
2.69.27	devatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca mātā pitros tathaiva ca mā sma kārṣīt sa śuśrūṣāṃ yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.28	satāṃ lokāt satāṃ kīrtyāḥ sajjuṣṭāt karmaṇas tathā bhraśyatu kṣipram adyaiva yasyāryo 'numate gataḥ
2.69.29	vihīnāṃ patiputrābhyāṃ kausalyāṃ pārthivātmajaḥ evam āśvasayann eva duḥkhārto nipapāta ha
2.69.30	tathā tu śapathaiḥ kaṣṭaiḥ śapamānam acetanam bharataṃ śokasaṃtaptaṃ kausalyā vākyam abravīt
2.69.31	mama duḥkham idaṃ putra bhūyaḥ samupajāyate śapathaiḥ śapamāno hi prāṇān uparuṇatsi me
2.69.32	diṣṭyā na calito dharmād ātmā te sahalakṣmaṇaḥ vatsa satyapratijño me satāṃ lokān avāpsyasi
2.69.33	evaṃ vilapamānasya duḥkhārtasya mahātmanaḥ mohāc ca śokasaṃrodhād babhūva lulitaṃ manaḥ
2.69.34	lālapyamānasya vicetanasya pranaṣṭabuddheḥ patitasya bhūmau muhur muhur niḥśvasataś ca dīrghaṃ sā tasya śokena jagāma rātriḥ
2.70.1	tam evaṃ śokasaṃtaptaṃ bharataṃ kekayīsutam uvāca vadatāṃ śreṣṭho vasiṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhavāg ṛṣiḥ
2.70.2	alaṃ śokena bhadraṃ te rājaputra mahāyaśaḥ prāptakālaṃ narapateḥ kuru saṃyānam uttaram
2.70.3	vasiṣṭhasya vacaḥ śrutvā bharato dhāraṇāṃ gataḥ pretakāryāṇi sarvāṇi kārayām āsa dharmavit
2.70.4	uddhṛtaṃ tailasaṃkledāt sa tu bhūmau niveśitam āpītavarṇavadanaṃ prasuptam iva bhūmipam
2.70.5	niveśya śayane cāgrye nānāratnapariṣkṛte tato daśarathaṃ putro vilalāpa suduḥkhitaḥ
2.70.6	kiṃ te vyavasitaṃ rājan proṣite mayy anāgate vivāsya rāmaṃ dharmajñaṃ lakṣmaṇaṃ ca mahābalam
2.70.7	kva yāsyasi mahārāja hitvemaṃ duḥkhitaṃ janam hīnaṃ puruṣasiṃhena rāmeṇākliṣṭakarmaṇā
2.70.8	yogakṣemaṃ tu te rājan ko 'smin kalpayitā pure tvayi prayāte svas tāta rāme ca vanam āśrite
2.70.9	vidhavā pṛthivī rājaṃs tvayā hīnā na rājate hīnacandreva rajanī nagarī pratibhāti mām
2.70.10	evaṃ vilapamānaṃ taṃ bharataṃ dīnamānasaṃ abravīd vacanaṃ bhūyo vasiṣṭhas tu mahān ṛṣiḥ
2.70.11	pretakāryāṇi yāny asya kartavyāni viśāmpateḥ tāny avyagraṃ mahābāho kriyatām avicāritam
2.70.12	tatheti bharato vākyaṃ vasiṣṭhasyābhipūjya tat ṛtvikpurohitācāryāṃs tvarayām āsa sarvaśaḥ
2.70.13	ye tv agrato narendrasya agny agārād bahiṣkṛtāḥ ṛtvigbhir yājakaiś caiva te hriyante yathāvidhi
2.70.14	śibilāyām athāropya rājānaṃ gatacetanam bāṣpakaṇṭhā vimanasas tam ūhuḥ paricārakāḥ
2.70.15	hiraṇyaṃ ca suvarṇaṃ ca vāsāṃsi vividhāni ca prakiranto janā mārgaṃ nṛpater agrato yayuḥ
2.70.16	candanāguruniryāsān saralaṃ padmakaṃ tathā devadārūṇi cāhṛtya citāṃ cakrus tathāpare
2.70.17	gandhān uccāvacāṃś cānyāṃs tatra dattvātha bhūmipam tataḥ saṃveśayām āsuś citāmadhye tam ṛtvijaḥ
2.70.18	tathā hutāśanaṃ hutvā jepus tasya tadartvijaḥ jaguś ca te yathāśāstraṃ tatra sāmāni sāmagāḥ
2.70.19	śibikābhiś ca yānaiś ca yathārhaṃ tasya yoṣitaḥ nagarān niryayus tatra vṛddhaiḥ parivṛtās tadā
2.70.20	prasavyaṃ cāpi taṃ cakrur ṛtvijo 'gnicitaṃ nṛpam striyaś ca śokasaṃtaptāḥ kausalyā pramukhās tadā
2.70.21	krauñcīnām iva nārīṇāṃ ninādas tatra śuśruve ārtānāṃ karuṇaṃ kāle krośantīnāṃ sahasraśaḥ
2.70.22	tato rudantyo vivaśā vilapya ca punaḥ punaḥ yānebhyaḥ sarayūtīram avaterur varāṅganāḥ
2.70.23	kṛtodakaṃ te bharatena sārdhaṃ nṛpāṅganā mantripurohitāś ca puraṃ praviśyāśruparītanetrā bhūmau daśāhaṃ vyanayanta duḥkham
2.71.1	tato daśāhe 'tigate kṛtaśauco nṛpātmajaḥ dvādaśe 'hani saṃprāpte śrāddhakarmāṇy akārayat
2.71.2	brāhmaṇebhyo dadau ratnaṃ dhanam annaṃ ca puṣkalam bāstikaṃ bahuśuklaṃ ca gāś cāpi śataśas tathā
2.71.3	dāsīdāsaṃ ca yānaṃ ca veśmāni sumahānti ca brāhmaṇebhyo dadau putro rājñas tasyaurdhvadaihikam
2.71.4	tataḥ prabhātasamaye divase 'tha trayodaśe vilalāpa mahābāhur bharataḥ śokamūrchitaḥ
2.71.5	śabdāpihitakaṇṭhaś ca śodhanārtham upāgataḥ citāmūle pitur vākyam idam āha suduḥkhitaḥ
2.71.6	tāta yasmin niṣṛṣṭo 'haṃ tvayā bhrātari rāghave tasmin vanaṃ pravrajite śūnye tyakto 'smy ahaṃ tvayā
2.71.7	yathāgatir anāthāyāḥ putraḥ pravrājito vanam tām ambāṃ tāta kausalyāṃ tyaktvā tvaṃ kva gato nṛpa
2.71.8	dṛṣṭvā bhasmāruṇaṃ tac ca dagdhāsthisthānamaṇḍalam pituḥ śarīra nirvāṇaṃ niṣṭanan viṣasāda ha
2.71.9	sa tu dṛṣṭvā rudan dīnaḥ papāta dharaṇītale utthāpyamānaḥ śakrasya yantra dhvaja iva cyutaḥ
2.71.10	abhipetus tataḥ sarve tasyāmātyāḥ śucivratam antakāle nipatitaṃ yayātim ṛṣayo yathā
2.71.11	śatrughnaś cāpi bharataṃ dṛṣṭvā śokapariplutam visaṃjño nyapatad bhūmau bhūmipālam anusmaran
2.71.12	unmatta iva niścetā vilalāpa suduḥkhitaḥ smṛtvā pitur guṇāṅgāni tāni tāni tadā tadā
2.71.13	mantharā prabhavas tīvraḥ kaikeyīgrāhasaṃkulaḥ varadānamayo 'kṣobhyo 'majjayac chokasāgaraḥ
2.71.14	sukumāraṃ ca bālaṃ ca satataṃ lālitaṃ tvayā kva tāta bharataṃ hitvā vilapantaṃ gato bhavān
2.71.15	nanu bhojyeṣu pāneṣu vastreṣv ābharaṇeṣu ca pravārayasi naḥ sarvāṃs tan naḥ ko 'dya kariṣyati
2.71.16	avadāraṇa kāle tu pṛthivī nāvadīryate vihīnā yā tvayā rājñā dharmajñena mahātmanā
2.71.17	pitari svargam āpanne rāme cāraṇyam āśrite kiṃ me jīvita sāmarthyaṃ pravekṣyāmi hutāśanam
2.71.18	hīno bhrātrā ca pitrā ca śūnyām ikṣvākupālitām ayodhyāṃ na pravekṣyāmi pravekṣyāmi tapovanam
2.71.19	tayor vilapitaṃ śrutvā vyasanaṃ cānvavekṣya tat bhṛśam ārtatarā bhūyaḥ sarva evānugāminaḥ
2.71.20	tato viṣaṇṇau śrāntau ca śatrughnabharatāv ubhau dharaṇyāṃ saṃvyaceṣṭetāṃ bhagnaśṛṅgāv ivarṣabhau
2.71.21	tataḥ prakṛtimān vaidyaḥ pitur eṣāṃ purohitaḥ vasiṣṭho bharataṃ vākyam utthāpya tam uvāca ha
2.71.22	trīṇi dvandvāni bhūteṣu pravṛttāny aviśeṣataḥ teṣu cāparihāryeṣu naivaṃ bhavitum arhati
2.71.23	sumantraś cāpi śatrughnam utthāpyābhiprasādya ca śrāvayām āsa tattvajñaḥ sarvabhūtabhavābhavau
2.71.24	utthitau tau naravyāghrau prakāśete yaśasvinau varṣātapapariklinnau pṛthag indradhvajāv iva
2.71.25	aśrūṇi parimṛdnantau raktākṣau dīnabhāṣiṇau amātyās tvarayanti sma tanayau cāparāḥ kriyāḥ
2.72.1	atra yātrāṃ samīhantaṃ śatrughno lakṣmaṇānujaḥ bharataṃ śokasaṃtaptam idaṃ vacanam abravīt
2.72.2	gatir yaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ duḥkhe kiṃ punar ātmanaḥ sa rāmaḥ sattva saṃpannaḥ striyā pravrājito vanam
2.72.3	balavān vīrya saṃpanno lakṣmaṇo nāma yo 'py asau kiṃ na mocayate rāmaṃ kṛtvāpi pitṛnigraham
2.72.4	pūrvam eva tu nigrāhyaḥ samavekṣya nayānayau utpathaṃ yaḥ samārūḍho nāryā rājā vaśaṃ gataḥ
2.72.5	iti saṃbhāṣamāṇe tu śatrughne lakṣmaṇānuje prāgdvāre 'bhūt tadā kubjā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā
2.72.6	liptā candanasāreṇa rājavastrāṇi bibhratī mekhalā dāmabhiś citrai rajjubaddheva vānarī
2.72.7	tāṃ samīkṣya tadā dvāḥstho bhṛśaṃ pāpasya kāriṇīm gṛhītvākaruṇaṃ kubjāṃ śatrughnāya nyavedayat
2.72.8	yasyāḥ kṛte vane rāmo nyastadehaś ca vaḥ pitā seyaṃ pāpā nṛśaṃsā ca tasyāḥ kuru yathāmati
2.72.9	śatrughnaś ca tad ājñāya vacanaṃ bhṛśaduḥkhitaḥ antaḥpuracarān sarvān ity uvāca dhṛtavrataḥ
2.72.10	tīvram utpāditaṃ duḥkhaṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ me tathā pituḥ yayā seyaṃ nṛśaṃsasya karmaṇaḥ phalam aśnutām
2.72.11	evam uktā ca tenāśu sakhī janasamāvṛtā gṛhītā balavat kubjā sā tadgṛham anādayat
2.72.12	tataḥ subhṛśa saṃtaptas tasyāḥ sarvaḥ sakhījanaḥ kruddham ājñāya śatrughnaṃ vyapalāyata sarvaśaḥ
2.72.13	amantrayata kṛtsnaś ca tasyāḥ sarvasakhījanaḥ yathāyaṃ samupakrānto niḥśeṣaṃ naḥ kariṣyati
2.72.14	sānukrośāṃ vadānyāṃ ca dharmajñāṃ ca yaśasvinīm kausalyāṃ śaraṇaṃ yāmaḥ sā hi no 'stu dhruvā gatiḥ
2.72.15	sa ca roṣeṇa tāmrākṣaḥ śatrughnaḥ śatrutāpanaḥ vicakarṣa tadā kubjāṃ krośantīṃ pṛthivītale
2.72.16	tasyā hy ākṛṣyamāṇāyā mantharāyās tatas tataḥ citraṃ bahuvidhaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ pṛthivyāṃ tad vyaśīryata
2.72.17	tena bhāṇḍena saṃkīrṇaṃ śrīmadrājaniveśanam aśobhata tadā bhūyaḥ śāradaṃ gaganaṃ yathā
2.72.18	sa balī balavat krodhād gṛhītvā puruṣarṣabhaḥ kaikeyīm abhinirbhartsya babhāṣe paruṣaṃ vacaḥ
2.72.19	tair vākyaiḥ paruṣair duḥkhaiḥ kaikeyī bhṛśaduḥkhitā śatrughna bhayasaṃtrastā putraṃ śaraṇam āgatā
2.72.20	tāṃ prekṣya bharataḥ kruddhaṃ śatrughnam idam abravīt avadhyāḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ pramadāḥ kṣamyatām iti
2.72.21	hanyām aham imāṃ pāpāṃ kaikeyīṃ duṣṭacāriṇīm yadi māṃ dhārmiko rāmo nāsūyen mātṛghātakam
2.72.22	imām api hatāṃ kubjāṃ yadi jānāti rāghavaḥ tvāṃ ca māṃ caiva dharmātmā nābhibhāṣiṣyate dhruvam
2.72.23	bharatasya vacaḥ śrutvā śatrughno lakṣmaṇānujaḥ nyavartata tato roṣāt tāṃ mumoca ca mantharām
2.72.24	sā pādamūle kaikeyyā mantharā nipapāta ha niḥśvasantī suduḥkhārtā kṛpaṇaṃ vilalāpa ca
2.72.25	śatrughnavikṣepavimūḍhasaṃjñāṃ samīkṣya kubjāṃ bharatasya mātā śanaiḥ samāśvāsayad ārtarūpāṃ krauñcīṃ vilagnām iva vīkṣamāṇām
2.73.1	tataḥ prabhātasamaye divase 'tha caturdaśe sametya rājakartāro bharataṃ vākyam abruvan
2.73.2	gato daśarathaḥ svargaṃ yo no gurutaro guruḥ rāmaṃ pravrājya vai jyeṣṭhaṃ lakṣmaṇaṃ ca mahābalam
2.73.3	tvam adya bhava no rājā rājaputra mahāyaśaḥ saṃgatyā nāparādhnoti rājyam etad anāyakam
2.73.4	ābhiṣecanikaṃ sarvam idam ādāya rāghava pratīkṣate tvāṃ svajanaḥ śreṇayaś ca nṛpātmaja
2.73.5	rājyaṃ gṛhāṇa bharata pitṛpaitāmahaṃ mahat abhiṣecaya cātmānaṃ pāhi cāsmān nararṣabha
2.73.6	ābhiṣecanikaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ kṛtvā sarvaṃ pradakṣiṇam bharatas taṃ janaṃ sarvaṃ pratyuvāca dhṛtavrataḥ
2.73.7	jyeṣṭhasya rājatā nityam ucitā hi kulasya naḥ naivaṃ bhavanto māṃ vaktum arhanti kuśalā janāḥ
2.73.8	rāmaḥ pūrvo hi no bhrātā bhaviṣyati mahīpatiḥ ahaṃ tv araṇye vatsyāmi varṣāṇi nava pañca ca
2.73.9	yujyatāṃ mahatī senā caturaṅgamahābalā ānayiṣyāmy ahaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ bhrātaraṃ rāghavaṃ vanāt
2.73.10	ābhiṣecanikaṃ caiva sarvam etad upaskṛtam puraskṛtya gamiṣyāmi rāmahetor vanaṃ prati
2.73.11	tatraiva taṃ naravyāghram abhiṣicya puraskṛtam āneṣyāmi tu vai rāmaṃ havyavāham ivādhvarāt
2.73.12	na sakāmā kariṣyāmi svam imāṃ mātṛgandhinīm vane vatsyāmy ahaṃ durge rāmo rājā bhaviṣyati
2.73.13	kriyatāṃ śilpibhiḥ panthāḥ samāni viṣamāṇi ca rakṣiṇaś cānusaṃyāntu pathi durgavicārakāḥ
2.73.14	evaṃ saṃbhāṣamāṇaṃ taṃ rāmahetor nṛpātmajam pratyuvāca janaḥ sarvaḥ śrīmadvākyam anuttamam
2.73.15	evaṃ te bhāṣamāṇasya padmā śrīr upatiṣṭhatām yas tvaṃ jyeṣṭhe nṛpasute pṛthivīṃ dātum icchasi
2.73.16	anuttamaṃ tad vacanaṃ nṛpātmaja prabhāṣitaṃ saṃśravaṇe niśamya ca praharṣajās taṃ prati bāṣpabindavo nipetur āryānananetrasaṃbhavāḥ
2.73.17	ūcus te vacanam idaṃ niśamya hṛṣṭāḥ sāmātyāḥ sapariṣado viyātaśokāḥ panthānaṃ naravarabhaktimāñ janaś ca vyādiṣṭas tava vacanāc ca śilpivargaḥ
2.74.1	atha bhūmipradeśajñāḥ sūtrakarmaviśāradāḥ svakarmābhiratāḥ śūrāḥ khanakā yantrakās tathā
2.74.2	karmāntikāḥ sthapatayaḥ puruṣā yantrakovidāḥ tathā vardhakayaś caiva mārgiṇo vṛkṣatakṣakāḥ
2.74.3	kūpakārāḥ sudhākārā vaṃśakarmakṛtas tathā samarthā ye ca draṣṭāraḥ puratas te pratasthire
2.74.4	sa tu harṣāt tam uddeśaṃ janaugho vipulaḥ prayān aśobhata mahāvegaḥ sāgarasyeva parvaṇi
2.74.5	te svavāraṃ samāsthāya vartmakarmāṇi kovidāḥ karaṇair vividhopetaiḥ purastāt saṃpratasthire
2.74.6	latāvallīś ca gulmāṃś ca sthāṇūn aśmana eva ca janās te cakrire mārgaṃ chindanto vividhān drumān
2.74.7	avṛkṣeṣu ca deśeṣu ke cid vṛkṣān aropayan ke cit kuṭhāraiṣ ṭaṅkaiś ca dātraiś chindan kva cit kva cit
2.74.8	apare vīraṇastambān balino balavattarāḥ vidhamanti sma durgāṇi sthalāni ca tatas tataḥ
2.74.9	apare 'pūrayan kūpān pāṃsubhiḥ śvabhram āyatam nimnabhāgāṃs tathā ke cit samāṃś cakruḥ samantataḥ
2.74.10	babandhur bandhanīyāṃś ca kṣodyān saṃcukṣudus tadā bibhidur bhedanīyāṃś ca tāṃs tān deśān narās tadā
2.74.11	acireṇaiva kālena parivāhān bahūdakān cakrur bahuvidhākārān sāgarapratimān bahūn udapānān bahuvidhān vedikā parimaṇḍitān
2.74.12	sasudhākuṭṭimatalaḥ prapuṣpitamahīruhaḥ mattodghuṣṭadvijagaṇaḥ patākābhir alaṃkṛtaḥ
2.74.13	candanodakasaṃsikto nānākusumabhūṣitaḥ bahv aśobhata senāyāḥ panthāḥ svargapathopamaḥ
2.74.14	ājñāpyātha yathājñapti yuktās te 'dhikṛtā narāḥ ramaṇīyeṣu deśeṣu bahusvāduphaleṣu ca
2.74.15	yo niveśas tv abhipreto bharatasya mahātmanaḥ bhūyas taṃ śobhayām āsur bhūṣābhir bhūṣaṇopamam
2.74.16	nakṣatreṣu praśasteṣu muhūrteṣu ca tadvidaḥ niveśaṃ sthāpayām āsur bharatasya mahātmanaḥ
2.74.17	bahupāṃsucayāś cāpi parikhāparivāritāḥ tatrendrakīlapratimāḥ pratolīvaraśobhitāḥ
2.74.18	prāsādamālāsaṃyuktāḥ saudhaprākārasaṃvṛtāḥ patākā śobhitāḥ sarve sunirmitamahāpathāḥ
2.74.19	visarpadbhir ivākāśe viṭaṅkāgravimānakaiḥ samucchritair niveśās te babhuḥ śakrapuropamāḥ
2.74.20	jāhnavīṃ tu samāsādya vividhadruma kānanām śītalāmalapānīyāṃ mahāmīnasamākulām
2.74.21	sacandratārāgaṇamaṇḍitaṃ yathā nabhaḥkṣapāyām amalaṃ virājate narendramārgaḥ sa tathā vyarājata krameṇa ramyaḥ śubhaśilpinirmitaḥ
2.75.1	tato nāndīmukhīṃ rātriṃ bharataṃ sūtamāgadhāḥ tuṣṭuvur vāgviśeṣajñāḥ stavair maṅgalasaṃhitaiḥ
2.75.2	suvarṇakoṇābhihataḥ prāṇadad yāmadundubhiḥ dadhmuḥ śaṅkhāṃś ca śataśo vādyāṃś coccāvacasvarān
2.75.3	sa tūrya ghoṣaḥ sumahān divam āpūrayann iva bharataṃ śokasaṃtaptaṃ bhūyaḥ śokair arandhrayat
2.75.4	tato prabuddho bharatas taṃ ghoṣaṃ saṃnivartya ca nāhaṃ rājeti cāpy uktvā śatrughnam idam abravīt
2.75.5	paśya śatrughna kaikeyyā lokasyāpakṛtaṃ mahat visṛjya mayi duḥkhāni rājā daśaratho gataḥ
2.75.6	tasyaiṣā dharmarājasya dharmamūlā mahātmanaḥ paribhramati rājaśrīr naur ivākarṇikā jale
2.75.7	ity evaṃ bharataṃ prekṣya vilapantaṃ vicetanam kṛpaṇaṃ ruruduḥ sarvāḥ sasvaraṃ yoṣitas tadā
2.75.8	tathā tasmin vilapati vasiṣṭho rājadharmavit sabhām ikṣvākunāthasya praviveśa mahāyaśāḥ
2.75.9	śāta kumbhamayīṃ ramyāṃ maṇiratnasamākulām sudharmām iva dharmātmā sagaṇaḥ pratyapadyata
2.75.10	sa kāñcanamayaṃ pīṭhaṃ parārdhyāstaraṇāvṛtam adhyāsta sarvavedajño dūtān anuśaśāsa ca
2.75.11	brāhmaṇān kṣatriyān yodhān amātyān gaṇaballabhān kṣipram ānayatāvyagrāḥ kṛtyam ātyayikaṃ hi naḥ
2.75.12	tato halahalāśabdo mahān samudapadyata rathair aśvair gajaiś cāpi janānām upagacchatām
2.75.13	tato bharatam āyāntaṃ śatakratum ivāmarāḥ pratyanandan prakṛtayo yathā daśarathaṃ tathā
2.75.14	hrada iva timināgasaṃvṛtaḥ stimitajalo maṇiśaṅkhaśarkaraḥ daśarathasutaśobhitā sabhā sadaśaratheva babhau yathā purā
2.76.1	tām āryagaṇasaṃpūrṇāṃ bharataḥ pragrahāṃ sabhām dadarśa buddhisaṃpannaḥ pūrṇacandrāṃ niśām iva
2.76.2	āsanāni yathānyāyam āryāṇāṃ viśatāṃ tadā adṛśyata ghanāpāye pūrṇacandreva śarvarī
2.76.3	rājñas tu prakṛtīḥ sarvāḥ samagrāḥ prekṣya dharmavit idaṃ purohito vākyaṃ bharataṃ mṛdu cābravīt
2.76.4	tāta rājā daśarathaḥ svargato dharmam ācaran dhana dhānyavatīṃ sphītāṃ pradāya pṛthivīṃ tava
2.76.5	rāmas tathā satyadhṛtiḥ satāṃ dharmam anusmaran nājahāt pitur ādeśaṃ śaśī jyotsnām ivoditaḥ
2.76.6	pitrā bhrātrā ca te dattaṃ rājyaṃ nihatakaṇṭakam tad bhuṅkṣva muditāmātyaḥ kṣipram evābhiṣecaya
2.76.7	udīcyāś ca pratīcyāś ca dākṣiṇātyāś ca kevalāḥ koṭyāparāntāḥ sāmudrā ratnāny abhiharantu te
2.76.8	tac chrutvā bharato vākyaṃ śokenābhipariplutaḥ jagāma manasā rāmaṃ dharmajño dharmakāṅkṣayā
2.76.9	sa bāṣpakalayā vācā kalahaṃsasvaro yuvā vilalāpa sabhāmadhye jagarhe ca purohitam
2.76.10	caritabrahmacaryasya vidyā snātasya dhīmataḥ dharme prayatamānasya ko rājyaṃ madvidho haret
2.76.11	kathaṃ daśarathāj jāto bhaved rājyāpahārakaḥ rājyaṃ cāhaṃ ca rāmasya dharmaṃ vaktum ihārhasi
2.76.12	jyeṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhaś ca dharmātmā dilīpanahuṣopamaḥ labdhum arhati kākutstho rājyaṃ daśaratho yathā
2.76.13	anāryajuṣṭam asvargyaṃ kuryāṃ pāpam ahaṃ yadi ikṣvākūṇām ahaṃ loke bhaveyaṃ kulapāṃsanaḥ
2.76.14	yad dhi mātrā kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ nāhaṃ tad abhirocaye ihastho vanadurgasthaṃ namasyāmi kṛtāñjaliḥ
2.76.15	rāmam evānugacchāmi sa rājā dvipadāṃ varaḥ trayāṇām api lokānāṃ rāghavo rājyam arhati
2.76.16	tad vākyaṃ dharmasaṃyuktaṃ śrutvā sarve sabhāsadaḥ harṣān mumucur aśrūṇi rāme nihitacetasaḥ
2.76.17	yadi tv āryaṃ na śakṣyāmi vinivartayituṃ vanāt vane tatraiva vatsyāmi yathāryo lakṣmaṇas tathā
2.76.18	sarvopāyaṃ tu vartiṣye vinivartayituṃ balāt samakṣam ārya miśrāṇāṃ sādhūnāṃ guṇavartinām
2.76.19	evam uktvā tu dharmātmā bharato bhrātṛvatsalaḥ samīpastham uvācedaṃ sumantraṃ mantrakovidam
2.76.20	tūrṇam utthāya gaccha tvaṃ sumantra mama śāsanāt yātrām ājñāpaya kṣipraṃ balaṃ caiva samānaya
2.76.21	evam uktaḥ sumantras tu bharatena mahātmanā prahṛṣṭaḥ so 'diśat sarvaṃ yathā saṃdiṣṭam iṣṭavat
2.76.22	tāḥ prahṛṣṭāḥ prakṛtayo balādhyakṣā balasya ca śrutvā yātrāṃ samājñaptāṃ rāghavasya nivartane
2.76.23	tato yodhāṅganāḥ sarvā bhartṝn sarvān gṛhegṛhe yātrā gamanam ājñāya tvarayanti sma harṣitāḥ
2.76.24	te hayair gorathaiḥ śīghraiḥ syandanaiś ca manojavaiḥ saha yodhair balādhyakṣā balaṃ sarvam acodayan
2.76.25	sajjaṃ tu tad balaṃ dṛṣṭvā bharato gurusaṃnidhau rathaṃ me tvarayasveti sumantraṃ pārśvato 'bravīt
2.76.26	bharatasya tu tasyājñāṃ pratigṛhya praharṣitaḥ rathaṃ gṛhītvā prayayau yuktaṃ paramavājibhiḥ
2.76.27	sa rāghavaḥ satyadhṛtiḥ pratāpavān bruvan suyuktaṃ dṛḍhasatyavikramaḥ guruṃ mahāraṇyagataṃ yaśasvinaṃ prasādayiṣyan bharato 'bravīt tadā
2.76.28	tūrṇaṃ samutthāya sumantra gaccha balasya yogāya balapradhānān ānetum icchāmi hi taṃ vanasthaṃ prasādya rāmaṃ jagato hitāya
2.76.29	sa sūtaputro bharatena samyag ājñāpitaḥ saṃparipūrṇakāmaḥ śaśāsa sarvān prakṛtipradhānān balasya mukhyāṃś ca suhṛjjanaṃ ca
2.76.30	tataḥ samutthāya kule kule te rājanyavaiśyā vṛṣalāś ca viprāḥ ayūyujann uṣṭrarathān kharāṃś ca nāgān hayāṃś caiva kulaprasūtān
2.77.1	tataḥ samutthitaḥ kālyam āsthāya syandanottamam prayayau bharataḥ śīghraṃ rāmadarśanakāṅkṣayā
2.77.2	agrataḥ prayayus tasya sarve mantripurodhasaḥ adhiruhya hayair yuktān rathān sūryarathopamān
2.77.3	navanāgasahasrāṇi kalpitāni yathāvidhi anvayur bharataṃ yāntam ikṣvāku kulanandanam
2.77.4	ṣaṣṭhī rathasahasrāṇi dhanvino vividhāyudhāḥ anvayur bharataṃ yāntaṃ rājaputraṃ yaśasvinam
2.77.5	śataṃ sahasrāṇy aśvānāṃ samārūḍhāni rāghavam anvayur bharataṃ yāntaṃ rājaputraṃ yaśasvinam
2.77.6	kaikeyī ca sumitrā ca kausalyā ca yaśasvinī rāmānayanasaṃhṛṣṭā yayur yānena bhāsvatā
2.77.7	prayātāś cāryasaṃghātā rāmaṃ draṣṭuṃ salakṣmaṇam tasyaiva ca kathāś citrāḥ kurvāṇā hṛṣṭamānasāḥ
2.77.8	meghaśyāmaṃ mahābāhuṃ sthirasattvaṃ dṛḍhavratam kadā drakṣyāmahe rāmaṃ jagataḥ śokanāśanam
2.77.9	dṛṣṭa eva hi naḥ śokam apaneṣyati rāghavaḥ tamaḥ sarvasya lokasya samudyann iva bhāskaraḥ
2.77.10	ity evaṃ kathayantas te saṃprahṛṣṭāḥ kathāḥ śubhāḥ pariṣvajānāś cānyonyaṃ yayur nāgarikās tadā
2.77.11	ye ca tatrāpare sarve saṃmatā ye ca naigamāḥ rāmaṃ prati yayur hṛṣṭāḥ sarvāḥ prakṛtayas tadā
2.77.12	maṇi kārāś ca ye ke cit kumbhakārāś ca śobhanāḥ sūtrakarmakṛtaś caiva ye ca śastropajīvinaḥ
2.77.13	māyūrakāḥ krākacikā rocakā vedhakās tathā dantakārāḥ sudhākārās tathā gandhopajīvinaḥ
2.77.14	suvarṇakārāḥ prakhyātās tathā kambaladhāvakāḥ snāpakācchādakā vaidyā dhūpakāḥ śauṇḍikās tathā
2.77.15	rajakās tunnavāyāś ca grāmaghoṣamahattarāḥ śailūṣāś ca saha strībhir yānti kaivartakās tathā
2.77.16	samāhitā vedavido brāhmaṇā vṛttasaṃmatāḥ gorathair bharataṃ yāntam anujagmuḥ sahasraśaḥ
2.77.17	suveṣāḥ śuddhavasanās tāmramṛṣṭānulepanāḥ sarve te vividhair yānaiḥ śanair bharatam anvayuḥ
2.77.18	prahṛṣṭamuditā senā sānvayāt kaikayīsutam vyavatiṣṭhata sā senā bharatasyānuyāyinī
2.77.19	nirīkṣyānugatāṃ senāṃ tāṃ ca gaṅgāṃ śivodakām bharataḥ sacivān sarvān abravīd vākyakovidaḥ
2.77.20	niveśayata me sainyam abhiprāyeṇa sarvaśaḥ viśrāntaḥ pratariṣyāmaḥ śva idānīṃ mahānadīm
2.77.21	dātuṃ ca tāvad icchāmi svar gatasya mahīpateḥ aurdhvadeha nimittārtham avatīryodakaṃ nadīm
2.77.22	tasyaivaṃ bruvato 'mātyās tathety uktvā samāhitāḥ nyaveśayaṃs tāṃś chandena svena svena pṛthakpṛthak
2.77.23	niveśya gaṅgām anu tāṃ mahānadīṃ camūṃ vidhānaiḥ paribarha śobhinīm uvāsa rāmasya tadā mahātmano vicintayāno bharato nivartanam
2.78.1	tato niviṣṭāṃ dhvajinīṃ gaṅgām anvāśritāṃ nadīm niṣādarājo dṛṣṭvaiva jñātīn saṃtvarito 'bravīt
2.78.2	mahatīyam ataḥ senā sāgarābhā pradṛśyate nāsyāntam avagacchāmi manasāpi vicintayan
2.78.3	sa eṣa hi mahākāyaḥ kovidāradhvajo rathe bandhayiṣyati vā dāśān atha vāsmān vadhiṣyati
2.78.4	atha dāśarathiṃ rāmaṃ pitrā rājyād vivāsitam bharataḥ kaikeyīputro hantuṃ samadhigacchati
2.78.5	bhartā caiva sakhā caiva rāmo dāśarathir mama tasyārthakāmāḥ saṃnaddhā gaṅgānūpe 'tra tiṣṭhata
2.78.6	tiṣṭhantu sarvadāśāś ca gaṅgām anvāśritā nadīm balayuktā nadīrakṣā māṃsamūlaphalāśanāḥ
2.78.7	nāvāṃ śatānāṃ pañcānāṃ kaivartānāṃ śataṃ śatam saṃnaddhānāṃ tathā yūnāṃ tiṣṭhantv atyabhyacodayat
2.78.8	yadā tuṣṭas tu bharato rāmasyeha bhaviṣyati seyaṃ svastimayī senā gaṅgām adya tariṣyati
2.78.9	ity uktvopāyanaṃ gṛhya matsyamāṃsamadhūni ca abhicakrāma bharataṃ niṣādādhipatir guhaḥ
2.78.10	tam āyāntaṃ tu saṃprekṣya sūtaputraḥ pratāpavān bharatāyācacakṣe 'tha vinayajño vinītavat
2.78.11	eṣa jñātisahasreṇa sthapatiḥ parivāritaḥ kuśalo daṇḍakāraṇye vṛddho bhrātuś ca te sakhā
2.78.12	tasmāt paśyatu kākutstha tvāṃ niṣādādhipo guhaḥ asaṃśayaṃ vijānīte yatra tau rāmalakṣmaṇau
2.78.13	etat tu vacanaṃ śrutvā sumantrād bharataḥ śubham uvāca vacanaṃ śīghraṃ guhaḥ paśyatu mām iti
2.78.14	labdhvābhyanujñāṃ saṃhṛṣṭo jñātibhiḥ parivāritaḥ āgamya bharataṃ prahvo guho vacanam abravīt
2.78.15	niṣkuṭaś caiva deśo 'yaṃ vañcitāś cāpi te vayam nivedayāmas te sarve svake dāśakule vasa
2.78.16	asti mūlaṃ phalaṃ caiva niṣādaiḥ samupāhṛtam ārdraṃ ca māṃsaṃ śuṣkaṃ ca vanyaṃ coccāvacaṃ mahat
2.78.17	āśaṃse svāśitā senā vatsyatīmāṃ vibhāvarīm arcito vividhaiḥ kāmaiḥ śvaḥ sasainyo gamiṣyasi
2.79.1	evam uktas tu bharato niṣādādhipatiṃ guham pratyuvāca mahāprājño vākyaṃ hetvarthasaṃhitam
2.79.2	ūrjitaḥ khalu te kāmaḥ kṛto mama guroḥ sakhe yo me tvam īdṛśīṃ senām eko 'bhyarcitum icchasi
2.79.3	ity uktvā tu mahātejā guhaṃ vacanam uttamam abravīd bharataḥ śrīmān niṣādādhipatiṃ punaḥ
2.79.4	katareṇa gamiṣyāmi bharadvājāśramaṃ guha gahano 'yaṃ bhṛśaṃ deśo gaṅgānūpo duratyayaḥ
2.79.5	tasya tad vacanaṃ śrutvā rājaputrasya dhīmataḥ abravīt prāñjalir vākyaṃ guho gahanagocaraḥ
2.79.6	dāśās tv anugamiṣyanti dhanvinaḥ susamāhitāḥ ahaṃ cānugamiṣyāmi rājaputra mahāyaśaḥ
2.79.7	kaccin na duṣṭo vrajasi rāmasyākliṣṭakarmaṇaḥ iyaṃ te mahatī senā śaṅkāṃ janayatīva me
2.79.8	tam evam abhibhāṣantam ākāśa iva nirmalaḥ bharataḥ ślakṣṇayā vācā guhaṃ vacanam abravīt
2.79.9	mā bhūt sa kālo yat kaṣṭaṃ na māṃ śaṅkitum arhasi rāghavaḥ sa hi me bhrātā jyeṣṭhaḥ pitṛsamo mama
2.79.10	taṃ nivartayituṃ yāmi kākutsthaṃ vanavāsinam buddhir anyā na te kāryā guha satyaṃ bravīmi te
2.79.11	sa tu saṃhṛṣṭavadanaḥ śrutvā bharatabhāṣitam punar evābravīd vākyaṃ bharataṃ prati harṣitaḥ
2.79.12	dhanyas tvaṃ na tvayā tulyaṃ paśyāmi jagatītale ayatnād āgataṃ rājyaṃ yas tvaṃ tyaktum ihecchasi
2.79.13	śāśvatī khalu te kīrtir lokān anucariṣyati yas tvaṃ kṛcchragataṃ rāmaṃ pratyānayitum icchasi
2.79.14	evaṃ saṃbhāṣamāṇasya guhasya bharataṃ tadā babhau naṣṭaprabhaḥ sūryo rajanī cābhyavartata
2.79.15	saṃniveśya sa tāṃ senāṃ guhena paritoṣitaḥ śatrughnena saha śrīmāñ śayanaṃ punar āgamat
2.79.16	rāmacintāmayaḥ śoko bharatasya mahātmanaḥ upasthito hy anarhasya dharmaprekṣasya tādṛśaḥ
2.79.17	antardāhena dahanaḥ saṃtāpayati rāghavam vanadāhābhisaṃtaptaṃ gūḍho 'gnir iva pādapam
2.79.18	prasrutaḥ sarvagātrebhyaḥ svedaḥ śokāgnisaṃbhavaḥ yathā sūryāṃśusaṃtapto himavān prasruto himam
2.79.19	dhyānanirdaraśailena viniḥśvasitadhātunā dainyapādapasaṃghena śokāyāsādhiśṛṅgiṇā
2.79.20	pramohānantasattvena saṃtāpauṣadhiveṇunā ākrānto duḥkhaśailena mahatā kaikayīsutaḥ
2.79.21	guhena sārdhaṃ bharataḥ samāgato mahānubhāvaḥ sajanaḥ samāhitaḥ sudurmanās taṃ bharataṃ tadā punar guhaḥ samāśvāsayad agrajaṃ prati
2.80.1	ācacakṣe 'tha sadbhāvaṃ lakṣmaṇasya mahātmanaḥ bharatāyāprameyāya guho gahanagocaraḥ
2.80.2	taṃ jāgrataṃ guṇair yuktaṃ varacāpeṣudhāriṇam bhrātṛ guptyartham atyantam ahaṃ lakṣmaṇam abravam
2.80.3	iyaṃ tāta sukhā śayyā tvadartham upakalpitā pratyāśvasihi śeṣvāsyāṃ sukhaṃ rāghavanandana
2.80.4	ucito 'yaṃ janaḥ sarve duḥkhānāṃ tvaṃ sukhocitaḥ dharmātmaṃs tasya guptyarthaṃ jāgariṣyāmahe vayam
2.80.5	na hi rāmāt priyataro mamāsti bhuvi kaś cana motsuko bhūr bravīmy etad apy asatyaṃ tavāgrataḥ
2.80.6	asya prasādād āśaṃse loke 'smin sumahad yaśaḥ dharmāvāptiṃ ca vipulām arthāvāptiṃ ca kevalām
2.80.7	so 'haṃ priyasakhaṃ rāmaṃ śayānaṃ saha sītayā rakṣiṣyāmi dhanuṣpāṇiḥ sarvaiḥ svair jñātibhiḥ saha
2.80.8	na hi me 'viditaṃ kiṃ cid vane 'smiṃś carataḥ sadā caturaṅgaṃ hy api balaṃ prasahema vayaṃ yudhi
2.80.9	evam asmābhir uktena lakṣmaṇena mahātmanā anunītā vayaṃ sarve dharmam evānupaśyatā
2.80.10	kathaṃ dāśarathau bhūmau śayāne saha sītayā śakyā nidrāmayā labdhuṃ jīvitaṃ vā sukhāni vā
2.80.11	yo na devāsuraiḥ sarvaiḥ śakyaḥ prasahituṃ yudhi taṃ paśya guha saṃviṣṭaṃ tṛṇeṣu saha sītayā
2.80.12	mahatā tapasā labdho vividhaiś ca pariśramaiḥ eko daśarathasyaiṣa putraḥ sadṛśalakṣaṇaḥ
2.80.13	asmin pravrājite rājā na ciraṃ vartayiṣyati vidhavā medinī nūnaṃ kṣipram eva bhaviṣyati
2.80.14	vinadya sumahānādaṃ śrameṇoparatāḥ striyaḥ nirghoṣoparataṃ nūnam adya rājaniveśanam
2.80.15	kausalyā caiva rājā ca tathaiva jananī mama nāśaṃse yadi te sarve jīveyuḥ śarvarīm imām
2.80.16	jīved api hi me mātā śatrughnasyānvavekṣayā duḥkhitā yā tu kausalyā vīrasūr vinaśiṣyati
2.80.17	atikrāntam atikrāntam anavāpya manoratham rājye rāmam anikṣipya pitā me vinaśiṣyati
2.80.18	siddhārthāḥ pitaraṃ vṛttaṃ tasmin kāle hy upasthite pretakāryeṣu sarveṣu saṃskariṣyanti bhūmipam
2.80.19	ramyacatvarasaṃsthānāṃ suvibhaktamahāpathām harmyaprāsādasaṃpannāṃ sarvaratnavibhūṣitām
2.80.20	gajāśvarathasaṃbādhāṃ tūryanādavināditām sarvakalyāṇasaṃpūrṇāṃ hṛṣṭapuṣṭajanākulām
2.80.21	ārāmodyānasaṃpūrṇāṃ samājotsavaśālinīm sukhitā vicariṣyanti rājadhānīṃ pitur mama
2.80.22	api satyapratijñena sārdhaṃ kuśalinā vayam nivṛtte samaye hy asmin sukhitāḥ praviśemahi
2.80.23	paridevayamānasya tasyaivaṃ sumahātmanaḥ tiṣṭhato rājaputrasya śarvarī sātyavartata
2.80.24	prabhāte vimale sūrye kārayitvā jaṭā ubhau asmin bhāgīrathītīre sukhaṃ saṃtāritau mayā
2.80.25	jaṭādharau tau drumacīravāsasau mahābalau kuñjarayūthapopamau vareṣucāpāsidharau paraṃtapau vyavekṣamāṇau saha sītayā gatau
2.81.1	guhasya vacanaṃ śrutvā bharato bhṛśam apriyam dhyānaṃ jagāma tatraiva yatra tac chrutam apriyam
2.81.2	sukumāro mahāsattvaḥ siṃhaskandho mahābhujaḥ puṇḍarīka viśālākṣas taruṇaḥ priyadarśanaḥ
2.81.3	pratyāśvasya muhūrtaṃ tu kālaṃ paramadurmanāḥ papāta sahasā totrair hṛdi viddha iva dvipaḥ
2.81.4	tadavasthaṃ tu bharataṃ śatrughno 'nantara sthitaḥ pariṣvajya rurodoccair visaṃjñaḥ śokakarśitaḥ
2.81.5	tataḥ sarvāḥ samāpetur mātaro bharatasya tāḥ upavāsa kṛśā dīnā bhartṛvyasanakarśitāḥ
2.81.6	tāś ca taṃ patitaṃ bhūmau rudantyaḥ paryavārayan kausalyā tv anusṛtyainaṃ durmanāḥ pariṣasvaje
2.81.7	vatsalā svaṃ yathā vatsam upagūhya tapasvinī paripapraccha bharataṃ rudantī śokalālasā
2.81.8	putravyādhir na te kaccic charīraṃ paribādhate adya rājakulasyāsya tvadadhīnaṃ hi jīvitam
2.81.9	tvāṃ dṛṣṭvā putra jīvāmi rāme sabhrātṛke gate vṛtte daśarathe rājñi nātha ekas tvam adya naḥ
2.81.10	kaccin na lakṣmaṇe putra śrutaṃ te kiṃ cid apriyam putra vā hy ekaputrāyāḥ sahabhārye vanaṃ gate
2.81.11	sa muhūrtaṃ samāśvasya rudann eva mahāyaśāḥ kausalyāṃ parisāntvyedaṃ guhaṃ vacanam abravīt
2.81.12	bhrātā me kvāvasad rātriṃ kva sītā kva ca lakṣmaṇaḥ asvapac chayane kasmin kiṃ bhuktvā guha śaṃsa me
2.81.13	so 'bravīd bharataṃ pṛṣṭo niṣādādhipatir guhaḥ yad vidhaṃ pratipede ca rāme priyahite 'tithau
2.81.14	annam uccāvacaṃ bhakṣyāḥ phalāni vividhāni ca rāmāyābhyavahārārthaṃ bahucopahṛtaṃ mayā
2.81.15	tat sarvaṃ pratyanujñāsīd rāmaḥ satyaparākramaḥ na hi tat pratyagṛhṇāt sa kṣatradharmam anusmaran
2.81.16	na hy asmābhiḥ pratigrāhyaṃ sakhe deyaṃ tu sarvadā iti tena vayaṃ rājann anunītā mahātmanā
2.81.17	lakṣmaṇena samānītaṃ pītvā vāri mahāyaśāḥ aupavāsyaṃ tadākārṣīd rāghavaḥ saha sītayā
2.81.18	tatas tu jalaśeṣeṇa lakṣmaṇo 'py akarot tadā vāg yatās te trayaḥ saṃdhyām upāsata samāhitāḥ
2.81.19	saumitris tu tataḥ paścād akarot svāstaraṃ śubham svayam ānīya barhīṃṣi kṣipraṃ rāghava kāraṇāt
2.81.20	tasmin samāviśad rāmaḥ svāstare saha sītayā prakṣālya ca tayoḥ pādāv apacakrāma lakṣmaṇaḥ
2.81.21	etat tad iṅgudīmūlam idam eva ca tat tṛṇam yasmin rāmaś ca sītā ca rātriṃ tāṃ śayitāv ubhau
2.81.22	niyamya pṛṣṭhe tu talāṅgulitravāñ śaraiḥ supūrṇāv iṣudhī paraṃtapaḥ mahad dhanuḥ sajyam upohya lakṣmaṇo niśām atiṣṭhat parito 'sya kevalam
2.81.23	tatas tv ahaṃ cottamabāṇacāpadhṛk sthito 'bhavaṃ tatra sa yatra lakṣmaṇaḥ atandribhir jñātibhir āttakārmukair mahendrakalpaṃ paripālayaṃs tadā
2.82.1	tac chrutvā nipuṇaṃ sarvaṃ bharataḥ saha mantribhiḥ iṅgudīmūlam āgamya rāmaśayyām avekṣya tām
2.82.2	abravīj jananīḥ sarvā iha tena mahātmanā śarvarī śayitā bhūmāv idam asya vimarditam
2.82.3	mahābhāgakulīnena mahābhāgena dhīmatā jāto daśarathenorvyāṃ na rāmaḥ svaptum arhati
2.82.4	ajinottarasaṃstīrṇe varāstaraṇasaṃcaye śayitvā puruṣavyāghraḥ kathaṃ śete mahītale
2.82.5	prāsādāgra vimāneṣu valabhīṣu ca sarvadā haimarājatabhaumeṣu varāstaraṇaśāliṣu
2.82.6	puṣpasaṃcayacitreṣu candanāgarugandhiṣu pāṇḍurābhraprakāśeṣu śukasaṃgharuteṣu ca
2.82.7	gītavāditranirghoṣair varābharaṇaniḥsvanaiḥ mṛdaṅgavaraśabdaiś ca satataṃ pratibodhitaḥ
2.82.8	bandibhir vanditaḥ kāle bahubhiḥ sūtamāgadhaiḥ gāthābhir anurūpābhiḥ stutibhiś ca paraṃtapaḥ
2.82.9	aśraddheyam idaṃ loke na satyaṃ pratibhāti mā muhyate khalu me bhāvaḥ svapno 'yam iti me matiḥ
2.82.10	na nūnaṃ daivataṃ kiṃ cit kālena balavattaram yatra dāśarathī rāmo bhūmāv evaṃ śayīta saḥ
2.82.11	videharājasya sutā sītā ca priyadarśanā dayitā śayitā bhūmau snuṣā daśarathasya ca
2.82.12	iyaṃ śayyā mama bhrātur idaṃ hi parivartitam sthaṇḍile kaṭhine sarvaṃ gātrair vimṛditaṃ tṛṇam
2.82.13	manye sābharaṇā suptā sītāsmiñ śayane tadā tatra tatra hi dṛśyante saktāḥ kanakabindavaḥ
2.82.14	uttarīyam ihāsaktaṃ suvyaktaṃ sītayā tadā tathā hy ete prakāśante saktāḥ kauśeyatantavaḥ
2.82.15	manye bhartuḥ sukhā śayyā yena bālā tapasvinī sukumārī satī duḥkhaṃ na vijānāti maithilī
2.82.16	sārvabhauma kule jātaḥ sarvalokasukhāvahaḥ sarvalokapriyas tyaktvā rājyaṃ priyam anuttamam
2.82.17	katham indīvaraśyāmo raktākṣaḥ priyadarśanaḥ sukhabhāgī ca duḥkhārhaḥ śayito bhuvi rāghavaḥ
2.82.18	siddhārthā khalu vaidehī patiṃ yānugatā vanam vayaṃ saṃśayitāḥ sarve hīnās tena mahātmanā
2.82.19	akarṇadhārā pṛthivī śūnyeva pratibhāti mā gate daśarathe svarge rāme cāraṇyam āśrite
2.82.20	na ca prārthayate kaś cin manasāpi vasuṃdharām vane 'pi vasatas tasya bāhuvīryābhirakṣitām
2.82.21	śūnyasaṃvaraṇārakṣām ayantritahayadvipām apāvṛtapuradvārāṃ rājadhānīm arakṣitām
2.82.22	aprahṛṣṭabalāṃ nyūnāṃ viṣamasthām anāvṛtām śatravo nābhimanyante bhakṣyān viṣakṛtān iva
2.82.23	adya prabhṛti bhūmau tu śayiṣye 'haṃ tṛṇeṣu vā phalamūlāśano nityaṃ jaṭācīrāṇi dhārayan
2.82.24	tasyārtham uttaraṃ kālaṃ nivatsyāmi sukhaṃ vane taṃ pratiśravam āmucya nāsya mithyā bhaviṣyati
2.82.25	vasantaṃ bhrātur arthāya śatrughno mānuvatsyati lakṣmaṇena saha tv āryo ayodhyāṃ pālayiṣyati
2.82.26	abhiṣekṣyanti kākutstham ayodhyāyāṃ dvijātayaḥ api me devatāḥ kuryur imaṃ satyaṃ manoratham
2.82.27	prasādyamānaḥ śirasā mayā svayaṃ bahuprakāraṃ yadi na prapatsyate tato 'nuvatsyāmi cirāya rāghavaṃ vane vasan nārhati mām upekṣitum
2.83.1	vyuṣya rātriṃ tu tatraiva gaṅgākūle sa rāghavaḥ bharataḥ kālyam utthāya śatrughnam idam abravīt
2.83.2	śatrughottiṣṭha kiṃ śeṣe niṣādādhipatiṃ guham śīghram ānaya bhadraṃ te tārayiṣyati vāhinīm
2.83.3	jāgarmi nāhaṃ svapimi tathaivāryaṃ vicintayan ity evam abravīd bhrātrā śatrughno 'pi pracoditaḥ
2.83.4	iti saṃvadator evam anyonyaṃ narasiṃhayoḥ āgamya prāñjaliḥ kāle guho bharatam abravīt
2.83.5	kaccit sukhaṃ nadītīre 'vātsīḥ kākutstha śarvarīm kaccic ca saha sainyasya tava sarvam anāmayam
2.83.6	guhasya tat tu vacanaṃ śrutvā snehād udīritam rāmasyānuvaśo vākyaṃ bharato 'pīdam abravīt
2.83.7	sukhā naḥ śarvarī rājan pūjitāś cāpi te vayam gaṅgāṃ tu naubhir bahvībhir dāśāḥ saṃtārayantu naḥ
2.83.8	tato guhaḥ saṃtvaritaḥ śrutvā bharataśāsanam pratipraviśya nagaraṃ taṃ jñātijanam abravīt
2.83.9	uttiṣṭhata prabudhyadhvaṃ bhadram astu hi vaḥ sadā nāvaḥ samanukarṣadhvaṃ tārayiṣyāma vāhinīm
2.83.10	te tathoktāḥ samutthāya tvaritā rājaśāsanāt pañca nāvāṃ śatāny eva samāninyuḥ samantataḥ
2.83.11	anyāḥ svastikavijñeyā mahāghaṇḍā dharā varāḥ śobhamānāḥ patākinyo yuktavātāḥ susaṃhatāḥ
2.83.12	tataḥ svastikavijñeyāṃ pāṇḍukambalasaṃvṛtām sanandighoṣāṃ kalyāṇīṃ guho nāvam upāharat
2.83.13	tām āruroha bharataḥ śatrughnaś ca mahābalaḥ kausalyā ca sumitrā ca yāś cānyā rājayoṣitaḥ
2.83.14	purohitaś ca tat pūrvaṃ gurave brāhmaṇāś ca ye anantaraṃ rājadārās tathaiva śakaṭāpaṇāḥ
2.83.15	āvāsam ādīpayatāṃ tīrthaṃ cāpy avagāhatām bhāṇḍāni cādadānānāṃ ghoṣas tridivam aspṛśat
2.83.16	patākinyas tu tā nāvaḥ svayaṃ dāśair adhiṣṭhitāḥ vahantyo janam ārūḍhaṃ tadā saṃpetur āśugāḥ
2.83.17	nārīṇām abhipūrṇās tu kāś cit kāś cit tu vājinām kaś cit tatra vahanti sma yānayugyaṃ mahādhanam
2.83.18	tāḥ sma gatvā paraṃ tīram avaropya ca taṃ janam nivṛttāḥ kāṇḍacitrāṇi kriyante dāśabandhubhiḥ
2.83.19	savaijayantās tu gajā gajārohaiḥ pracoditāḥ tarantaḥ sma prakāśante sadhvajā iva parvatāḥ
2.83.20	nāvaś cāruruhus tv anye plavais terus tathāpare anye kumbhaghaṭais terur anye teruś ca bāhubhiḥ
2.83.21	sā puṇyā dhvajinī gaṅgāṃ dāśaiḥ saṃtāritā svayam maitre muhūrte prayayau prayāgavanam uttamam
2.83.22	āśvāsayitvā ca camūṃ mahātmā niveśayitvā ca yathopajoṣam draṣṭuṃ bharadvājam ṛṣipravaryam ṛtvig vṛtaḥ san bharataḥ pratasthe
2.84.1	bharadvājāśramaṃ dṛṣṭvā krośād eva nararṣabhaḥ balaṃ sarvam avasthāpya jagāma saha mantribhiḥ
2.84.2	padbhyām eva hi dharmajño nyastaśastraparicchadaḥ vasāno vāsasī kṣaume purodhāya purohitam
2.84.3	tataḥ saṃdarśane tasya bharadvājasya rāghavaḥ mantriṇas tān avasthāpya jagāmānu purohitam
2.84.4	vasiṣṭham atha dṛṣṭvaiva bharadvājo mahātapāḥ saṃcacālāsanāt tūrṇaṃ śiṣyān arghyam iti bruvan
2.84.5	samāgamya vasiṣṭhena bharatenābhivāditaḥ abudhyata mahātejāḥ sutaṃ daśarathasya tam
2.84.6	tābhyām arghyaṃ ca pādyaṃ ca dattvā paścāt phalāni ca ānupūrvyāc ca dharmajñaḥ papraccha kuśalaṃ kule
2.84.7	ayodhyāyāṃ bale kośe mitreṣv api ca mantriṣu jānan daśarathaṃ vṛttaṃ na rājānam udāharat
2.84.8	vasiṣṭho bharataś cainaṃ papracchatur anāmayam śarīre 'gniṣu vṛkṣeṣu śiṣyeṣu mṛgapakṣiṣu
2.84.9	tatheti ca pratijñāya bharadvājo mahātapāḥ bharataṃ pratyuvācedaṃ rāghavasnehabandhanāt
2.84.10	kim ihāgamane kāryaṃ tava rājyaṃ praśāsataḥ etad ācakṣva me sarvaṃ na hi me śudhyate manaḥ
2.84.11	suṣuve yama mitraghnaṃ kausalyānandavardhanam bhrātrā saha sabhāryo yaś ciraṃ pravrājito vanam
2.84.12	niyuktaḥ strīniyuktena pitrā yo 'sau mahāyaśāḥ vanavāsī bhavetīha samāḥ kila caturdaśa
2.84.13	kaccin na tasyāpāpasya pāpaṃ kartum ihecchasi akaṇṭakaṃ bhoktumanā rājyaṃ tasyānujasya ca
2.84.14	evam ukto bharadvājaṃ bharataḥ pratyuvāca ha paryaśru nayano duḥkhād vācā saṃsajjamānayā
2.84.15	hato 'smi yadi mām evaṃ bhagavān api manyate matto na doṣam āśaṅker naivaṃ mām anuśādhi hi
2.84.16	na caitad iṣṭaṃ mātā me yad avocan madantare nāham etena tuṣṭaś ca na tad vacanam ādade
2.84.17	ahaṃ tu taṃ naravyāghram upayātaḥ prasādakaḥ pratinetum ayodhyāṃ ca pādau tasyābhivanditum
2.84.18	tvaṃ mām evaṃgataṃ matvā prasādaṃ kartum arhasi śaṃsa me bhagavan rāmaḥ kva saṃprati mahīpatiḥ
2.84.19	uvāca taṃ bharadvājaḥ prasādād bharataṃ vacaḥ tvayy etat puruṣavyāghra yuktaṃ rāghavavaṃśaje guruvṛttir damaś caiva sādhūnāṃ cānuyāyitā
2.84.20	jāne caitan manaḥsthaṃ te dṛḍhīkaraṇam astv iti apṛcchaṃ tvāṃ tavātyarthaṃ kīrtiṃ samabhivardhayan
2.84.21	asau vasati te bhrātā citrakūṭe mahāgirau śvas tu gantāsi taṃ deśaṃ vasādya saha mantribhiḥ etaṃ me kuru suprājña kāmaṃ kāmārthakovida
2.84.22	tatas tathety evam udāradarśanaḥ pratītarūpo bharato 'bravīd vacaḥ cakāra buddhiṃ ca tadā mahāśrame niśānivāsāya narādhipātmajaḥ
2.85.1	kṛtabuddhiṃ nivāsāya tathaiva sa munis tadā bharataṃ kaikayī putram ātithyena nyamantrayat
2.85.2	abravīd bharatas tv enaṃ nanv idaṃ bhavatā kṛtam pādyam arghyaṃ tathātithyaṃ vane yad ūpapadyate
2.85.3	athovāca bharadvājo bharataṃ prahasann iva jāne tvāṃ prīti saṃyuktaṃ tuṣyes tvaṃ yena kena cit
2.85.4	senāyās tu tavaitasyāḥ kartum icchāmi bhojanam mama pritir yathā rūpā tvam arho manujarṣabha
2.85.5	kimarthaṃ cāpi nikṣipya dūre balam ihāgataḥ kasmān nehopayāto 'si sabalaḥ puruṣarṣabha
2.85.6	bharataḥ pratyuvācedaṃ prāñjalis taṃ tapodhanam sasainyo nopayāto 'smi bhagavan bhagavad bhayāt
2.85.7	vāji mukhyā manuṣyāś ca mattāś ca vara vāraṇāḥ pracchādya mahatīṃ bhūmiṃ bhagavann anuyānti mām
2.85.8	te vṛkṣān udakaṃ bhūmim āśrameṣūṭajāṃs tathā na hiṃsyur iti tenāham eka evāgatas tataḥ
2.85.9	ānīyatām itaḥ senety ājñaptaḥ paramarṣiṇā tathā tu cakre bharataḥ senāyāḥ samupāgamam
2.85.10	agniśālāṃ praviśyātha pītvāpaḥ parimṛjya ca ātithyasya kriyāhetor viśvakarmāṇam āhvayat
2.85.11	āhvaye viśvakarmāṇam ahaṃ tvaṣṭāram eva ca ātithyaṃ kartum icchāmi tatra me saṃvidhīyatām
2.85.12	prāk srotasaś ca yā nadyaḥ pratyak srotasa eva ca pṛthivyām antarikṣe ca samāyāntv adya sarvaśaḥ
2.85.13	anyāḥ sravantu maireyaṃ surām anyāḥ suniṣṭhitām aparāś codakaṃ śītam ikṣukāṇḍarasopamam
2.85.14	āhvaye devagandharvān viśvāvasuhahāhuhūn tathaivāpsaraso devīr gandharvīś cāpi sarvaśaḥ
2.85.15	ghṛtācīm atha viśvācīṃ miśrakeśīm alambusām śakraṃ yāś copatiṣṭhanti brahmāṇaṃ yāś ca bhāminīḥ sarvās tumburuṇā sārdham āhvaye saparicchadāḥ
2.85.16	vanaṃ kuruṣu yad divyaṃ vāso bhūṣaṇapatravat divyanārīphalaṃ śaśvat tat kauberam ihaiva tu
2.85.17	iha me bhagavān somo vidhattām annam uttamam bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca coṣyaṃ ca lehyaṃ ca vividhaṃ bahu
2.85.18	vicitrāṇi ca mālyāni pādapapracyutāni ca surādīni ca peyāni māṃsāni vividhāni ca
2.85.19	evaṃ samādhinā yuktas tejasāpratimena ca śikṣāsvarasamāyuktaṃ tapasā cābravīn muniḥ
2.85.20	manasā dhyāyatas tasya prāṅmukhasya kṛtāñjaleḥ ājagmus tāni sarvāṇi daivatāni pṛthakpṛthak
2.85.21	malayaṃ durduraṃ caiva tataḥ svedanudo 'nilaḥ upaspṛśya vavau yuktyā supriyātmā sukhaḥ śivaḥ
2.85.22	tato 'bhyavartanta ghanā divyāḥ kusumavṛṣṭayaḥ devadundubhighoṣaś ca dikṣu sarvāsu śuśruve
2.85.23	pravavuś cottamā vātā nanṛtuś cāpsarogaṇāḥ prajagur devagandharvā vīṇā pramumucuḥ svarān
2.85.24	sa śabdo dyāṃ ca bhūmiṃ ca prāṇināṃ śravaṇāni ca viveśoccāritaḥ ślakṣṇaḥ samo layaguṇānvitaḥ
2.85.25	tasminn uparate śabde divye śrotrasukhe nṛṇām dadarśa bhārataṃ sainyaṃ vidhānaṃ viśvakarmaṇaḥ
2.85.26	babhūva hi samā bhūmiḥ samantāt pañcayojanam śādvalair bahubhiś channā nīlavaidūryasaṃnibhaiḥ
2.85.27	tasmin bilvāḥ kapitthāś ca panasā bījapūrakāḥ āmalakyo babhūvuś ca cūtāś ca phalabhūṣaṇāḥ
2.85.28	uttarebhyaḥ kurubhyaś ca vanaṃ divyopabhogavat ājagāma nadī divyā tīrajair bahubhir vṛtā
2.85.29	catuḥśālāni śubhrāṇi śālāś ca gajavājinām harmyaprāsādasaṃghātās toraṇāni śubhāni ca
2.85.30	sitameghanibhaṃ cāpi rājaveśma sutoraṇam śuklamālyakṛtākāraṃ divyagandhasamukṣitam
2.85.31	caturasram asaṃbādhaṃ śayanāsanayānavat divyaiḥ sarvarasair yuktaṃ divyabhojanavastravat
2.85.32	upakalpita sarvānnaṃ dhautanirmalabhājanam kḷptasarvāsanaṃ śrīmat svāstīrṇaśayanottamam
2.85.33	praviveśa mahābāhur anujñāto maharṣiṇā veśma tad ratnasaṃpūrṇaṃ bharataḥ kaikayīsutaḥ
2.85.34	anujagmuś ca taṃ sarve mantriṇaḥ sapurohitāḥ babhūvuś ca mudā yuktā taṃ dṛṣṭvā veśma saṃvidhim
2.85.35	tatra rājāsanaṃ divyaṃ vyajanaṃ chatram eva ca bharato mantribhiḥ sārdham abhyavartata rājavat
2.85.36	āsanaṃ pūjayām āsa rāmāyābhipraṇamya ca vālavyajanam ādāya nyaṣīdat sacivāsane
2.85.37	ānupūrvyān niṣeduś ca sarve mantrapurohitāḥ tataḥ senāpatiḥ paścāt praśāstā ca niṣedatuḥ
2.85.38	tatas tatra muhūrtena nadyaḥ pāyasakardamāḥ upātiṣṭhanta bharataṃ bharadvājasya śāsanat
2.85.39	tāsām ubhayataḥ kūlaṃ pāṇḍumṛttikalepanāḥ ramyāś cāvasathā divyā brahmaṇas tu prasādajāḥ
2.85.40	tenaiva ca muhūrtena divyābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ āgur viṃśatisāhasrā brahmaṇā prahitāḥ striyaḥ
2.85.41	suvarṇamaṇimuktena pravālena ca śobhitāḥ āgur viṃśatisāhasrāḥ kuberaprahitāḥ striyaḥ
2.85.42	yābhir gṛhītaḥ puruṣaḥ sonmāda iva lakṣyate āgur viṃśatisāhasrā nandanād apsarogaṇāḥ
2.85.43	nāradas tumburur gopaḥ parvataḥ sūryavarcasaḥ ete gandharvarājāno bharatasyāgrato jaguḥ
2.85.44	alambusā miśrakeśī puṇḍarīkātha vāmanā upānṛtyaṃs tu bharataṃ bharadvājasya śāsanāt
2.85.45	yāni mālyāni deveṣu yāni caitrarathe vane prayāge tāny adṛśyanta bharadvājasya śāsanāt
2.85.46	bilvā mārdaṅgikā āsañ śamyā grāhā bibhītakāḥ aśvatthā nartakāś cāsan bharadvājasya tejasā
2.85.47	tataḥ saralatālāś ca tilakā naktamālakāḥ prahṛṣṭās tatra saṃpetuḥ kubjābhūtātha vāmanāḥ
2.85.48	śiṃśapāmalakī jambūr yāś cānyāḥ kānane latāḥ pramadā vigrahaṃ kṛtvā bharadvājāśrame 'vasan
2.85.49	surāṃ surāpāḥ pibata pāyasaṃ ca bubhukśitāḥ māṃsani ca sumedhyāni bhakṣyantāṃ yāvad icchatha
2.85.50	utsādya snāpayanti sma nadītīreṣu valguṣu apy ekam ekaṃ puruṣaṃ pramadāḥ satpa cāṣṭa ca
2.85.51	saṃvahantyaḥ samāpetur nāryo ruciralocanāḥ parimṛjya tathānyonyaṃ pāyayanti varāṅganāḥ
2.85.52	hayān gajān kharān uṣṭrāṃs tathaiva surabheḥ sutān ikṣūṃś ca madhujālāṃś ca bhojayanti sma vāhanān ikṣvākuvarayodhānāṃ codayanto mahābalāḥ
2.85.53	nāśvabandho 'śvam ājānān na gajaṃ kuñjaragrahaḥ mattapramattamuditā camūḥ sā tatra saṃbabhau
2.85.54	tarpitā sarvakāmais te raktacandanarūṣitāḥ apsarogaṇasaṃyuktāḥ sainyā vācam udairayan
2.85.55	naivāyodhyāṃ gamiṣyāmo na gamiṣyāma daṇḍakān kuśalaṃ bharatasyāstu rāmasyāstu tathā sukham
2.85.56	iti pādātayodhāś ca hastyaśvārohabandhakāḥ anāthās taṃ vidhiṃ labdhvā vācam etām udairayan
2.85.57	saṃprahṛṣṭā vinedus te narās tatra sahasraśaḥ bharatasyānuyātāraḥ svarge 'yam iti cābruvan
2.85.58	tato bhuktavatāṃ teṣāṃ tad annam amṛtopamam divyān udvīkṣya bhakṣyāṃs tān abhavad bhakṣaṇe matiḥ
2.85.59	preṣyāś ceṭyaś ca vadhvaś ca balasthāś cāpi sarvaśaḥ babhūvus te bhṛśaṃ tṛptāḥ sarve cāhatavāsasaḥ
2.85.60	kuñjarāś ca kharoṣṭraś ca go'śvāś ca mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ babhūvuḥ subhṛtās tatra nānyo hy anyam akalpayat
2.85.61	nāśuklavāsās tatrāsīt kṣudhito malino 'pi vā rajasā dhvastakeśo vā naraḥ kaś cid adṛśyata
2.85.62	ājaiś cāpi ca vārāhair niṣṭhānavarasaṃcayaiḥ phalaniryūhasaṃsiddhaiḥ sūpair gandharasānvitaiḥ
2.85.63	puṣpadhvajavatīḥ pūrṇāḥ śuklasyānnasya cābhitaḥ dadṛśur vismitās tatra narā lauhīḥ sahasraśaḥ
2.85.64	babhūvur vanapārśveṣu kūpāḥ pāyasakardamāḥ tāś ca kāmadughā gāvo drumāś cāsan madhuścyutaḥ
2.85.65	vāpyo maireyapūrṇāś ca mṛṣṭamāṃsacayair vṛtāḥ prataptapiṭharaiś cāpi mārgamāyūrakaukkuṭaiḥ
2.85.66	pātrīṇāṃ ca sahasrāṇi śātakumbhamayāni ca sthālyaḥ kumbhyaḥ karambhyaś ca dadhipūrṇāḥ susaṃskṛtāḥ yauvanasthasya gaurasya kapitthasya sugandhinaḥ
2.85.67	hradāḥ pūrṇā rasālasya dadhnaḥ śvetasya cāpare babhūvuḥ pāyasasyānte śarkarāyāś ca saṃcayāḥ
2.85.68	kalkāṃś cūrṇakaṣāyāṃś ca snānāni vividhāni ca dadṛśur bhājanasthāni tīrtheṣu saritāṃ narāḥ
2.85.69	śuklān aṃśumataś cāpi dantadhāvanasaṃcayān śuklāṃś candanakalkāṃś ca samudgeṣv avatiṣṭhataḥ
2.85.70	darpaṇān parimṛṣṭāṃś ca vāsasāṃ cāpi saṃcayān pādukopānahāṃ caiva yugmān yatra sahasraśaḥ
2.85.71	āñjanīḥ kaṅkatān kūrcāṃś chatrāṇi ca dhanūṃṣi ca marmatrāṇāni citrāṇi śayanāny āsanāni ca
2.85.72	pratipānahradān pūrṇān kharoṣṭragajavājinām avagāhya sutīrthāṃś ca hradān sotpala puṣkarān
