2.85.73	nīlavaidūryavarṇāṃś ca mṛdūn yavasasaṃcayān nirvāpārthaṃ paśūnāṃ te dadṛśus tatra sarvaśaḥ
2.85.74	vyasmayanta manuṣyās te svapnakalpaṃ tad adbhutam dṛṣṭvātithyaṃ kṛtaṃ tādṛg bharatasya maharṣiṇā
2.85.75	ity evaṃ ramamāṇānāṃ devānām iva nandane bharadvājāśrame ramye sā rātrir vyatyavartata
2.85.76	pratijagmuś ca tā nadyo gandharvāś ca yathāgatam bharadvājam anujñāpya tāś ca sarvā varāṅganāḥ
2.85.77	tathaiva mattā madirotkaṭā narās tathaiva divyāgurucandanokṣitāḥ tathaiva divyā vividhāḥ sraguttamāḥ pṛthakprakīrṇā manujaiḥ pramarditāḥ
2.86.1	tatas tāṃ rajanīm uṣya bharataḥ saparicchadaḥ kṛtātithyo bharadvājaṃ kāmād abhijagāma ha
2.86.2	tam ṛṣiḥ puruṣavyāghraṃ prekṣya prāñjalim āgatam hutāgnihotro bharataṃ bharadvājo 'bhyabhāṣata
2.86.3	kaccid atra sukhā rātris tavāsmadviṣaye gatā samagras te janaḥ kaccid ātithye śaṃsa me 'nagha
2.86.4	tam uvācāñjaliṃ kṛtvā bharato 'bhipraṇamya ca āśramād abhiniṣkrantam ṛṣim uttama tejasaṃ
2.86.5	sukhoṣito 'smi bhagavan samagrabalavāhanaḥ tarpitaḥ sarvakāmaiś ca sāmātyo balavat tvayā
2.86.6	apetaklamasaṃtāpāḥ subhakṣyāḥ supratiśrayāḥ api preṣyān upādāya sarve sma susukhoṣitāḥ
2.86.7	āmantraye 'haṃ bhagavan kāmaṃ tvām ṛṣisattama samīpaṃ prasthitaṃ bhrātur maireṇekṣasva cakṣuṣā
2.86.8	āśramaṃ tasya dharmajña dhārmikasya mahātmanaḥ ācakṣva katamo mārgaḥ kiyān iti ca śaṃsa me
2.86.9	iti pṛṣṭas tu bharataṃ bhrātṛdarśanalālasaṃ pratyuvāca mahātejā bharadvājo mahātapāḥ
2.86.10	bharatārdhatṛtīyeṣu yojaneṣv ajane vane citrakūṭo giris tatra ramyanirdarakānanaḥ
2.86.11	uttaraṃ pārśvam āsādya tasya mandākinī nadī puṣpitadrumasaṃchannā ramyapuṣpitakānanā
2.86.12	anantaraṃ tat saritaś citrakūṭaś ca parvataḥ tato parṇakuṭī tāta tatra tau vasato dhruvam
2.86.13	dakṣiṇenaiva mārgeṇa savyadakṣiṇam eva ca gajavājirathākīrṇāṃ vāhinīṃ vāhinīpate vāhayasva mahābhāga tato drakṣyasi rāghavam
2.86.14	prayāṇam iti ca śrutvā rājarājasya yoṣitaḥ hitvā yānāni yānārhā brāhmaṇaṃ paryavārayan
2.86.15	vepamānā kṛśā dīnā saha devyā sumantriyā kausalyā tatra jagrāha karābhyāṃ caraṇau muneḥ
2.86.16	asamṛddhena kāmena sarvalokasya garhitā kaikeyī tasya jagrāha caraṇau savyapatrapā
2.86.17	taṃ pradakṣiṇam āgamya bhagavantaṃ mahāmunim adūrād bharatasyaiva tasthau dīnamanās tadā
2.86.18	tataḥ papraccha bharataṃ bharadvājo dṛḍhavrataḥ viśeṣaṃ jñātum icchāmi mātṝṇāṃ tava rāghava
2.86.19	evam uktas tu bharato bharadvājena dhārmikaḥ uvāca prāñjalir bhūtvā vākyaṃ vacanakovidaḥ
2.86.20	yām imāṃ bhagavan dīnāṃ śokān aśanakarśitām pitur hi mahiṣīṃ devīṃ devatām iva paśyasi
2.86.21	eṣā taṃ puruṣavyāghraṃ siṃhavikrāntagāminam kausalyā suṣuve rāmaṃ dhātāram aditir yathā
2.86.22	asyā vāmabhujaṃ śliṣṭā yaiṣā tiṣṭhati durmanāḥ karṇikārasya śākheva śīrṇapuṣpā vanāntare
2.86.23	etasyās tau sutau devyāḥ kumārau devavarṇinau ubhau lakṣmaṇaśatrughnau vīrau satyaparākramau
2.86.24	yasyāḥ kṛte naravyāghrau jīvanāśam ito gatau rājā putravihīnaś ca svargaṃ daśaratho gataḥ
2.86.25	aiśvaryakāmāṃ kaikeyīm anāryām āryarūpiṇīm mamaitāṃ mātaraṃ viddhi nṛśaṃsāṃ pāpaniścayām yatomūlaṃ hi paśyāmi vyasanaṃ mahad ātmanaḥ
2.86.26	ity uktvā naraśārdūlo bāṣpagadgadayā girā sa niśaśvāsa tāmrākṣo kruddho nāga ivāsakṛt
2.86.27	bharadvājo maharṣis taṃ bruvantaṃ bharataṃ tadā pratyuvāca mahābuddhir idaṃ vacanam arthavat
2.86.28	na doṣeṇāvagantavyā kaikeyī bharata tvayā rāmapravrājanaṃ hy etat sukhodarkaṃ bhaviṣyati
2.86.29	abhivādya tu saṃsiddhaḥ kṛtvā cainaṃ pradakṣiṇam āmantrya bharataḥ sainyaṃ yujyatām ity acodayat
2.86.30	tato vājirathān yuktvā divyān hemapariṣkritān adhyārohat prayāṇārthī bahūn bahuvidho janaḥ
2.86.31	gajakanyāgajāś caiva hemakakṣyāḥ patākinaḥ jīmūtā iva gharmānte saghoṣāḥ saṃpratasthire
2.86.32	vividhāny api yānāni mahāni ca laghūni ca prayayuḥ sumahārhāṇi pādair eva padātayaḥ
2.86.33	atha yānapravekais tu kausalyāpramukhāḥ striyaḥ rāmadarśanakāṅkṣiṇyaḥ prayayur muditās tadā
2.86.34	sa cārkataruṇābhāsāṃ niyuktāṃ śibikāṃ śubhām āsthāya prayayau śrīmān bharataḥ saparicchadaḥ
2.86.35	sā prayātā mahāsenā gajavājirathākulā dakṣiṇāṃ diśam āvṛtya mahāmegha ivotthitaḥ vanāni tu vyatikramya juṣṭāni mṛgapakṣibhiḥ
2.86.36	sā saṃprahṛṣṭadvipavājiyodhā vitrāsayantī mṛgapakṣisaṃghān mahad vanaṃ tat pravigāhamānā rarāja senā bharatasya tatra
2.87.1	tayā mahatyā yāyinyā dhvajinyā vanavāsinaḥ arditā yūthapā mattāḥ sayūthāḥ saṃpradudruvuḥ
2.87.2	ṛkṣāḥ pṛṣatasaṃghāś ca ruravaś ca samantataḥ dṛśyante vanarājīṣu giriṣv api nadīṣu ca
2.87.3	sa saṃpratasthe dharmātmā prīto daśarathātmajaḥ vṛto mahatyā nādinyā senayā caturaṅgayā
2.87.4	sāgaraughanibhā senā bharatasya mahātmanaḥ mahīṃ saṃchādayām āsa prāvṛṣi dyām ivāmbudaḥ
2.87.5	turaṃgaughair avatatā vāraṇaiś ca mahājavaiḥ anālakṣyā ciraṃ kālaṃ tasmin kāle babhūva bhūḥ
2.87.6	sa yātvā dūram adhvānaṃ supariśrānta vāhanaḥ uvāca bharataḥ śrīmān vasiṣṭhaṃ mantriṇāṃ varam
2.87.7	yādṛśaṃ lakṣyate rūpaṃ yathā caiva śrutaṃ mayā vyaktaṃ prāptāḥ sma taṃ deśaṃ bharadvājo yam abravīt
2.87.8	ayaṃ giriś citrakūṭas tathā mandākinī nadī etat prakāśate dūrān nīlameghanibhaṃ vanam
2.87.9	gireḥ sānūni ramyāṇi citrakūṭasya saṃprati vāraṇair avamṛdyante māmakaiḥ parvatopamaiḥ
2.87.10	muñcanti kusumāny ete nagāḥ parvatasānuṣu nīlā ivātapāpāye toyaṃ toyadharā ghanāḥ
2.87.11	kinnarācaritoddeśaṃ paśya śatrughna parvatam hayaiḥ samantād ākīrṇaṃ makarair iva sāgaram
2.87.12	ete mṛgagaṇā bhānti śīghravegāḥ pracoditāḥ vāyupraviddhāḥ śaradi megharājya ivāmbare
2.87.13	kurvanti kusumāpīḍāñ śiraḥsu surabhīn amī meghaprakāśaiḥ phalakair dākṣiṇātyā yathā narāḥ
2.87.14	niṣkūjam iva bhūtvedaṃ vanaṃ ghorapradarśanam ayodhyeva janākīrṇā saṃprati pratibhāti mā
2.87.15	khurair udīrito reṇur divaṃ pracchādya tiṣṭhati taṃ vahaty anilaḥ śīghraṃ kurvann iva mama priyam
2.87.16	syandanāṃs turagopetān sūtamukhyair adhiṣṭhitān etān saṃpatataḥ śīghraṃ paśya śatrughna kānane
2.87.17	etān vitrāsitān paśya barhiṇaḥ priyadarśanān etam āviśataḥ śailam adhivāsaṃ patatriṇām
2.87.18	atimātram ayaṃ deśo manojñaḥ pratibhāti mā tāpasānāṃ nivāso 'yaṃ vyaktaṃ svargapatho yathā
2.87.19	mṛgā mṛgībhiḥ sahitā bahavaḥ pṛṣatā vane manojña rūpā lakṣyante kusumair iva citritaḥ
2.87.20	sādhu sainyāḥ pratiṣṭhantāṃ vicinvantu ca kānanam yathā tau puruṣavyāghrau dṛśyete rāmalakṣmaṇau
2.87.21	bharatasya vacaḥ śrutvā puruṣāḥ śastrapāṇayaḥ viviśus tad vanaṃ śūrā dhūmaṃ ca dadṛśus tataḥ
2.87.22	te samālokya dhūmāgram ūcur bharatam āgatāḥ nāmanuṣye bhavaty agnir vyaktam atraiva rāghavau
2.87.23	atha nātra naravyāghrau rājaputrau paraṃtapau anye rāmopamāḥ santi vyaktam atra tapasvinaḥ
2.87.24	tac chrutvā bharatas teṣāṃ vacanaṃ sādhu saṃmatam sainyān uvāca sarvāṃs tān amitrabalamardanaḥ
2.87.25	yat tā bhavantas tiṣṭhantu neto gantavyam agrataḥ aham eva gamiṣyāmi sumantro gurur eva ca
2.87.26	evam uktās tataḥ sarve tatra tasthuḥ samantataḥ bharato yatra dhūmāgraṃ tatra dṛṣṭiṃ samādadhat
2.87.27	vyavasthitā yā bharatena sā camūr nirīkṣamāṇāpi ca dhūmam agrataḥ babhūva hṛṣṭā nacireṇa jānatī priyasya rāmasya samāgamaṃ tadā
2.88.1	dīrghakāloṣitas tasmin girau girivanapriyaḥ videhyāḥ priyamākāṅkṣan svaṃ ca cittaṃ vilobhayan
2.88.2	atha dāśarathiś citraṃ citrakūṭam adarśayat bhāryām amarasaṃkāśaḥ śacīm iva puraṃdaraḥ
2.88.3	na rājyād bhraṃśanaṃ bhadre na suhṛdbhir vinābhavaḥ mano me bādhate dṛṣṭvā ramaṇīyam imaṃ girim
2.88.4	paśyemam acalaṃ bhadre nānādvijagaṇāyutam śikharaiḥ kham ivodviddhair dhātumadbhir vibhūṣitam
2.88.5	ke cid rajatasaṃkāśāḥ ke cit kṣatajasaṃnibhāḥ pītamāñjiṣṭhavarṇāś ca ke cin maṇivaraprabhāḥ
2.88.6	puṣyārkaketukābhāś ca ke cij jyotī rasaprabhāḥ virājante 'calendrasya deśā dhātuvibhūṣitāḥ
2.88.7	nānāmṛgagaṇadvīpitarakṣvṛkṣagaṇair vṛtaḥ aduṣṭair bhāty ayaṃ śailo bahupakṣisamākulaḥ
2.88.8	āmrajambvasanair lodhraiḥ priyālaiḥ panasair dhavaiḥ aṅkolair bhavyatiniśair bilvatindukaveṇubhiḥ
2.88.9	kāśmaryariṣṭavaraṇair madhūkais tilakais tathā badaryāmalakair nīpair vetradhanvanabījakaiḥ
2.88.10	puṣpavadbhiḥ phalopetaiś chāyāvadbhir manoramaiḥ evamādibhir ākīrṇaḥ śriyaṃ puṣyaty ayaṃ giriḥ
2.88.11	śailaprastheṣu ramyeṣu paśyemān kāmaharṣaṇān kinnarān dvaṃdvaśo bhadre ramamāṇān manasvinaḥ
2.88.12	śākhāvasaktān khaḍgāṃś ca pravarāṇy ambarāṇi ca paśya vidyādharastrīṇāṃ krīḍed deśān manoramān
2.88.13	jalaprapātair udbhedair niṣyandaiś ca kva cit kva cit sravadbhir bhāty ayaṃ śailaḥ sravan mada iva dvipaḥ
2.88.14	guhāsamīraṇo gandhān nānāpuṣpabhavān vahan ghrāṇatarpaṇam abhyetya kaṃ naraṃ na praharṣayet
2.88.15	yadīha śarado 'nekās tvayā sārdham anindite lakṣmaṇena ca vatsyāmi na māṃ śokaḥ pradhakṣyati
2.88.16	bahupuṣpaphale ramye nānādvijagaṇāyute vicitraśikhare hy asmin ratavān asmi bhāmini
2.88.17	anena vanavāsena mayā prāptaṃ phaladvayam pituś cānṛṇatā dharme bharatasya priyaṃ tathā
2.88.18	vaidehi ramase kaccic citrakūṭe mayā saha paśyantī vividhān bhāvān manovākkāyasaṃyatān
2.88.19	idam evāmṛtaṃ prāhū rājñāṃ rājarṣayaḥ pare vanavāsaṃ bhavārthāya pretya me prapitāmahāḥ
2.88.20	śilāḥ śailasya śobhante viśālāḥ śataśo 'bhitaḥ bahulā bahulair varṇair nīlapītasitāruṇaiḥ
2.88.21	niśi bhānty acalendrasya hutāśanaśikhā iva oṣadhyaḥ svaprabhā lakṣmyā bhrājamānāḥ sahasraśaḥ
2.88.22	ke cit kṣayanibhā deśāḥ ke cid udyānasaṃnibhāḥ ke cid ekaśilā bhānti parvatasyāsya bhāmini
2.88.23	bhittveva vasudhāṃ bhāti citrakūṭaḥ samutthitaḥ citrakūṭasya kūṭo 'sau dṛśyate sarvataḥ śivaḥ
2.88.24	kuṣṭhapuṃnāgatagarabhūrjapatrottaracchadān kāmināṃ svāstarān paśya kuśeśayadalāyutān
2.88.25	mṛditāś cāpaviddhāś ca dṛśyante kamalasrajaḥ kāmibhir vanite paśya phalāni vividhāni ca
2.88.26	vasvaukasārāṃ nalinīm atyetīvottarān kurūn parvataś citrakūṭo 'sau bahumūlaphalodakaḥ
2.88.27	imaṃ tu kālaṃ vanite vijahrivāṃs tvayā ca sīte saha lakṣmaṇena ca ratiṃ prapatsye kuladharmavardhinīṃ satāṃ pathi svair niyamaiḥ paraiḥ sthitaḥ
2.89.1	atha śailād viniṣkramya maithilīṃ kosaleśvaraḥ adarśayac chubhajalāṃ ramyāṃ mandākinīṃ nadīm
2.89.2	abravīc ca varārohāṃ cārucandranibhānanām videharājasya sutāṃ rāmo rājīvalocanaḥ
2.89.3	vicitrapulināṃ ramyāṃ haṃsasārasasevitām kusumair upasaṃpannāṃ paśya mandākinīṃ nadīm
2.89.4	nānāvidhais tīraruhair vṛtāṃ puṣpaphaladrumaiḥ rājantīṃ rājarājasya nalinīm iva sarvataḥ
2.89.5	mṛgayūthanipītāni kaluṣāmbhāṃsi sāmpratam tīrthāni ramaṇīyāni ratiṃ saṃjanayanti me
2.89.6	jaṭājinadharāḥ kāle valkalottaravāsasaḥ ṛṣayas tv avagāhante nadīṃ mandākinīṃ priye
2.89.7	ādityam upatiṣṭhante niyamād ūrdhvabāhavaḥ ete 'pare viśālākṣi munayaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ
2.89.8	mārutoddhūta śikharaiḥ pranṛtta iva parvataḥ pādapaiḥ patrapuṣpāṇi sṛjadbhir abhito nadīm
2.89.9	kaccin maṇinikāśodāṃ kaccit pulinaśālinīm kaccit siddhajanākīrṇāṃ paśya mandākinīṃ nadīm
2.89.10	nirdhūtān vāyunā paśya vitatān puṣpasaṃcayān poplūyamānān aparān paśya tvaṃ jalamadhyagān
2.89.11	tāṃś cātivalgu vacaso rathāṅgāhvayanā dvijāḥ adhirohanti kalyāṇi niṣkūjantaḥ śubhā giraḥ
2.89.12	darśanaṃ citrakūṭasya mandākinyāś ca śobhane adhikaṃ puravāsāc ca manye ca tava darśanāt
2.89.13	vidhūtakaluṣaiḥ siddhais tapodamaśamānvitaiḥ nityavikṣobhita jalāṃ vihāhasva mayā saha
2.89.14	sakhīvac ca vigāhasva sīte mandakinīm imām kamalāny avamajjantī puṣkarāṇi ca bhāmini
2.89.15	tvaṃ paurajanavad vyālān ayodhyām iva parvatam manyasva vanite nityaṃ sarayūvad imāṃ nadīm
2.89.16	lakṣmaṇaś caiva dharmātmā mannideśe vyavasthitaḥ tvaṃ cānukūlā vaidehi prītiṃ janayatho mama
2.89.17	upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇaṃ madhumūlaphalāśanaḥ nāyodhyāyai na rājyāya spṛhaye 'dya tvayā saha
2.89.18	imāṃ hi ramyāṃ gajayūthalolitāṃ nipītatoyāṃ gajasiṃhavānaraiḥ supuṣpitaiḥ puṣpadharair alaṃkṛtāṃ na so 'sti yaḥ syān na gatakramaḥ sukhī
2.89.19	itīva rāmo bahusaṃgataṃ vacaḥ priyā sahāyaḥ saritaṃ prati bruvan cacāra ramyaṃ nayanāñjanaprabhaṃ sa citrakūṭaṃ raghuvaṃśavardhanaḥ
2.90.1	tathā tatrāsatas tasya bharatasyopayāyinaḥ sainya reṇuś ca śabdaś ca prādurāstāṃ nabhaḥ spṛśau
2.90.2	etasminn antare trastāḥ śabdena mahatā tataḥ arditā yūthapā mattāḥ sayūthā dudruvur diśaḥ
2.90.3	sa taṃ sainyasamudbhūtaṃ śabdaṃ śuśrava rāghavaḥ tāṃś ca vipradrutān sarvān yūthapān anvavaikṣata
2.90.4	tāṃś ca vidravato dṛṣṭvā taṃ ca śrutvā sa niḥsvanam uvāca rāmaḥ saumitriṃ lakṣmaṇaṃ dīptatejasaṃ
2.90.5	hanta lakṣmaṇa paśyeha sumitrā suprajās tvayā bhīmastanitagambhīras tumulaḥ śrūyate svanaḥ
2.90.6	rājā vā rājamātro vā mṛgayām aṭate vane anyad vā śvāpadaṃ kiṃ cit saumitre jñātum arhasi sarvam etad yathātattvam acirāj jñātum arhasi
2.90.7	sa lakṣmaṇaḥ saṃtvaritaḥ sālam āruhya puṣpitam prekṣamāṇo diśaḥ sarvāḥ pūrvāṃ diśam avaikṣata
2.90.8	udaṅmukhaḥ prekṣamāṇo dadarśa mahatīṃ camūm rathāśvagajasaṃbādhāṃ yattair yuktāṃ padātibhiḥ
2.90.9	tām aśvagajasaṃpūrṇāṃ rathadhvajavibhūṣitām śaśaṃsa senāṃ rāmāya vacanaṃ cedam abravīt
2.90.10	agniṃ saṃśamayatv āryaḥ sītā ca bhajatāṃ guhām sajyaṃ kuruṣva cāpaṃ ca śarāṃś ca kavacaṃ tathā
2.90.11	taṃ rāmaḥ puruṣavyāghro lakṣmaṇaṃ pratyuvāca ha aṅgāvekṣasva saumitre kasyaitāṃ manyase camūm
2.90.12	evam ukktas tu rāmeṇa lakṣmāṇo vākyam abravīt didhakṣann iva tāṃ senāṃ ruṣitaḥ pāvako yathā
2.90.13	saṃpannaṃ rājyam icchaṃs tu vyaktaṃ prāpyābhiṣecanam āvāṃ hantuṃ samabhyeti kaikeyyā bharataḥ sutaḥ
2.90.14	eṣa vai sumahāñ śrīmān viṭapī saṃprakāśate virājaty udgataskandhaḥ kovidāra dhvajo rathe
2.90.15	bhajanty ete yathākāmam aśvān āruhya śīghragān ete bhrājanti saṃhṛṣṭā gajān āruhya sādinaḥ
2.90.16	gṛhītadhanuṣau cāvāṃ giriṃ vīra śrayāvahe atha vehaiva tiṣṭhāvaḥ saṃnaddhāv udyatāyudhau api nau vaśam āgacchet kovidāradhvajo raṇe
2.90.17	api drakṣyāmi bharataṃ yatkṛte vyasanaṃ mahat tvayā rāghava saṃprāptaṃ sītayā ca mayā tathā
2.90.18	yannimittaṃ bhavān rājyāc cyuto rāghava śāśvatīm saṃprāpto 'yam arir vīra bharato vadhya eva me
2.90.19	bharatasya vadhe doṣaṃ nāhaṃ paśyāmi rāghava pūrvāpakāriṇāṃ tyāge na hy adharmo vidhīyate etasmin nihate kṛtsnām anuśādhi vasuṃdharām
2.90.20	adya putraṃ hataṃ saṃkhye kaikeyī rājyakāmukā mayā paśyet suduḥkhārtā hastibhagnam iva drumam
2.90.21	kaikeyīṃ ca vadhiṣyāmi sānubandhāṃ sabāndhavām kaluṣeṇādya mahatā medinī parimucyatām
2.90.22	adyemaṃ saṃyataṃ krodham asatkāraṃ ca mānada mokṣyāmi śatrusainyeṣu kakṣeṣv iva hutāśanam
2.90.23	adyaitac citrakūṭasya kānanaṃ niśitaiḥ śaraiḥ bhindañ śatruśarīrāṇi kariṣye śoṇitokṣitam
2.90.24	śarair nirbhinnahṛdayān kuñjarāṃs turagāṃs tathā śvāpadāḥ parikarṣantu narāś ca nihatān mayā
2.90.25	śarāṇāṃ dhanuṣaś cāham anṛṇo 'smi mahāvane sasainyaṃ bharataṃ hatvā bhaviṣyāmi na saṃśayaḥ
2.91.1	susaṃrabdhaṃ tu saumitriṃ lakṣmaṇaṃ krodhamūrchitam rāmas tu parisāntvyātha vacanaṃ cedam abravīt
2.91.2	kim atra dhanuṣā kāryam asinā vā sacarmaṇā maheṣvāse mahāprājñe bharate svayam āgate
2.91.3	prāptakālaṃ yad eṣo 'smān bharato draṣṭum icchati asmāsu manasāpy eṣa nāhitaṃ kiṃ cid ācaret
2.91.4	vipriyaṃ kṛtapūrvaṃ te bharatena kadā na kim īdṛśaṃ vā bhayaṃ te 'dya bharataṃ yo 'tra śaṅkase
2.91.5	na hi te niṣṭhuraṃ vācyo bharato nāpriyaṃ vacaḥ ahaṃ hy apriyam uktaḥ syāṃ bharatasyāpriye kṛte
2.91.6	kathaṃ nu putrāḥ pitaraṃ hanyuḥ kasyāṃ cid āpadi bhrātā vā bhrātaraṃ hanyāt saumitre prāṇam ātmanaḥ
2.91.7	yadi rājyasya hetos tvam imāṃ vācaṃ prabhāṣase vakṣyāmi bharataṃ dṛṣṭvā rājyam asmai pradīyatām
2.91.8	ucyamāno hi bharato mayā lakṣmaṇa tattvataḥ rājyam asmai prayaccheti bāḍham ity eva vakṣyati
2.91.9	tathokto dharmaśīlena bhrātrā tasya hite rataḥ lakṣmaṇaḥ praviveśeva svāni gātrāṇi lajjayā
2.91.10	vrīḍitaṃ lakṣmaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā rāghavaḥ pratyuvāca ha eṣa manye mahābāhur ihāsmān draṣṭum āgataḥ
2.91.11	vanavāsam anudhyāya gṛhāya pratineṣyati imāṃ vāpy eśa vaidehīm atyantasukhasevinīm
2.91.12	etau tau saṃprakāśete gotravantau manoramau vāyuvegasamau vīra javanau turagottamau
2.91.13	sa eṣa sumahākāyaḥ kampate vāhinīmukhe nāgaḥ śatruṃjayo nāma vṛddhas tātasya dhīmataḥ
2.91.14	avatīrya tu sālāgrāt tasmāt sa samitiṃjayaḥ lakṣmaṇaḥ prāñjalir bhūtvā tasthau rāmasya pārśvataḥ
2.91.15	bharatenātha saṃdiṣṭā saṃmardo na bhaved iti samantāt tasya śailasya senāvāsam akalpayat
2.91.16	adhyardham ikṣvākucamūr yojanaṃ parvatasya sā pārśve nyaviśad āvṛtya gajavājirathākulā
2.91.17	sā citrakūṭe bharatena senā dharmaṃ puraskṛtya vidhūya darpam prasādanārthaṃ raghunandanasya virocate nītimatā praṇītā
2.92.1	niveśya senāṃ tu vibhuḥ padbhyāṃ pādavatāṃ varaḥ abhigantuṃ sa kākutstham iyeṣa guruvartakam
2.92.2	niviṣṭa mātre sainye tu yathoddeśaṃ vinītavat bharato bhrātaraṃ vākyaṃ śatrughnam idam abravīt
2.92.3	kṣipraṃ vanam idaṃ saumya narasaṃghaiḥ samantataḥ lubdhaiś ca sahitair ebhis tvam anveṣitum arhasi
2.92.4	yāvan na rāmaṃ drakṣyāmi lakṣmaṇaṃ vā mahābalam vaidehīṃ vā mahābhāgāṃ na me śāntir bhaviṣyati
2.92.5	yāvan na candrasaṃkāśaṃ drakṣyāmi śubham ānanam bhrātuḥ padmapalāśākṣaṃ na me śāntir bhaviṣyati
2.92.6	yāvan na caraṇau bhrātuḥ pārthiva vyañjanānvitau śirasā dhārayiṣyāmi na me śāntir bhaviṣyati
2.92.7	yāvan na rājye rājyārhaḥ pitṛpaitāmahe sthitaḥ abhiṣekajalaklinno na me śāntir bhaviṣyati
2.92.8	kṛtakṛtyā mahābhāgā vaidehī janakātmajā bhartāraṃ sāgarāntāyāḥ pṛthivyā yānugacchati
2.92.9	subhagaś citrakūṭo 'sau girirājopamo giriḥ yasmin vasati kākutsthaḥ kubera ivanandane
2.92.10	kṛtakāryam idaṃ durgaṃ vanaṃ vyālaniṣevitam yad adhyāste mahātejā rāmaḥ śastrabhṛtāṃ varaḥ
2.92.11	evam uktvā mahātejā bharataḥ puruṣarṣabhaḥ padbhyām eva mahātejāḥ praviveśa mahad vanam
2.92.12	sa tāni drumajālāni jātāni girisānuṣu puṣpitāgrāṇi madhyena jagāma vadatāṃ varaḥ
2.92.13	sa gireś citrakūṭasya sālam āsādya puṣpitam rāmāśramagatasyāgner dadarśa dhvajam ucchritam
2.92.14	taṃ dṛṣṭvā bharataḥ śrīmān mumoda sahabāndhavaḥ atra rāma iti jñātvā gataḥ pāram ivāmbhasaḥ
2.92.15	sa citrakūṭe tu girau niśāmya rāmāśramaṃ puṇyajanopapannam guhena sārdhaṃ tvarito jagāma punar niveśyaiva camūṃ mahātmā
2.93.1	niviṣṭāyāṃ tu senāyām utsuko bharatas tadā jagāma bhrātaraṃ draṣṭuṃ śatrughnam anudarśayan
2.93.2	ṛṣiṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ saṃdiśya mātṝr me śīghram ānaya iti taritam agre sa jāgama guruvatsalaḥ
2.93.3	sumantras tv api śatughnam adūrād anvapadyata rāmadārśanajas tarṣo bharatasyeva tasya ca
2.93.4	gacchann evātha bharatas tāpasālayasaṃsthitām bhrātuḥ parṇakuṭīṃ śrīmān uṭajaṃ ca dadarśa ha
2.93.5	śālāyās tv agratas tasyā dadarśa bharatas tadā kāṣṭāni cāvabhagnāni puṣpāṇy avacitāni ca
2.93.6	dadarśa ca vane tasmin mahataḥ saṃcayān kṛtān mṛgāṇāṃ mahiṣāṇāṃ ca karīṣaiḥ śītakāraṇāt
2.93.7	gacchan eva mahābāhur dyutimān bharatas tadā śatrughnaṃ cābravīd dhṛṣṭas tān amātyāṃś ca sarvaśaḥ
2.93.8	manye prāptāḥ sma taṃ deśaṃ bharadvājo yam abravīt nātidūre hi manye 'haṃ nadīṃ mandākinīm itaḥ
2.93.9	uccair baddhāni cīrāṇi lakṣmaṇena bhaved ayam abhijñānakṛtaḥ panthā vikāle gantum icchatā
2.93.10	idaṃ codāttadantānāṃ kuñjarāṇāṃ tarasvinām śailapārśve parikrāntam anyonyam abhigarjatām
2.93.11	yam evādhātum icchanti tāpasāḥ satataṃ vane tasyāsau dṛśyate dhūmaḥ saṃkulaḥ kṛṣṭavartmanaḥ
2.93.12	atrāhaṃ puruṣavyāghraṃ gurusatkārakāriṇam āryaṃ drakṣyāmi saṃhṛṣṭo maharṣim iva rāghavam
2.93.13	atha gatvā muhūrtaṃ tu citrakūṭaṃ sa rāghavaḥ mandākinīm anuprāptas taṃ janaṃ cedam abravīt
2.93.14	jagatyāṃ puruṣavyāghra āste vīrāsane rataḥ janendro nirjanaṃ prāpya dhin me janma sajīvitam
2.93.15	matkṛte vyasanaṃ prāpto lokanātho mahādyutiḥ sarān kāmān parityajya vane vasati rāghavaḥ
2.93.16	iti lokasamākruṣṭaḥ pādeṣv adya prasādayan rāmasya nipatiṣyāmi sītāyāś ca punaḥ punaḥ
2.93.17	evaṃ sa vilapaṃs tasmin vane daśarathātmajaḥ dadarśa mahatīṃ puṇyāṃ parṇaśālāṃ manoramām
2.93.18	sālatālāśvakarṇānāṃ parṇair bahubhir āvṛtām viśālāṃ mṛdubhis tīrṇāṃ kuśair vedim ivādhvare
2.93.19	śakrāyudha nikāśaiś ca kārmukair bhārasādhanaiḥ rukmapṛṣṭhair mahāsāraiḥ śobhitāṃ śatrubādhakaiḥ
2.93.20	arkaraśmipratīkāśair ghorais tūṇīgataiḥ śaraiḥ śobhitāṃ dīptavadanaiḥ sarpair bhogavatīm iva
2.93.21	mahārajatavāsobhyām asibhyāṃ ca virājitām rukmabinduvicitrābhyāṃ carmabhyāṃ cāpi śobhitām
2.93.22	godhāṅgulitrair āsāktaiś citraiḥ kāñcanabhūṣitaiḥ arisaṃghair anādhṛṣyāṃ mṛgaiḥ siṃhaguhām iva
2.93.23	prāgudaksravaṇāṃ vediṃ viśālāṃ dīptapāvakām dadarśa bharatas tatra puṇyāṃ rāmaniveśane
2.93.24	nirīkṣya sa muhūrtaṃ tu dadarśa bharato gurum uṭaje rāmam āsīnāṃ jaṭāmaṇḍaladhāriṇam
2.93.25	taṃ tu kṛṣṇājinadharaṃ cīravalkalavāsasaṃ dadarśa rāmam āsīnam abhitaḥ pāvakopamam
2.93.26	siṃhaskandhaṃ mahābāhuṃ puṇḍarīkanibhekṣaṇam pṛthivyāḥ sagarāntāyā bhartāraṃ dharmacāriṇam
2.93.27	upaviṣṭaṃ mahābāhuṃ brahmāṇam iva śāśvatam sthaṇḍile darbhasasmtīrṇe sītayā lakṣmaṇena ca
2.93.28	taṃ dṛṣṭvā bharataḥ śrīmān duḥkhamohapariplutaḥ abhyadhāvata dharmātmā bharataḥ kaikayīsutaḥ
2.93.29	dṛṣṭvā ca vilalāpārto bāṣpasaṃdigdhayā girā aśaknuvan dhārayituṃ dhairyād vacanam abravīt
2.93.30	yaḥ saṃsadi prakṛtibhir bhaved yukta upāsitum vanyair mṛgair upāsīnaḥ so 'yam āste mamāgrajaḥ
2.93.31	vāsobhir bahusāhasrair yo mahātmā purocitaḥ mṛgājine so 'yam iha pravaste dharmam ācaran
2.93.32	adhārayad yo vividhāś citrāḥ sumanasas tadā so 'yaṃ jaṭābhāram imaṃ sahate rāghavaḥ katham
2.93.33	yasya yajñair yathādiṣṭair yukto dharmasya saṃcayaḥ śarīra kleśasaṃbhūtaṃ sa dharmaṃ parimārgate
2.93.34	candanena mahārheṇa yasyāṅgam upasevitam malena tasyāṅgam idaṃ katham āryasya sevyate
2.93.35	mannimittam idaṃ duḥkhaṃ prāpto rāmaḥ sukhocitaḥ dhig jīvitaṃ nṛśaṃsasya mama lokavigarhitam
2.93.36	ity evaṃ vilapan dīnaḥ prasvinnamukhapaṅkajaḥ pādāv aprāpya rāmasya papāta bharato rudan
2.93.37	duḥkhābhitapto bharato rājaputro mahābalaḥ uktvāryeti sakṛd dīnaṃ punar novāca kiṃ cana
2.93.38	bāṣpāpihita kaṇṭhaś ca prekṣya rāmaṃ yaśasvinam āryety evābhisaṃkruśya vyāhartuṃ nāśakat tataḥ
2.93.39	śatrughnaś cāpi rāmasya vavande caraṇau rudan tāv ubhau sa samāliṅgya rāmo 'py aśrūṇy avartayat
2.93.40	tataḥ sumantreṇa guhena caiva samīyatū rājasutāv araṇye divākaraś caiva niśākaraś ca yathāmbare śukrabṛhaspatibhyām
2.93.41	tān pārthivān vāraṇayūthapābhān samāgatāṃs tatra mahaty araṇye vanaukasas te 'pi samīkṣya sarve 'py aśrūṇy amuñcan pravihāya harṣam
2.94.1	āghrāya rāmas taṃ mūrdhni pariṣvajya ca rāghavaḥ aṅke bharatam āropya paryapṛcchat samāhitaḥ
2.94.2	kva nu te 'bhūt pitā tāta yad araṇyaṃ tvam āgataḥ na hi tvaṃ jīvatas tasya vanam āgantum arhasi
2.94.3	cirasya bata paśyāmi dūrād bharatam āgatam duṣpratīkam araṇye 'smin kiṃ tāta vanam āgataḥ
2.94.4	kaccid daśaratho rājā kuśalī satyasaṃgaraḥ rājasūyāśvamedhānām āhartā dharmaniścayaḥ
2.94.5	sa kaccid brāhmaṇo vidvān dharmanityo mahādyutiḥ ikṣvākūṇām upādhyāyo yathāvat tāta pūjyate
2.94.6	tāta kaccic ca kausalyā sumitrā ca prajāvatī sukhinī kaccid āryā ca devī nandati kaikayī
2.94.7	kaccid vinaya saṃpannaḥ kulaputro bahuśrutaḥ anasūyur anudraṣṭā satkṛtas te purohitaḥ
2.94.8	kaccid agniṣu te yukto vidhijño matimān ṛjuḥ hutaṃ ca hoṣyamāṇaṃ ca kāle vedayate sadā
2.94.9	iṣvastravarasaṃpannam arthaśāstraviśāradam sudhanvānam upādhyāyaṃ kaccit tvaṃ tāta manyase
2.94.10	kaccid ātma samāḥ śūrāḥ śrutavanto jitendriyāḥ kulīnāś ceṅgitajñāś ca kṛtās te tāta mantriṇaḥ
2.94.11	mantro vijayamūlaṃ hi rājñāṃ bhavati rāghava susaṃvṛto mantradharair amātyaiḥ śāstrakovidaiḥ
2.94.12	kaccin nidrāvaśaṃ naiṣi kaccit kāle vibudhyase kac ciṃś cāpararātriṣu cintayasy arthanaipuṇam
2.94.13	kaccin mantrayase naikaḥ kaccin na bahubhiḥ saha kaccit te mantrito mantro rāṣṭraṃ na paridhāvati
2.94.14	kaccid arthaṃ viniścitya laghumūlaṃ mahodayam kṣipram ārabhase kartuṃ na dīrghayasi rāghava
2.94.15	kaccit tu sukṛtāny eva kṛtarūpāṇi vā punaḥ vidus te sarvakāryāṇi na kartavyāni pārthivāḥ
2.94.16	kaccin na tarkair yuktvā vā ye cāpy aparikīrtitāḥ tvayā vā tava vāmātyair budhyate tāta mantritam
2.94.17	kaccit sahasrān mūrkhāṇām ekam icchasi paṇḍitam paṇḍito hy arthakṛcchreṣu kuryān niḥśreyasaṃ mahat
2.94.18	sahasrāṇy api mūrkhāṇāṃ yady upāste mahīpatiḥ atha vāpy ayutāny eva nāsti teṣu sahāyatā
2.94.19	eko 'py amātyo medhāvī śūro dakṣo vicakṣaṇaḥ rājānaṃ rājamātraṃ vā prāpayen mahatīṃ śriyam
2.94.20	kaccin mukhyā mahatsv eva madhyameṣu ca madhyamāḥ jaghanyāś ca jaghanyeṣu bhṛtyāḥ karmasu yojitāḥ
2.94.21	amātyān upadhātītān pitṛpaitāmahāñ śucīn śreṣṭhāñ śreṣṭheṣu kaccit tvaṃ niyojayasi karmasu
2.94.22	kaccit tvāṃ nāvajānanti yājakāḥ patitaṃ yathā ugrapratigrahītāraṃ kāmayānam iva striyaḥ
2.94.23	upāyakuśalaṃ vaidyaṃ bhṛtyasaṃdūṣaṇe ratam śūram aiśvaryakāmaṃ ca yo na hanti sa vadhyate
2.94.24	kaccid dhṛṣṭaś ca śūraś ca dhṛtimān matimāñ śuciḥ kulīnaś cānuraktaś ca dakṣaḥ senāpatiḥ kṛtaḥ
2.94.25	balavantaś ca kaccit te mukhyā yuddhaviśāradāḥ dṛṣṭāpadānā vikrāntās tvayā satkṛtya mānitāḥ
2.94.26	ka cid balasya bhaktaṃ ca vetanaṃ ca yathocitam saṃprāptakālaṃ dātavyaṃ dadāsi na vilambase
2.94.27	kālātikramaṇe hy eva bhakta vetanayor bhṛtāḥ bhartuḥ kupyanti duṣyanti so 'narthaḥ sumahān smṛtaḥ
2.94.28	kaccit sarve 'nuraktās tvāṃ kulaputrāḥ pradhānataḥ kaccit prāṇāṃs tavārtheṣu saṃtyajanti samāhitāḥ
2.94.29	kaccij jānapado vidvān dakṣiṇaḥ pratibhānavān yathoktavādī dūtas te kṛto bharata paṇḍitaḥ
2.94.30	kaccid aṣṭādaśāny eṣu svapakṣe daśa pañca ca tribhis tribhir avijñātair vetsi tīrthāni cārakaiḥ
2.94.31	kaccid vyapāstān ahitān pratiyātāṃś ca sarvadā durbalān anavajñāya vartase ripusūdana
2.94.32	kaccin na lokāyatikān brāhmaṇāṃs tāta sevase anartha kuśalā hy ete bālāḥ paṇḍitamāninaḥ
2.94.33	dharmaśāstreṣu mukhyeṣu vidyamāneṣu durbudhāḥ buddhimān vīkṣikīṃ prāpya nirarthaṃ pravadanti te
2.94.34	vīrair adhyuṣitāṃ pūrvam asmākaṃ tāta pūrvakaiḥ satyanāmāṃ dṛḍhadvārāṃ hastyaśvarathasaṃkulām
2.94.35	brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyair vaiśyaiḥ svakarmanirataiḥ sadā jitendriyair mahotsāhair vṛtāmātyaiḥ sahasraśaḥ
2.94.36	prāsādair vividhākārair vṛtāṃ vaidyajanākulām kaccit samuditāṃ sphītām ayodhyāṃ parirakṣasi
2.94.37	kaccic caityaśatair juṣṭaḥ suniviṣṭajanākulaḥ devasthānaiḥ prapābhiś ca taḍāgaiś copaśobhitaḥ
2.94.38	prahṛṣṭanaranārīkaḥ samājotsavaśobhitaḥ sukṛṣṭasīmā paśumān hiṃsābhir abhivarjitaḥ
2.94.39	adevamātṛko ramyaḥ śvāpadaiḥ parivarjitaḥ kaccij janapadaḥ sphītaḥ sukhaṃ vasati rāghava
2.94.40	kaccit te dayitāḥ sarve kṛṣigorakṣajīvinaḥ vārtāyāṃ saṃśritas tāta loko hi sukham edhate
2.94.41	teṣāṃ guptiparīhāraiḥ kaccit te bharaṇaṃ kṛtam rakṣyā hi rājñā dharmeṇa sarve viṣayavāsinaḥ
2.94.42	kaccit striyaḥ sāntvayasi kaccit tāś ca surakṣitāḥ kaccin na śraddadhāsyāsāṃ kaccid guhyaṃ na bhāṣase
2.94.43	kaccin nāgavanaṃ guptaṃ kuñjarāṇāṃ ca tṛpyasi kaccid darśayase nityaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ vibhūṣitam utthāyotthāya pūrvāhṇe rājaputro mahāpathe
2.94.44	kaccit sarvāṇi durgāṇi dhanadhānyāyudhodakaiḥ yantraiś ca paripūrṇāni tathā śilpidhanurdharaiḥ
2.94.45	āyas te vipulaḥ kaccit kaccid alpataro vyayaḥ apātreṣu na te kaccit kośo gacchati rāghava
2.94.46	devatārthe ca pitrarthe brāhmaṇābhyāgateṣu ca yodheṣu mitravargeṣu kaccid gacchati te vyayaḥ
2.94.47	kaccid āryo viśuddhātmā kṣāritaś corakarmaṇā apṛṣṭaḥ śāstrakuśalair na lobhād badhyate śuciḥ
2.94.48	gṛhītaś caiva pṛṣṭaś ca kāle dṛṣṭaḥ sakāraṇaḥ kaccin na mucyate coro dhanalobhān nararṣabha
2.94.49	vyasane kaccid āḍhyasya dugatasya ca rāghava arthaṃ virāgāḥ paśyanti tavāmātyā bahuśrutāḥ
2.94.50	yāni mithyābhiśastānāṃ patanty asrāṇi rāghava tāni putrapaśūn ghnanti prītyartham anuśāsataḥ
2.94.51	kaccid vṛdhāṃś ca bālāṃś ca vaidyamukhyāṃś ca rāghava dānena manasā vācā tribhir etair bubhūṣase
2.94.52	kaccid gurūṃś ca vṛddhāṃś ca tāpasān devatātithīn caityāṃś ca sarvān siddhārthān brāhmaṇāṃś ca namasyasi
2.94.53	kaccid arthena vā dharmaṃ dharmaṃ dharmeṇa vā punaḥ ubhau vā prītilobhena kāmena na vibādhase
2.94.54	kaccid arthaṃ ca dharmaṃ ca kāmaṃ ca jayatāṃ vara vibhajya kāle kālajña sarvān bharata sevase
2.94.55	kaccit te brāhmaṇāḥ śarma sarvaśāstrārthakovidaḥ āśaṃsante mahāprājña paurajānapadaiḥ saha
2.94.56	nāstikyam anṛtaṃ krodhaṃ pramādaṃ dīrghasūtratām adarśanaṃ jñānavatām ālasyaṃ pañcavṛttitām
2.94.57	ekacintanam arthānām anarthajñaiś ca mantraṇam niścitānām anārambhaṃ mantrasyāparilakṣaṇam
2.94.58	maṅgalasyāprayogaṃ ca pratyutthānaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ kaccit tvaṃ varjayasy etān rājadoṣāṃś caturdaśa
2.94.59	kaccit svādukṛtaṃ bhojyam eko nāśnāsi rāghava kaccid āśaṃsamānebhyo mitrebhyaḥ saṃprayacchasi
2.95.1	rāmasya vacanaṃ śrutvā bharataḥ pratyuvāca ha kiṃ me dharmād vihīnasya rājadharmaḥ kariṣyati
2.95.2	śāśvato 'yaṃ sadā dharmaḥ sthito 'smāsu nararṣabha jyeṣṭha putre sthite rājan na kanīyān bhaven nṛpaḥ
2.95.3	sa samṛddhāṃ mayā sārdham ayodhyāṃ gaccha rāghava abhiṣecaya cātmānaṃ kulasyāsya bhavāya naḥ
2.95.4	rājānaṃ mānuṣaṃ prāhur devatve saṃmato mama yasya dharmārthasahitaṃ vṛttam āhur amānuṣam
2.95.5	kekayasthe ca mayi tu tvayi cāraṇyam āśrite divam ārya gato rājā yāyajūkaḥ satāṃ mataḥ
2.95.6	uttiṣṭha puruṣavyāghra kriyatām udakaṃ pituḥ ahaṃ cāyaṃ ca śatrughnaḥ pūrvam eva kṛtodakau
2.95.7	priyeṇa kila dattaṃ hi pitṛlokeṣu rāghava akṣayyaṃ bhavatīty āhur bhavāṃś caiva pituḥ priyaḥ
2.95.8	tāṃ śrutvā karuṇāṃ vācaṃ pitur maraṇasaṃhitām rāghavo bharatenoktāṃ babhūva gatacetanaḥ
2.95.9	vāgvajraṃ bharatenoktam amanojñaṃ paraṃtapaḥ pragṛhya bāhū rāmo vai puṣpitāgro yathā drumaḥ vane paraśunā kṛttas tathā bhuvi papāta ha
2.95.10	tathā hi patitaṃ rāmaṃ jagatyāṃ jagatīpatim kūlaghātapariśrāntaṃ prasuptam iva kuñjaram
2.95.11	bhrātaras te maheṣvāsaṃ sarvataḥ śokakarśitam rudantaḥ saha vaidehyā siṣicuḥ salilena vai
2.95.12	sa tu saṃjñāṃ punar labdhvā netrābhyām āsram utsṛjan upākrāmata kākutsthaḥ kṛpaṇaṃ bahubhāṣitum
2.95.13	kiṃ nu tasya mayā kāryaṃ durjātena mahātmanā yo mṛto mama śokena na mayā cāpi saṃskṛtaḥ
2.95.14	aho bharata siddhārtho yena rājā tvayānagha śatrugheṇa ca sarveṣu pretakṛtyeṣu satkṛtaḥ
2.95.15	niṣpradhānām anekāgraṃ narendreṇa vinākṛtām nivṛttavanavāso 'pi nāyodhyāṃ gantum utsahe
2.95.16	samāptavanavāsaṃ mām ayodhyāyāṃ paraṃtapa ko nu śāsiṣyati punas tāte lokāntaraṃ gate
2.95.17	purā prekṣya suvṛttaṃ māṃ pitā yāny āha sāntvayan vākyāni tāni śroṣyāmi kutaḥ karṇasukhāny aham
2.95.18	evam uktvā sa bharataṃ bhāryām abhyetya rāghavaḥ uvāca śokasaṃtaptaḥ pūrṇacandranibhānanām
2.95.19	sīte mṛtas te śvaśuraḥ pitrā hīno 'si lakṣmaṇa bharato duḥkham ācaṣṭe svargataṃ pṛthivīpatim
2.95.20	sāntvayitvā tu tāṃ rāmo rudantīṃ janakātmajām uvāca lakṣmaṇaṃ tatra duḥkhito duḥkhitaṃ vacaḥ
2.95.21	ānayeṅgudipiṇyākaṃ cīram āhara cottaram jalakriyārthaṃ tātasya gamiṣyāmi mahātmanaḥ
2.95.22	sītā purastād vrajatu tvam enām abhito vraja ahaṃ paścād gamiṣyāmi gatir hy eṣā sudāruṇā
2.95.23	tato nityānugas teṣāṃ viditātmā mahāmatiḥ mṛdur dāntaś ca śāntaś ca rāme ca dṛḍha bhaktimān
2.95.24	sumantras tair nṛpasutaiḥ sārdham āśvāsya rāghavam avātārayad ālambya nadīṃ mandākinīṃ śivām
2.95.25	te sutīrthāṃ tataḥ kṛcchrād upāgamya yaśasvinaḥ nadīṃ mandākinīṃ ramyāṃ sadā puṣpitakānanām
2.95.26	śīghrasrotasam āsādya tīrthaṃ śivam akardamam siṣicus tūdakaṃ rājñe tata etad bhavatv iti
2.95.27	pragṛhya ca mahīpālo jalapūritam añjalim diśaṃ yāmyām abhimukho rudan vacanam abravīt
2.95.28	etat te rājaśārdūla vimalaṃ toyam akṣayam pitṛlokagatasyādya maddattam upatiṣṭhatu
2.95.29	tato mandākinī tīrāt pratyuttīrya sa rāghavaḥ pituś cakāra tejasvī nivāpaṃ bhrātṛbhiḥ saha
2.95.30	aiṅgudaṃ badarīmiśraṃ piṇyākaṃ darbhasaṃstare nyasya rāmaḥ suduḥkhārto rudan vacanam abravīt
2.95.31	idaṃ bhuṅkṣva mahārājaprīto yad aśanā vayam yadannaḥ puruṣo bhavati tadannās tasya devatāḥ
2.95.32	tatas tenaiva mārgeṇa pratyuttīrya nadītaṭāt āruroha naravyāghro ramyasānuṃ mahīdharam
2.95.33	tataḥ parṇakuṭīdvāram āsādya jagatīpatiḥ parijagrāha pāṇibhyām ubhau bharatalakṣmaṇau
2.95.34	teṣāṃ tu rudatāṃ śabdāt pratiśrutkābhavad girau bhrātṝṇāṃ saha vaidehyā siṃhānāṃ nardatām iva
2.95.35	vijñāya tumulaṃ śabdaṃ trastā bharatasainikāḥ abruvaṃś cāpi rāmeṇa bharataḥ saṃgato dhruvam teṣām eva mahāñ śabdaḥ śocatāṃ pitaraṃ mṛtam
2.95.36	atha vāsān parityajya taṃ sarve 'bhimukhāḥ svanam apy eka manaso jagmur yathāsthānaṃ pradhāvitāḥ
2.95.37	hayair anye gajair anye rathair anye svalaṃkṛtaiḥ sukumārās tathaivānye padbhir eva narā yayuḥ
2.95.38	aciraproṣitaṃ rāmaṃ ciraviproṣitaṃ yathā draṣṭukāmo janaḥ sarvo jagāma sahasāśramam
2.95.39	bhrātṝṇāṃ tvaritās te tu draṣṭukāmāḥ samāgamam yayur bahuvidhair yānaiḥ khuranemisamākulaiḥ
2.95.40	sā bhūmir bahubhir yānaiḥ khuranemisamāhatā mumoca tumulaṃ śabdaṃ dyaur ivābhrasamāgame
2.95.41	tena vitrāsitā nāgāḥ kareṇuparivāritāḥ āvāsayanto gandhena jagmur anyad vanaṃ tataḥ
2.95.42	varāhamṛgasiṃhāś ca mahiṣāḥ sarkṣavānarāḥ vyāghra gokarṇagavayā vitreṣuḥ pṛṣataiḥ saha
2.95.43	rathāṅgasāhvā natyūhā haṃsāḥ kāraṇḍavāḥ plavāḥ tathā puṃskokilāḥ krauñcā visaṃjñā bhejire diśaḥ
2.95.44	tena śabdena vitrastair ākāśaṃ pakṣibhir vṛtam manuṣyair āvṛtā bhūmir ubhayaṃ prababhau tadā
2.95.45	tān narān bāṣpapūrṇākṣān samīkṣyātha suduḥkhitān paryaṣvajata dharmajñaḥ pitṛvan mātṛvac ca saḥ
2.95.46	sa tatra kāṃś cit pariṣasvaje narān narāś ca ke cit tu tam abhyavādayan cakāra sarvān savayasyabāndhavān yathārham āsādya tadā nṛpātmajaḥ
2.95.47	tataḥ sa teṣāṃ rudatāṃ mahātmanāṃ bhuvaṃ ca khaṃ cānuvinādayan svanaḥ guhā girīṇāṃ ca diśaś ca saṃtataṃ mṛdaṅgaghoṣapratimo viśuśruve
2.96.1	vasiṣṭhaḥ purataḥ kṛtvā dārān daśarathasya ca abhicakrāma taṃ deśaṃ rāmadarśanatarṣitaḥ
2.96.2	rājapatnyaś ca gacchantyo mandaṃ mandākinīṃ prati dadṛśus tatra tat tīrthaṃ rāmalakṣmaṇasevitam
2.96.3	kausalyā bāṣpapūrṇena mukhena pariśuṣyatā sumitrām abravīd dīnā yāś cānyā rājayoṣitaḥ
2.96.4	idaṃ teṣām anāthānāṃ kliṣṭam akliṣṭa karmaṇām vane prāk kevalaṃ tīrthaṃ ye te nirviṣayī kṛtāḥ
2.96.5	itaḥ sumitre putras te sadā jalam atandritaḥ svayaṃ harati saumitrir mama putrasya kāraṇāt
2.96.6	dakṣiṇāgreṣu darbheṣu sā dadarśa mahītale pitur iṅgudipiṇyākaṃ nyastam āyatalocanā
2.96.7	taṃ bhūmau pitur ārtena nyastaṃ rāmeṇa vīkṣya sā uvāca devī kausalyā sarvā daśarathastriyaḥ
2.96.8	idam ikṣvākunāthasya rāghavasya mahātmanaḥ rāghaveṇa pitur dattaṃ paśyataitad yathāvidhi
2.96.9	tasya devasamānasya pārthivasya mahātmanaḥ naitad aupayikaṃ manye bhuktabhogasya bhojanam
2.96.10	caturantāṃ mahīṃ bhuktvā mahendra sadṛśo bhuvi katham iṅgudipiṇyākaṃ sa bhuṅkte vasudhādhipaḥ
2.96.11	ato duḥkhataraṃ loke na kiṃ cit pratibhāti mā yatra rāmaḥ pitur dadyād iṅgudīkṣodam ṛddhimān
2.96.12	rāmeṇeṅgudipiṇyākaṃ pitur dattaṃ samīkṣya me kathaṃ duḥkhena hṛdayaṃ na sphoṭati sahasradhā
2.96.13	evam ārtāṃ sapatnyas tā jagmur āśvāsya tāṃ tadā dadṛśuś cāśrame rāmaṃ svargāc cyutam ivāmaram
2.96.14	sarvabhogaiḥ parityaktaṃ rāma saṃprekṣya mātaraḥ ārtā mumucur aśrūṇi sasvaraṃ śokakarśitāḥ
2.96.15	tāsāṃ rāmaḥ samutthāya jagrāha caraṇāñ śubhān mātṝṇāṃ manujavyāghraḥ sarvāsāṃ satyasaṃgaraḥ
2.96.16	tāḥ pāṇibhiḥ sukhasparśair mṛdvaṅgulitalaiḥ śubhaiḥ pramamārjū rajaḥ pṛṣṭhād rāmasyāyatalocanāḥ
2.96.17	saumitrir api tāḥ sarvā mātṝḥ saṃprekṣya duḥkhitaḥ abhyavādayatāsaktaṃ śanai rāmād anantaram
2.96.18	yathā rāme tathā tasmin sarvā vavṛtire striyaḥ vṛttiṃ daśarathāj jāte lakṣmaṇe śubhalakṣaṇe
2.96.19	sītāpi caraṇāṃs tāsām upasaṃgṛhya duḥkhitā śvaśrūṇām aśrupūrṇākṣī sā babhūvāgrataḥ sthitā
2.96.20	tāṃ pariṣvajya duḥkhārtāṃ mātā duhitaraṃ yathā vanavāsakṛśāṃ dīnāṃ kausalyā vākyam abravīt
2.96.21	videharājasya sutā snuṣā daśarathasya ca rāmapatnī kathaṃ duḥkhaṃ saṃprāptā nirjane vane
2.96.22	padmam ātapasaṃtaptaṃ parikliṣṭam ivotpalam kāñcanaṃ rajasā dhvastaṃ kliṣṭaṃ candram ivāmbudaiḥ
2.96.23	mukhaṃ te prekṣya māṃ śoko dahaty agnir ivāśrayam bhṛśaṃ manasi vaidehi vyasanāraṇisaṃbhavaḥ
2.96.24	bruvantyām evam ārtāyāṃ jananyāṃ bharatāgrajaḥ pādāv āsādya jagrāha vasiṣṭhasya sa rāghavaḥ
2.96.25	purohitasyāgnisamasya tasya vai bṛhaspater indra ivāmarādhipaḥ pragṛhya pādau susamṛddhatejasaḥ sahaiva tenopaviveśa rāghavaḥ
2.96.26	tato jaghanyaṃ sahitaiḥ sa mantribhiḥ purapradhānaiś ca sahaiva sainikaiḥ janena dharmajñatamena dharmavān upopaviṣṭo bharatas tadāgrajam
2.96.27	upopaviṣṭas tu tadā sa vīryavāṃs tapasviveṣeṇa samīkṣya rāghavam śriyā jvalantaṃ bharataḥ kṛtāñjalir yathā mahendraḥ prayataḥ prajāpatim
2.96.28	kim eṣa vākyaṃ bharato 'dya rāghavaṃ praṇamya satkṛtya ca sādhu vakṣyati itīva tasyāryajanasya tattvato babhūva kautūhalam uttamaṃ tadā
2.96.29	sa rāghavaḥ satyadhṛtiś ca lakṣmaṇo mahānubhāvo bharataś ca dhārmikaḥ vṛtāḥ suhṛdbhiś ca virejur adhvare yathā sadasyaiḥ sahitās trayo 'gnayaḥ
2.97.1	taṃ tu rāmaḥ samāśvāsya bhrātaraṃ guruvatsalam lakṣmaṇena saha bhrātrā praṣṭuṃ samupacakrame
2.97.2	kim etad iccheyam ahaṃ śrotuṃ pravyāhṛtaṃ tvayā yasmāt tvam āgato deśam imaṃ cīrajaṭājinī
2.97.3	yannimittam imaṃ deśaṃ kṛṣṇājinajaṭādharaḥ hitvā rājyaṃ praviṣṭas tvaṃ tat sarvaṃ vaktum arhasi
2.97.4	ity uktaḥ kekayīputraḥ kākutsthena mahātmanā pragṛhya balavad bhūyaḥ prāñjalir vākyam abravīt
2.97.5	āryaṃ tātaḥ parityajya kṛtvā karma suduṣkaram gataḥ svargaṃ mahābāhuḥ putraśokābhipīḍitaḥ
2.97.6	striyā niyuktaḥ kaikeyyā mama mātrā paraṃtapa cakāra sumahat pāpam idam ātmayaśoharam
2.97.7	sā rājyaphalam aprāpya vidhavā śokakarśitā patiṣyati mahāghore niraye jananī mama
2.97.8	tasya me dāsabhūtasya prasādaṃ kartum arhasi abhiṣiñcasva cādyaiva rājyena maghavān iva
2.97.9	imāḥ prakṛtayaḥ sarvā vidhavā māturaś ca yāḥ tvat sakāśam anuprāptāḥ prasādaṃ kartum arhasi
2.97.10	tadānupūrvyā yuktaṃ ca yuktaṃ cātmani mānada rājyaṃ prāpnuhi dharmeṇa sakāmān suhṛdaḥ kuru
2.97.11	bhavatv avidhavā bhūmiḥ samagrā patinā tvayā śaśinā vimaleneva śāradī rajanī yathā
2.97.12	ebhiś ca sacivaiḥ sārdhaṃ śirasā yācito mayā bhrātuḥ śiṣyasya dāsasya prasādaṃ kartum arhasi
2.97.13	tad idaṃ śāśvataṃ pitryaṃ sarvaṃ sacivamaṇḍalam pūjitaṃ puruṣavyāghra nātikramitum utsahe
2.97.14	evam uktvā mahābāhuḥ sabāṣpaḥ kekayīsutaḥ rāmasya śirasā pādau jagrāha bharataḥ punaḥ
2.97.15	taṃ mattam iva mātaṅgaṃ niḥśvasantaṃ punaḥ punaḥ bhrātaraṃ bharataṃ rāmaḥ pariṣvajyedam abravīt
2.97.16	kulīnaḥ sattvasaṃpannas tejasvī caritavrataḥ rājyahetoḥ kathaṃ pāpam ācaret tvadvidho janaḥ
2.97.17	na doṣaṃ tvayi paśyāmi sūkṣmam apy ari sūdana na cāpi jananīṃ bālyāt tvaṃ vigarhitum arhasi
2.97.18	yāvat pitari dharmajña gauravaṃ lokasatkṛte tāvad dharmabhṛtāṃ śreṣṭha jananyām api gauravam
2.97.19	etābhyāṃ dharmaśīlābhyāṃ vanaṃ gaccheti rāghava mātā pitṛbhyām ukto 'haṃ katham anyat samācare
2.97.20	tvayā rājyam ayodhyāyāṃ prāptavyaṃ lokasatkṛtam vastavyaṃ daṇḍakāraṇye mayā valkalavāsasā
2.97.21	evaṃ kṛtvā mahārājo vibhāgaṃ lokasaṃnidhau vyādiśya ca mahātejā divaṃ daśaratho gataḥ
2.97.22	sa ca pramāṇaṃ dharmātmā rājā lokagurus tava pitrā dattaṃ yathābhāgam upabhoktuṃ tvam arhasi
2.97.23	caturdaśa samāḥ saumya daṇḍakāraṇyam āśritaḥ upabhokṣye tv ahaṃ dattaṃ bhāgaṃ pitrā mahātmanā
2.97.24	yad abravīn māṃ naralokasatkṛtaḥ pitā mahātmā vibudhādhipopamaḥ tad eva manye paramātmano hitaṃ na sarvalokeśvarabhāvam avyayam
2.98.1	tataḥ puruṣasiṃhānāṃ vṛtānāṃ taiḥ suhṛdgaṇaiḥ śocatām eva rajanī duḥkhena vyatyavartata
2.98.2	rajanyāṃ suprabhātāyāṃ bhrātaras te suhṛdvṛtāḥ mandākinyāṃ hutaṃ japyaṃ kṛtvā rāmam upāgaman
2.98.3	tūṣṇīṃ te samupāsīnā na kaś cit kiṃ cid abravīt bharatas tu suhṛnmadhye rāmavacanam abravīt
2.98.4	sāntvitā māmikā mātā dattaṃ rājyam idaṃ mama tad dadāmi tavaivāhaṃ bhuṅkṣva rājyam akaṇṭakam
2.98.5	mahatevāmbuvegena bhinnaḥ setur jalāgame durāvāraṃ tvadanyena rājyakhaṇḍam idaṃ mahat
2.98.6	gatiṃ khara ivāśvasya tārkṣyasyeva patatriṇaḥ anugantuṃ na śaktir me gatiṃ tava mahīpate
2.98.7	sujīvaṃ nityaśas tasya yaḥ parair upajīvyate rāma tena tu durjīvaṃ yaḥ parān upajīvati
2.98.8	yathā tu ropito vṛkṣaḥ puruṣeṇa vivardhitaḥ hrasvakena durāroho rūḍhaskandho mahādrumaḥ
2.98.9	sa yadā puṣpito bhūtvā phalāni na vidarśayet sa tāṃ nānubhavet prītiṃ yasya hetoḥ prabhāvitaḥ
2.98.10	eṣopamā mahābāho tvam arthaṃ vettum arhasi yadi tvam asmān ṛṣabho bhartā bhṛtyān na śādhi hi
2.98.11	śreṇayas tvāṃ mahārāja paśyantv agryāś ca sarvaśaḥ pratapantam ivādityaṃ rājye sthitam ariṃdamam
2.98.12	tavānuyāne kākutṣṭha mattā nardantu kuñjarāḥ antaḥpura gatā nāryo nandantu susamāhitāḥ
2.98.13	tasya sādhv ity amanyanta nāgarā vividhā janāḥ bharatasya vacaḥ śrutvā rāmaṃ pratyanuyācataḥ
2.98.14	tam evaṃ duḥkhitaṃ prekṣya vilapantaṃ yaśasvinam rāmaḥ kṛtātmā bharataṃ samāśvāsayad ātmavān
2.98.15	nātmanaḥ kāmakāro 'sti puruṣo 'yam anīśvaraḥ itaś cetarataś cainaṃ kṛtāntaḥ parikarṣati
2.98.16	sarve kṣayāntā nicayāḥ patanāntāḥ samucchrayāḥ saṃyogā viprayogāntā maraṇāntaṃ ca jīvitam
2.98.17	yathā phalānaṃ pakvānāṃ nānyatra patanād bhayam evaṃ narasya jātasya nānyatra maraṇād bhayam
2.98.18	yathāgāraṃ dṛḍhasthūṇaṃ jīrṇaṃ bhūtvāvasīdati tathāvasīdanti narā jarāmṛtyuvaśaṃ gatāḥ
2.98.19	ahorātrāṇi gacchanti sarveṣāṃ prāṇinām iha āyūṃṣi kṣapayanty āśu grīṣme jalam ivāṃśavaḥ
2.98.20	ātmānam anuśoca tvaṃ kim anyam anuśocasi āyus te hīyate yasya sthitasya ca gatasya ca
2.98.21	sahaiva mṛtyur vrajati saha mṛtyur niṣīdati gatvā sudīrgham adhvānaṃ saha mṛtyur nivartate
2.98.22	gātreṣu valayaḥ prāptāḥ śvetāś caiva śiroruhāḥ jarayā puruṣo jīrṇaḥ kiṃ hi kṛtvā prabhāvayet
2.98.23	nandanty udita āditye nandanty astam ite ravau ātmano nāvabudhyante manuṣyā jīvitakṣayam
2.98.24	hṛṣyanty ṛtumukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā navaṃ navam ihāgatam ṛtūnāṃ parivartena prāṇināṃ prāṇasaṃkṣayaḥ
2.98.25	yathā kāṣṭhaṃ ca kāṣṭhaṃ ca sameyātāṃ mahārṇave sametya ca vyapeyātāṃ kālam āsādya kaṃ cana
2.98.26	evaṃ bhāryāś ca putrāś ca jñātayaś ca vasūni ca sametya vyavadhāvanti dhruvo hy eṣāṃ vinābhavaḥ
2.98.27	nātra kaś cid yathā bhāvaṃ prāṇī samabhivartate tena tasmin na sāmarthyaṃ pretasyāsty anuśocataḥ
2.98.28	yathā hi sārthaṃ gacchantaṃ brūyāt kaś cit pathi sthitaḥ aham apy āgamiṣyāmi pṛṣṭhato bhavatām iti
2.98.29	evaṃ pūrvair gato mārgaḥ pitṛpaitāmaho dhruvaḥ tam āpannaḥ kathaṃ śoced yasya nāsti vyatikramaḥ
2.98.30	vayasaḥ patamānasya srotaso vānivartinaḥ ātmā sukhe niyoktavyaḥ sukhabhājaḥ prajāḥ smṛtāḥ
2.98.31	dharmātmā sa śubhaiḥ kṛtsnaiḥ kratubhiś cāptadakṣiṇaiḥ dhūtapāpo gataḥ svargaṃ pitā naḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ
2.98.32	bhṛtyānāṃ bharaṇāt samyak prajānāṃ paripālanāt arthādānāc ca dhārmeṇa pitā nas tridivaṃ gataḥ
2.98.33	iṣṭvā bahuvidhair yajñair bhogāṃś cāvāpya puṣkalān uttamaṃ cāyur āsādya svar gataḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ
2.98.34	sa jīrṇaṃ mānuṣaṃ dehaṃ parityajya pitā hi naḥ daivīm ṛddhim anuprāpto brahmalokavihāriṇīm
2.98.35	taṃ tu naivaṃ vidhaḥ kaś cit prājñaḥ śocitum arhati tvadvidho yadvidhaś cāpi śrutavān buddhimattaraḥ
2.98.36	ete bahuvidhāḥ śokā vilāpa rudite tathā varjanīyā hi dhīreṇa sarvāvasthāsu dhīmatā
2.98.37	sa svastho bhava mā śoco yātvā cāvasa tāṃ purīm tathā pitrā niyukto 'si vaśinā vadatāmv vara
2.98.38	yatrāham api tenaiva niyuktaḥ puṇyakarmaṇā tatraivāhaṃ kariṣyāmi pitur āryasya śāsanam
2.98.39	na mayā śāsanaṃ tasya tyaktuṃ nyāyyam ariṃdama tat tvayāpi sadā mānyaṃ sa vai bandhuḥ sa naḥ pitā
2.98.40	evam uktvā tu virate rāme vacanam arthavat uvāca bharataś citraṃ dhārmiko dhārmikaṃ vacaḥ
2.98.41	ko hi syād īdṛśo loke yādṛśas tvam ariṃdama na tvāṃ pravyathayed duḥkhaṃ prītir vā na praharṣayet
2.98.42	saṃmataś cāsi vṛddhānāṃ tāṃś ca pṛcchasi saṃśayān yathā mṛtas tathā jīvan yathāsati tathā sati
2.98.43	yasyaiṣa buddhilābhaḥ syāt paritapyeta kena saḥ sa evaṃ vyasanaṃ prāpya na viṣīditum arhati
2.98.44	amaropamasattvas tvaṃ mahātmā satyasaṃgaraḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvadarśī ca buddhimāṃś cāsi rāghava
2.98.45	na tvām evaṃ guṇair yuktaṃ prabhavābhavakovidam aviṣahyatamaṃ duḥkham āsādayitum arhati
2.98.46	proṣite mayi yat pāpaṃ mātrā matkāraṇāt kṛtam kṣudrayā tad aniṣṭaṃ me prasīdatu bhavān mama
2.98.47	dharmabandhena baddho 'smi tenemāṃ neha mātaram hanmi tīvreṇa daṇḍena daṇḍārhāṃ pāpakāriṇīm
2.98.48	kathaṃ daśarathāj jātaḥ śuddhābhijanakarmaṇaḥ jānan dharmam adharmiṣṭhaṃ kuryāṃ karma jugupsitam
2.98.49	guruḥ kriyāvān vṛddhaś ca rājā pretaḥ piteti ca tātaṃ na parigarheyaṃ daivataṃ ceti saṃsadi
2.98.50	ko hi dharmārthayor hīnam īdṛśaṃ karma kilbiṣam striyāḥ priyacikīrṣuḥ san kuryād dharmajña dharmavit
2.98.51	antakāle hi bhūtāni muhyantīti purāśrutiḥ rājñaivaṃ kurvatā loke pratyakṣā sā śrutiḥ kṛtā
2.98.52	sādhv artham abhisaṃdhāya krodhān mohāc ca sāhasāt tātasya yad atikrāntaṃ pratyāharatu tad bhavān
2.98.53	pitur hi samatikrāntaṃ putro yaḥ sādhu manyate tad apatyaṃ mataṃ loke viparītam ato 'nyathā
2.98.54	tad apatyaṃ bhavān astu mā bhavān duṣkṛtaṃ pituḥ abhipat tat kṛtaṃ karma loke dhīravigarhitam
2.98.55	kaikeyīṃ māṃ ca tātaṃ ca suhṛdo bāndhavāṃś ca naḥ paurajānapadān sarvāṃs trātu sarvam idaṃ bhavān
2.98.56	kva cāraṇyaṃ kva ca kṣātraṃ kva jaṭāḥ kva ca pālanam īdṛśaṃ vyāhataṃ karma na bhavān kartum arhati
2.98.57	atha kleśajam eva tvaṃ dharmaṃ caritum icchasi dharmeṇa caturo varṇān pālayan kleśam āpnuhi
2.98.58	caturṇām āśramāṇāṃ hi gārhasthyaṃ śreṣṭham āśramam āhur dharmajña dharmajñās taṃ kathaṃ tyaktum arhasi
2.98.59	śrutena bālaḥ sthānena janmanā bhavato hy aham sa kathaṃ pālayiṣyāmi bhūmiṃ bhavati tiṣṭhati
2.98.60	hīnabuddhiguṇo bālo hīnaḥ sthānena cāpy aham bhavatā ca vinā bhūto na vartayitum utsahe
2.98.61	idaṃ nikhilam avyagraṃ pitryaṃ rājyam akaṇṭakam anuśādhi svadharmeṇa dharmajña saha bāndhavaiḥ
2.98.62	ihaiva tvābhiṣiñcantu dharmajña saha bāndhavaiḥ ṛtvijaḥ savasiṣṭhāś ca mantravan mantrakovidāḥ
2.98.63	abhiṣiktas tvam asmābhir ayodhyāṃ pālane vraja vijitya tarasā lokān marudbhir iva vāsavaḥ
2.98.64	ṛṇāni trīṇy apākurvan durhṛdaḥ sādhu nirdahan suhṛdas tarpayan kāmais tvam evātrānuśādhi mām
2.98.65	adyārya muditāḥ santu suhṛdas te 'bhiṣecane adya bhītāḥ pālayantāṃ durhṛdas te diśo daśa
2.98.66	ākrośaṃ mama mātuś ca pramṛjya puruṣarṣabha adya tatra bhavantaṃ ca pitaraṃ rakṣa kilbiṣāt
2.98.67	śirasā tvābhiyāce 'haṃ kuruṣva karuṇāṃ mayi bāndhaveṣu ca sarveṣu bhūteṣv iva maheśvaraḥ
2.98.68	atha vā pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā vanam eva bhavān itaḥ gamiṣyati gamiṣyāmi bhavatā sārdham apy aham
2.98.69	tathāpi rāmo bharatena tāmyata prasādyamānaḥ śirasā mahīpatiḥ na caiva cakre gamanāya sattvavān matiṃ pitus tadvacane pratiṣṭhitaḥ
2.98.70	tad adbhutaṃ sthairyam avekṣya rāghave samaṃ jano harṣam avāpa duḥkhitaḥ na yāty ayodhyām iti duḥkhito 'bhavat sthirapratijñatvam avekṣya harṣitaḥ
2.98.71	tam ṛtvijo naigamayūthavallabhās tathā visaṃjñāśrukalāś ca mātaraḥ tathā bruvāṇaṃ bharataṃ pratuṣṭuvuḥ praṇamya rāmaṃ ca yayācire saha
2.99.1	punar evaṃ bruvāṇaṃ tu bharataṃ lakṣmaṇāgrajaḥ pratyuvaca tataḥ śrīmāñ jñātimadhye 'tisatkṛtaḥ
2.99.2	upapannam idaṃ vākyaṃ yat tvam evam abhāṣathāḥ jātaḥ putro daśarathāt kaikeyyāṃ rājasattamāt
2.99.3	purā bhrātaḥ pitā naḥ sa mātaraṃ te samudvahan mātāmahe samāśrauṣīd rājyaśulkam anuttamam
2.99.4	devāsure ca saṃgrāme jananyai tava pārthivaḥ saṃprahṛṣṭo dadau rājā varam ārādhitaḥ prabhuḥ
2.99.5	tataḥ sā saṃpratiśrāvya tava mātā yaśasvinī ayācata naraśreṣṭhaṃ dvau varau varavarṇinī
2.99.6	tava rājyaṃ naravyāghra mama pravrājanaṃ tathā tac ca rājā tathā tasyai niyuktaḥ pradadau varam
2.99.7	tena pitrāham apy atra niyuktaḥ puruṣarṣabha caturdaśa vane vāsaṃ varṣāṇi varadānikam
2.99.8	so 'haṃ vanam idaṃ prāpto nirjanaṃ lakṣmaṇānvitaḥ śītayā cāpratidvandvaḥ satyavāde sthitaḥ pituḥ
2.99.9	bhavān api tathety eva pitaraṃ satyavādinam kartum arhati rājendraṃ kṣipram evābhiṣecanāt
2.99.10	ṛṇān mocaya rājānaṃ matkṛte bharata prabhum pitaraṃ trāhi dharmajña mātaraṃ cābhinandaya
2.99.11	śrūyate hi purā tāta śrutir gītā yaśasvinī gayena yajamānena gayeṣv eva pitṝn prati
2.99.12	puṃ nāmnā narakād yasmāt pitaraṃ trāyate sutaḥ tasmāt putra iti proktaḥ pitṝn yat pāti vā sutaḥ
2.99.13	eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā guṇavanto bahuśrutāḥ teṣāṃ vai samavetānām api kaś cid gayāṃ vrajet
2.99.14	evaṃ rājarṣayaḥ sarve pratītā rājanandana tasmāt trāhi naraśreṣṭha pitaraṃ narakāt prabho
2.99.15	ayodhyāṃ gaccha bharata prakṛtīr anurañjaya śatrughna sahito vīra saha sarvair dvijātibhiḥ
2.99.16	pravekṣye daṇḍakāraṇyam aham apy avilambayan ābhyāṃ tu sahito rājan vaidehyā lakṣmaṇena ca
2.99.17	tvaṃ rājā bhava bharata svayaṃ narāṇāṃ vanyānām aham api rājarāṇ mṛgāṇām gaccha tvaṃ puravaram adya saṃprahṛṣṭaḥ saṃhṛṣṭas tv aham api daṇḍakān pravekṣye
2.99.18	chāyāṃ te dinakarabhāḥ prabādhamānaṃ varṣatraṃ bharata karotu mūrdhni śītām eteṣām aham api kānanadrumāṇāṃ chāyāṃ tām atiśayinīṃ sukhaṃ śrayiṣye
2.99.19	śatrughnaḥ kuśalamatis tu te sahāyaḥ saumitrir mama viditaḥ pradhānamitram catvāras tanayavarā vayaṃ narendraṃ satyasthaṃ bharata carāma mā viṣādam
2.100.1	āśvāsayantaṃ bharataṃ jābālir brāhmaṇottamaḥ uvāca rāmaṃ dharmajñaṃ dharmāpetam idaṃ vacaḥ
2.100.2	sādhu rāghava mā bhūt te buddhir evaṃ nirarthakā prākṛtasya narasyeva ārya buddhes tapasvinaḥ
2.100.3	kaḥ kasya puruṣo bandhuḥ kim āpyaṃ kasya kena cit yad eko jāyate jantur eka eva vinaśyati
2.100.4	tasmān mātā pitā ceti rāma sajjeta yo naraḥ unmatta iva sa jñeyo nāsti kā cid dhi kasya cit
2.100.5	yathā grāmāntaraṃ gacchan naraḥ kaś cit kva cid vaset utsṛjya ca tam āvāsaṃ pratiṣṭhetāpare 'hani
2.100.6	evam eva manuṣyāṇāṃ pitā mātā gṛhaṃ vasu āvāsamātraṃ kākutstha sajjante nātra sajjanāḥ
2.100.7	pitryaṃ rājyaṃ samutsṛjya sa nārhati narottama āsthātuṃ kāpathaṃ duḥkhaṃ viṣamaṃ bahukaṇṭakam
2.100.8	samṛddhāyām ayodhyāyām ātmānam abhiṣecaya ekaveṇīdharā hi tvāṃ nagarī saṃpratīkṣate
2.100.9	rājabhogān anubhavan mahārhān pārthivātmaja vihara tvam ayodhyāyāṃ yathā śakras triviṣṭape
2.100.10	na te kaś cid daśarataḥs tvaṃ ca tasya na kaś cana anyo rājā tvam anyaś ca tasmāt kuru yad ucyate
2.100.11	gataḥ sa nṛpatis tatra gantavyaṃ yatra tena vai pravṛttir eṣā martyānāṃ tvaṃ tu mithyā vihanyase
2.100.12	arthadharmaparā ye ye tāṃs tāñ śocāmi netarān te hi duḥkham iha prāpya vināśaṃ pretya bhejire
2.100.13	aṣṭakā pitṛdaivatyam ity ayaṃ prasṛto janaḥ annasyopadravaṃ paśya mṛto hi kim aśiṣyati
2.100.14	yadi bhuktam ihānyena deham anyasya gacchati dadyāt pravasataḥ śrāddhaṃ na tat pathy aśanaṃ bhavet
2.100.15	dānasaṃvananā hy ete granthā medhāvibhiḥ kṛtāḥ yajasva dehi dīkṣasva tapas tapyasva saṃtyaja
2.100.16	sa nāsti param ity eva kuru buddhiṃ mahāmate pratyakṣaṃ yat tad ātiṣṭha parokṣaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kuru
2.100.17	satāṃ buddhiṃ puraskṛtya sarvalokanidarśinīm rājyaṃ tvaṃ pratigṛhṇīṣva bharatena prasāditaḥ
2.101.1	jābāles tu vacaḥ śrutvā rāmaḥ satyātmanāṃ varaḥ uvāca parayā yuktyā svabuddhyā cāvipannayā
2.101.2	bhavān me priyakāmārthaṃ vacanaṃ yad ihoktavān akāryaṃ kāryasaṃkāśam apathyaṃ pathyasaṃmitam
2.101.3	nirmaryādas tu puruṣaḥ pāpācārasamanvitaḥ mānaṃ na labhate satsu bhinnacāritradarśanaḥ
2.101.4	kulīnam akulīnaṃ vā vīraṃ puruṣamāninam cāritram eva vyākhyāti śuciṃ vā yadi vāśucim
2.101.5	anāryas tv āryasaṃkāśaḥ śaucād dhīnas tathā śuciḥ lakṣaṇyavad alakṣaṇyo duḥśīlaḥ śīlavān iva
2.101.6	adharmaṃ dharmaveṣeṇa yadīmaṃ lokasaṃkaram abhipatsye śubhaṃ hitvā kriyāvidhivivarjitam
2.101.7	kaś cetayānaḥ puruṣaḥ kāryākāryavicakṣaṇaḥ bahu maṃsyati māṃ loke durvṛttaṃ lokadūṣaṇam
2.101.8	kasya yāsyāmy ahaṃ vṛttaṃ kena vā svargam āpnuyām anayā vartamāno 'haṃ vṛttyā hīnapratijñayā
2.101.9	kāmavṛttas tv ayaṃ lokaḥ kṛtsnaḥ samupavartate yadvṛttāḥ santi rājānas tadvṛttāḥ santi hi prajāḥ
2.101.10	satyam evānṛśaṃsyaṃ ca rājavṛttaṃ sanātanam tasmāt satyātmakaṃ rājyaṃ satye lokaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ
2.101.11	ṛṣayaś caiva devāś ca satyam eva hi menire satyavādī hi loke 'smin paramaṃ gacchati kṣayam
2.101.12	udvijante yathā sarpān narād anṛtavādinaḥ dharmaḥ satyaṃ paro loke mūlaṃ svargasya cocyate
2.101.13	satyam eveśvaro loke satyaṃ padmā samāśritā satyamūlāni sarvāṇi satyān nāsti paraṃ padam
2.101.14	dattam iṣṭaṃ hutaṃ caiva taptāni ca tapāṃsi ca vedāḥ satyapratiṣṭhānās tasmāt satyaparo bhavet
2.101.15	ekaḥ pālayate lokam ekaḥ pālayate kulam majjaty eko hi niraya ekaḥ svarge mahīyate
2.101.16	so 'haṃ pitur nideśaṃ tu kimarthaṃ nānupālaye satyapratiśravaḥ satyaṃ satyena samayīkṛtaḥ
2.101.17	naiva lobhān na mohād vā na cājñānāt tamo'nvitaḥ setuṃ satyasya bhetsyāmi guroḥ satyapratiśravaḥ
2.101.18	asatyasaṃdhasya sataś calasyāsthiracetasaḥ naiva devā na pitaraḥ pratīcchantīti naḥ śrutam
2.101.19	pratyagātmam imaṃ dharmaṃ satyaṃ paśyāmy ahaṃ svayam bhāraḥ satpuruṣācīrṇas tad artham abhinandyate
2.101.20	kṣātraṃ dharmam ahaṃ tyakṣye hy adharmaṃ dharmasaṃhitam kṣudraur nṛśaṃsair lubdhaiś ca sevitaṃ pāpakarmabhiḥ
2.101.21	kāyena kurute pāpaṃ manasā saṃpradhārya ca anṛtaṃ jihvayā cāha trividhaṃ karma pātakam
2.101.22	bhūmiḥ kīrtir yaśo lakṣmīḥ puruṣaṃ prārthayanti hi svargasthaṃ cānubadhnanti satyam eva bhajeta tat
2.101.23	śreṣṭhaṃ hy anāryam eva syād yad bhavān avadhārya mām āha yuktikarair vākyair idaṃ bhadraṃ kuruṣva ha
2.101.24	kathaṃ hy ahaṃ pratijñāya vanavāsam imaṃ guroḥ bharatasya kariṣyāmi vaco hitvā guror vacaḥ
2.101.25	sthirā mayā pratijñātā pratijñā gurusaṃnidhau prahṛṣṭamānasā devī kaikeyī cābhavat tadā
2.101.26	vanavāsaṃ vasann evaṃ śucir niyatabhojanaḥ mūlaiḥ puṣpaiḥ phalaiḥ puṇyaiḥ pitṝn devāṃś ca tarpayan
2.101.27	saṃtuṣṭapañcavargo 'haṃ lokayātrāṃ pravartaye akuhaḥ śraddadhānaḥ san kāryākāryavicakṣaṇaḥ
2.101.28	karmabhūmim imāṃ prāpya kartavyaṃ karma yac chubham agnir vāyuś ca somaś ca karmaṇāṃ phalabhāginaḥ
2.101.29	śataṃ kratūnām āhṛtya devarāṭ tridivaṃ gataḥ tapāṃsy ugrāṇi cāsthāya divaṃ yātā maharṣayaḥ
2.101.30	satyaṃ ca dharmaṃ ca parākramaṃ ca bhūtānukampāṃ priyavāditāṃ ca dvijātidevātithipūjanaṃ ca panthānam āhus tridivasya santaḥ
2.101.31	dharme ratāḥ satpuruṣaiḥ sametās tejasvino dānaguṇapradhānāḥ ahiṃsakā vītamalāś ca loke bhavanti pūjyā munayaḥ pradhānāḥ
2.102.1	kruddham ājñāya rāmaṃ tu vasiṣṭhaḥ pratyuvāca ha jābālir api jānīte lokasyāsya gatāgatim nivartayitukāmas tu tvām etad vākyam abravīt
2.102.2	imāṃ lokasamutpattiṃ lokanātha nibodha me sarvaṃ salilam evāsīt pṛthivī yatra nirmitā tataḥ samabhavad brahmā svayambhūr daivataiḥ saha
2.102.3	sa varāhas tato bhūtvā projjahāra vasuṃdharām asṛjac ca jagat sarvaṃ saha putraiḥ kṛtātmabhiḥ
2.102.4	ākāśaprabhavo brahmā śāśvato nitya avyayaḥ tasmān marīciḥ saṃjajñe marīceḥ kaśyapaḥ sutaḥ
2.102.5	vivasvān kaśyapāj jajñe manur vaivasvataḥ smṛtaḥ sa tu prajāpatiḥ pūrvam ikṣvākus tu manoḥ sutaḥ
2.102.6	yasyeyaṃ prathamaṃ dattā samṛddhā manunā mahī tam ikṣvākum ayodhyāyāṃ rājānaṃ viddhi pūrvakam
2.102.7	ikṣvākos tu sutaḥ śrīmān kukṣir eveti viśrutaḥ kukṣer athātmajo vīro vikukṣir udapadyata
2.102.8	vikukṣes tu mahātejā bāṇaḥ putraḥ pratāpavān bāṇasya tu mahābāhur anaraṇyo mahāyaśāḥ
2.102.9	nānāvṛṣṭir babhūvāsmin na durbhikṣaṃ satāṃ vare anaraṇye mahārāje taskaro vāpi kaś cana
2.102.10	anaraṇyān mahābāhuḥ pṛthū rājā babhūva ha tasmāt pṛthor mahārājas triśaṅkur udapadyata sa satyavacanād vīraḥ saśarīro divaṃ gataḥ
2.102.11	triśaṅkor abhavat sūnur dhundhumāro mahāyaśāḥ dhundhumārān mahātejā yuvanāśvo vyajāyata
2.102.12	yuvanāśva sutaḥ śrīmān māndhātā samapadyata māndhātus tu mahātejāḥ susaṃdhir udapadyata
2.102.13	susaṃdher api putrau dvau dhruvasaṃdhiḥ prasenajit yaśasvī dhruvasaṃdhes tu bharato ripusūdanaḥ
2.102.14	bharatāt tu mahābāhor asito nāma jāyata yasyaite pratirājāna udapadyanta śatravaḥ haihayās tālajaṅghāś ca śūrāś ca śaśabindavaḥ
2.102.15	tāṃs tu sarvān prativyūhya yuddhe rājā pravāsitaḥ sa ca śailavare ramye babhūvābhirato muniḥ dve cāsya bhārye garbhiṇyau babhūvatur iti śrutiḥ
2.102.16	bhārgavaś cyavano nāma himavantam upāśritaḥ tam ṛṣiṃ samupāgamya kālindī tv abhyavādayat
2.102.17	sa tām abhyavadad vipro varepsuṃ putrajanmani tataḥ sā gṛham āgamya devī putraṃ vyajāyata
2.102.18	sapatnyā tu garas tasyai datto garbhajighāṃsayā gareṇa saha tenaiva jātaḥ sa sagaro 'bhavat
2.102.19	sa rājā sagaro nāma yaḥ samudram akhānayat iṣṭvā parvaṇi vegena trāsayantam imāḥ prajāḥ
2.102.20	asamañjas tu putro 'bhūt sagarasyeti naḥ śrutam jīvann eva sa pitrā tu nirastaḥ pāpakarmakṛt
2.102.21	aṃśumān iti putro 'bhūd asamañjasya vīryavān dilīpo 'ṃśumataḥ putro dilīpasya bhagīrathaḥ
2.102.22	bhagīrathāt kakutsthas tu kākutsthā yena tu smṛtāḥ kakutsthasya tu putro 'bhūd raghur yena tu rāghavaḥ
2.102.23	raghos tu putras tejasvī pravṛddhaḥ puruṣādakaḥ kalmāṣapādaḥ saudāsa ity evaṃ prathito bhuvi
2.102.24	kalmāṣapādaputro 'bhūc chaṅkhaṇas tv iti viśrutaḥ yas tu tad vīryam āsādya sahaseno vyanīnaśat
2.102.25	śaṅkhaṇasya tu putro 'bhūc chūraḥ śrīmān sudarśanaḥ sudarśanasyāgnivarṇa agnivarṣasya śīghragaḥ
2.102.26	śīghragasya maruḥ putro maroḥ putraḥ praśuśrukaḥ praśuśrukasya putro 'bhūd ambarīṣo mahādyutiḥ
2.102.27	ambarīṣasya putro 'bhūn nahuṣaḥ satyavikramaḥ nahuṣasya ca nābhāgaḥ putraḥ paramadhārmikaḥ
2.102.28	ajaś ca suvrataś caiva nābhāgasya sutāv ubhau ajasya caiva dharmātmā rājā daśarathaḥ sutaḥ
2.102.29	tasya jyeṣṭho 'si dāyādo rāma ity abhiviśrutaḥ tad gṛhāṇa svakaṃ rājyam avekṣasva jagan nṛpa
2.102.30	ikṣvākūṇāṃ hi sarveṣāṃ rājā bhavati pūrvajaḥ pūrvaje nāvaraḥ putro jyeṣṭho rājye 'bhiṣicyate
2.102.31	sa rāghavāṇāṃ kuladharmam ātmanaḥ sanātanaṃ nādya vihātum arhasi prabhūtaratnām anuśādhi medinīṃ prabhūtarāṣṭrāṃ pitṛvan mahāyaśāḥ
2.103.1	vasiṣṭhas tu tadā rāmam uktvā rājapurohitaḥ abravīd dharmasaṃyuktaṃ punar evāparaṃ vacaḥ
2.103.2	puruṣasyeha jātasya bhavanti guravas trayaḥ ācāryaś caiva kākutstha pitā mātā ca rāghava
2.103.3	pitā hy enaṃ janayati puruṣaṃ puruṣarṣabha prajñāṃ dadāti cācāryas tasmāt sa gurur ucyate
2.103.4	sa te 'haṃ pitur ācāryas tava caiva paraṃtapa mama tvaṃ vacanaṃ kurvan nātivarteḥ satāṃ gatim
2.103.5	imā hi te pariṣadaḥ śreṇayaś ca samāgatāḥ eṣu tāta caran dharmaṃ nātivarteḥ satāṃ gatim
2.103.6	vṛddhāyā dharmaśīlāyā mātur nārhasy avartitum asyās tu vacanaṃ kurvan nātivarteḥ satāṃ gatim
2.103.7	bharatasya vacaḥ kurvan yācamānasya rāghava ātmānaṃ nātivartes tvaṃ satyadharmaparākrama
2.103.8	evaṃ madhuram uktas tu guruṇā rāghavaḥ svayam pratyuvāca samāsīnaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ puruṣarṣabhaḥ
2.103.9	yan mātāpitarau vṛttaṃ tanaye kurutaḥ sadā na supratikaraṃ tat tu mātrā pitrā ca yat kṛtam
2.103.10	yathāśakti pradānena snāpanāc chādanena ca nityaṃ ca priyavādena tathā saṃvardhanena ca
2.103.11	sa hi rājā janayitā pitā daśaratho mama ājñātaṃ yan mayā tasya na tan mithyā bhaviṣyati
2.103.12	evam uktas tu rāmeṇa bharataḥ pratyanantaram uvāca paramodāraḥ sūtaṃ paramadurmanāḥ
2.103.13	iha me sthaṇḍile śīghraṃ kuśān āstara sārathe āryaṃ pratyupavekṣyāmi yāvan me na prasīdati
2.103.14	anāhāro nirāloko dhanahīno yathā dvijaḥ śeṣye purastāc chālāyā yāvan na pratiyāsyati
2.103.15	sa tu rāmam avekṣantaṃ sumantraṃ prekṣya durmanāḥ kuśottaram upasthāpya bhūmāv evāstarat svayam
2.103.16	tam uvāca mahātejā rāmo rājarṣisattamāḥ kiṃ māṃ bharata kurvāṇaṃ tāta pratyupavekṣyasi
2.103.17	brāhmaṇo hy ekapārśvena narān roddhum ihārhati na tu mūrdhāvasiktānāṃ vidhiḥ pratyupaveśane
2.103.18	uttiṣṭha naraśārdūla hitvaitad dāruṇaṃ vratam puravaryām itaḥ kṣipram ayodhyāṃ yāhi rāghava
2.103.19	āsīnas tv eva bharataḥ paurajānapadaṃ janam uvāca sarvataḥ prekṣya kim āryaṃ nānuśāsatha
2.103.20	te tam ūcur mahātmānaṃ paurajānapadā janāḥ kākutstham abhijānīmaḥ samyag vadati rāghavaḥ
2.103.21	eṣo 'pi hi mahābhāgaḥ pitur vacasi tiṣṭhati ata eva na śaktāḥ smo vyāvartayitum añjasā
2.103.22	teṣām ājñāya vacanaṃ rāmo vacanam abravīt evaṃ nibodha vacanaṃ suhṛdāṃ dharmacakṣuṣām
2.103.23	etac caivobhayaṃ śrutvā samyak saṃpaśya rāghava uttiṣṭha tvaṃ mahābāho māṃ ca spṛśa tathodakam
2.103.24	athotthāya jalaṃ spṛṣṭvā bharato vākyam abravīt śṛṇvantu me pariṣado mantriṇaḥ śreṇayas tathā
2.103.25	na yāce pitaraṃ rājyaṃ nānuśāsāmi mātaram āryaṃ paramadharmajñam abhijānāmi rāghavam
2.103.26	yadi tv avaśyaṃ vastavyaṃ kartavyaṃ ca pitur vacaḥ aham eva nivatsyāmi caturdaśa vane samāḥ
2.103.27	dharmātmā tasya tathyena bhrātur vākyena vismitaḥ uvāca rāmaḥ saṃprekṣya paurajānapadaṃ janam
2.103.28	vikrītam āhitaṃ krītaṃ yat pitrā jīvatā mama na tal lopayituṃ śakyaṃ mayā vā bharatena vā
2.103.29	upadhir na mayā kāryo vanavāse jugupsitaḥ yuktam uktaṃ ca kaikeyyā pitrā me sukṛtaṃ kṛtam
2.103.30	jānāmi bharataṃ kṣāntaṃ gurusatkārakāriṇam sarvam evātra kalyāṇaṃ satyasaṃdhe mahātmani
2.103.31	anena dharmaśīlena vanāt pratyāgataḥ punaḥ bhrātrā saha bhaviṣyāmi pṛthivyāḥ patir uttamaḥ
2.103.32	vṛto rājā hi kaikeyyā mayā tad vacanaṃ kṛtam anṛtān mocayānena pitaraṃ taṃ mahīpatim
2.104.1	tam apratimatejobhyāṃ bhrātṛbhyāṃ romaharṣaṇam vismitāḥ saṃgamaṃ prekṣya samavetā maharṣayaḥ
2.104.2	antarhitās tv ṛṣigaṇāḥ siddhāś ca paramarṣayaḥ tau bhrātarau mahātmānau kākutsthau praśaśaṃsire
2.104.3	sa dhanyo yasya putrau dvau dharmajñau dharmavikramau śrutvā vayaṃ hi saṃbhāṣām ubhayoḥ spṛhayāmahe
2.104.4	tatas tv ṛṣigaṇāḥ kṣipraṃ daśagrīvavadhaiṣiṇaḥ bharataṃ rājaśārdūlam ity ūcuḥ saṃgatā vacaḥ
2.104.5	kule jāta mahāprājña mahāvṛtta mahāyaśaḥ grāhyaṃ rāmasya vākyaṃ te pitaraṃ yady avekṣase
2.104.6	sadānṛṇam imaṃ rāmaṃ vayam icchāmahe pituḥ anṛṇatvāc ca kaikeyyāḥ svargaṃ daśaratho gataḥ
2.104.7	etāvad uktvā vacanaṃ gandharvāḥ samaharṣayaḥ rājarṣayaś caiva tathā sarve svāṃ svāṃ gatiṃ gatāḥ
2.104.8	hlāditas tena vākyena śubhena śubhadarśanaḥ rāmaḥ saṃhṛṣṭavadanas tān ṛṣīn abhyapūjayat
2.104.9	srastagātras tu bharataḥ sa vācā sajjamānayā kṛtāñjalir idaṃ vākyaṃ rāghavaṃ punar abravīt
2.104.10	rājadharmam anuprekṣya kuladharmānusaṃtatim kartum arhasi kākutstha mama mātuś ca yācanām
2.104.11	rakṣituṃ sumahad rājyam aham ekas tu notsahe paurajānapadāṃś cāpi raktān rañjayituṃ tathā
2.104.12	jñātayaś ca hi yodhāś ca mitrāṇi suhṛdaś ca naḥ tvām eva pratikāṅkṣante parjanyam iva karṣakāḥ
2.104.13	idaṃ rājyaṃ mahāprājña sthāpaya pratipadya hi śaktimān asi kākutstha lokasya paripālane
2.104.14	ity uktvā nyapatad bhrātuḥ pādayor bharatas tadā bhṛśaṃ saṃprārthayām āsa rāmam evaṃ priyaṃ vadaḥ
2.104.15	tam aṅke bhrātaraṃ kṛtvā rāmo vacanam abravīt śyāmaṃ nalinapatrākṣaṃ mattahaṃsasvaraḥ svayam
2.104.16	āgatā tvām iyaṃ buddhiḥ svajā vainayikī ca yā bhṛśam utsahase tāta rakṣituṃ pṛthivīm api
2.104.17	amātyaiś ca suhṛdbhiś ca buddhimadbhiś ca mantribhiḥ sarvakāryāṇi saṃmantrya sumahānty api kāraya
2.104.18	lakṣmīś candrād apeyād vā himavān vā himaṃ tyajet atīyāt sāgaro velāṃ na pratijñām ahaṃ pituḥ
2.104.19	kāmād vā tāta lobhād vā mātrā tubhyam idaṃ kṛtam na tan manasi kartavyaṃ vartitavyaṃ ca mātṛvat
2.104.20	evaṃ bruvāṇaṃ bharataḥ kausalyāsutam abravīt tejasādityasaṃkāśaṃ pratipaccandradarśanam
2.104.21	adhirohārya pādābhyāṃ pāduke hemabhūṣite ete hi sarvalokasya yogakṣemaṃ vidhāsyataḥ
2.104.22	so 'dhiruhya naravyāghraḥ pāduke hy avaruhya ca prāyacchat sumahātejā bharatāya mahātmane
2.104.23	sa pāduke te bharataḥ pratāpavān svalaṃkṛte saṃparigṛhya dharmavit pradakṣiṇaṃ caiva cakāra rāghavaṃ cakāra caivottamanāgamūrdhani
2.104.24	athānupūrvyāt pratipūjya taṃ janaṃ gurūṃś ca mantriprakṛtīs tathānujau vyasarjayad rāghavavaṃśavardhanaḥ sthitaḥ svadharme himavān ivācalaḥ
2.104.25	taṃ mātaro bāṣpagṛhītakaṇṭho duḥkhena nāmantrayituṃ hi śekuḥ sa tv eva mātṝr abhivādya sarvā rudan kuṭīṃ svāṃ praviveśa rāmaḥ
2.105.1	tataḥ śirasi kṛtvā tu pāduke bharatas tadā āruroha rathaṃ hṛṣṭaḥ śatrughnena samanvitaḥ
2.105.2	vasiṣṭho vāmadevaś ca jābāliś ca dṛḍhavrataḥ agrataḥ prayayuḥ sarve mantriṇo mantrapūjitāḥ
2.105.3	mandākinīṃ nadīṃ ramyāṃ prāṅmukhās te yayus tadā pradakṣiṇaṃ ca kurvāṇāś citrakūṭaṃ mahāgirim
2.105.4	paśyan dhātusahasrāṇi ramyāṇi vividhāni ca prayayau tasya pārśvena sasainyo bharatas tadā
2.105.5	adūrāc citrakūṭasya dadarśa bharatas tadā āśramaṃ yatra sa munir bharadvājaḥ kṛtālayaḥ
2.105.6	sa tam āśramam āgamya bharadvājasya buddhimān avatīrya rathāt pādau vavande kulanandanaḥ
2.105.7	tato hṛṣṭo bharadvājo bharataṃ vākyam abravīt api kṛtyaṃ kṛtaṃ tāta rāmeṇa ca samāgatam
2.105.8	evam uktas tu bharato bharadvājena dhīmatā pratyuvāca bharadvājaṃ bharato dharmavatsalaḥ
2.105.9	sa yācyamāno guruṇā mayā ca dṛḍhavikramaḥ rāghavaḥ paramaprīto vasiṣṭhaṃ vākyam abravīt
2.105.10	pituḥ pratijñāṃ tām eva pālayiṣyāmi tattvataḥ caturdaśa hi varṣāṇi ya pratijñā pitur mama
2.105.11	evam ukto mahāprājño vasiṣṭhaḥ pratyuvāca ha vākyajño vākyakuśalaṃ rāghavaṃ vacanaṃ mahat
2.105.12	ete prayaccha saṃhṛṣṭaḥ pāduke hemabhūṣite ayodhyāyāṃ mahāprājña yogakṣemakare tava
2.105.13	evam ukto vasiṣṭhena rāghavaḥ prāṅmukhaḥ sthitaḥ pāduke hemavikṛte mama rājyāya te dadau
2.105.14	nivṛtto 'ham anujñāto rāmeṇa sumahātmanā ayodhyām eva gacchāmi gṛhītvā pāduke śubhe
2.105.15	etac chrutvā śubhaṃ vākyaṃ bharatasya mahātmanaḥ bharadvājaḥ śubhataraṃ munir vākyam udāharat
2.105.16	naitac citraṃ naravyāghra śīlavṛttavatāṃ vara yad āryaṃ tvayi tiṣṭhet tu nimne vṛṣṭim ivodakam
2.105.17	amṛtaḥ sa mahābāhuḥ pitā daśarathas tava yasya tvam īdṛśaḥ putro dharmātmā dharmavatsalaḥ
2.105.18	tam ṛṣiṃ tu mahātmānam uktavākyaṃ kṛtāñjaliḥ āmantrayitum ārebhe caraṇāv upagṛhya ca
2.105.19	tataḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā bharadvājaṃ punaḥ punaḥ bharatas tu yayau śrīmān ayodhyāṃ saha mantribhiḥ
2.105.20	yānaiś ca śakaṭaiś caiva hayaiś nāgaiś ca sā camūḥ punar nivṛttā vistīrṇā bharatasyānuyāyinī
2.105.21	tatas te yamunāṃ divyāṃ nadīṃ tīrtvormimālinīm dadṛśus tāṃ punaḥ sarve gaṅgāṃ śivajalāṃ nadīm
2.105.22	tāṃ ramyajalasaṃpūrṇāṃ saṃtīrya saha bāndhavaḥ śṛṅgaverapuraṃ ramyaṃ praviveśa sasainikaḥ
2.105.23	śṛṅgaverapurād bhūya ayodhyāṃ saṃdadarśa ha bharato duḥkhasaṃtaptaḥ sārathiṃ cedam abravīt
2.105.24	sārathe paśya vidhvastā ayodhyā na prakāśate nirākārā nirānandā dīnā pratihatasvanā
2.106.1	snigdhagambhīraghoṣeṇa syandanenopayān prabhuḥ ayodhyāṃ bharataḥ kṣipraṃ praviveśa mahāyaśāḥ
2.106.2	biḍālolūkacaritām ālīnanaravāraṇām timirābhyāhatāṃ kālīm aprakāśāṃ niśām iva
2.106.3	rāhuśatroḥ priyāṃ patnīṃ śriyā prajvalitaprabhām graheṇābhyutthitenaikāṃ rohiṇīm iva pīḍitām
2.106.4	alpoṣṇakṣubdhasalilāṃ gharmottaptavihaṃgamām līnamīnajhaṣagrāhāṃ kṛśāṃ girinadīm iva
2.106.5	vidhūmām iva hemābhām adhvarāgnisamutthitām havirabhyukṣitāṃ paścāc chikhāṃ vipralayaṃ gatām
2.106.6	vidhvastakavacāṃ rugṇagajavājirathadhvajām hatapravīrām āpannāṃ camūm iva mahāhave
2.106.7	saphenāṃ sasvanāṃ bhūtvā sāgarasya samutthitām praśāntamārutoddhūtāṃ jalormim iva niḥsvanām
2.106.8	tyaktāṃ yajñāyudhaiḥ sarvair abhirūpaiś ca yājakaiḥ sutyākāle vinirvṛtte vediṃ gataravām iva
2.106.9	goṣṭhamadhye sthitām ārtām acarantīṃ navaṃ tṛṇam govṛṣeṇa parityaktāṃ gavāṃ patnīm ivotsukām
2.106.10	prabhākarālaiḥ susnigdhaiḥ prajvaladbhir ivottamaiḥ viyuktāṃ maṇibhir jātyair navāṃ muktāvalīm iva
2.106.11	sahasā calitāṃ sthānān mahīṃ puṇyakṣayād gatām saṃhṛtadyutivistārāṃ tārām iva divaś cyutām
2.106.12	puṣpanaddhāṃ vasantānte mattabhramaraśālinīm drutadāvāgnivipluṣṭāṃ klāntāṃ vanalatām iva
2.106.13	saṃmūḍhanigamāṃ sarvāṃ saṃkṣiptavipaṇāpaṇām pracchannaśaśinakṣatrāṃ dyām ivāmbudharair vṛtām
2.106.14	kṣīṇapānottamair bhinnaiḥ śarāvair abhisaṃvṛtām hataśauṇḍām ivākāśe pānabhūmim asaṃskṛtām
2.106.15	vṛkṇabhūmitalāṃ nimnāṃ vṛkṇapātraiḥ samāvṛtām upayuktodakāṃ bhagnāṃ prapāṃ nipatitām iva
2.106.16	vipulāṃ vitatāṃ caiva yuktapāśāṃ tarasvinām bhūmau bāṇair viniṣkṛttāṃ patitāṃ jyām ivāyudhāt
2.106.17	sahasā yuddhaśauṇḍena hayāroheṇa vāhitām nikṣiptabhāṇḍām utsṛṣṭāṃ kiśorīm iva durbalām
2.106.18	prāvṛṣi pravigāḍhāyāṃ praviṣṭasyābhra maṇḍalam pracchannāṃ nīlajīmūtair bhāskarasya prabhām iva
2.106.19	bharatas tu rathasthaḥ sañ śrīmān daśarathātmajaḥ vāhayantaṃ rathaśreṣṭhaṃ sārathiṃ vākyam abravīt
2.106.20	kiṃ nu khalv adya gambhīro mūrchito na niśamyate yathāpuram ayodhyāyāṃ gītavāditraniḥsvanaḥ
2.106.21	vāruṇīmadagandhāś ca mālyagandhaś ca mūrchitaḥ dhūpitāgarugandhaś ca na pravāti samantataḥ
2.106.22	yānapravaraghoṣaś ca snigdhaś ca hayaniḥsvanaḥ pramattagajanādaś ca mahāṃś ca rathaniḥsvanaḥ nedānīṃ śrūyate puryām asyāṃ rāme vivāsite
2.106.23	taruṇaiś cāru veṣaiś ca narair unnatagāmibhiḥ saṃpatadbhir ayodhyāyāṃ na vibhānti mahāpathāḥ
2.106.24	evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ jalpan viveśa vasatiṃ pituḥ tena hīnāṃ narendreṇa siṃhahīnāṃ guhām iva
2.107.1	tato nikṣipya mātṝḥ sa ayodhyāyāṃ dṛḍhavrataḥ bharataḥ śokasaṃtapto gurūn idam athābravīt
2.107.2	nandigrāmaṃ gamiṣyāmi sarvān āmantraye 'dya vaḥ tatra duḥkham idaṃ sarvaṃ sahiṣye rāghavaṃ vinā
2.107.3	gataś ca hi divaṃ rājā vanasthaś ca gurur mama rāmaṃ pratīkṣe rājyāya sa hi rājā mahāyaśāḥ
2.107.4	etac chrutvā śubhaṃ vākyaṃ bharatasya mahātmanaḥ abruvan mantriṇaḥ sarve vasiṣṭhaś ca purohitaḥ
2.107.5	sadṛśaṃ ślāghanīyaṃ ca yad uktaṃ bharata tvayā vacanaṃ bhrātṛvātsalyād anurūpaṃ tavaiva tat
2.107.6	nityaṃ te bandhulubdhasya tiṣṭhato bhrātṛsauhṛde āryamārgaṃ prapannasya nānumanyeta kaḥ pumān
2.107.7	mantriṇāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā yathābhilaṣitaṃ priyam abravīt sārathiṃ vākyaṃ ratho me yujyatām iti
2.107.8	prahṛṣṭavadanaḥ sarvā mātṝḥ samabhivādya saḥ āruroha rathaṃ śrīmāñ śatrughnena samanvitaḥ
2.107.9	āruhya tu rathaṃ śīghraṃ śatrughnabharatāv ubhau yayatuḥ paramaprītau vṛtau mantripurohitaiḥ
2.107.10	agrato puravas tatra vasiṣṭha pramukhā dvijāḥ prayayuḥ prāṅmukhāḥ sarve nandigrāmo yato 'bhavat
2.107.11	balaṃ ca tad anāhūtaṃ gajāśvarathasaṃkulam prayayau bharate yāte sarve ca puravāsinaḥ
2.107.12	rathasthaḥ sa tu dharmātmā bharato bhrātṛvatsalaḥ nandigrāmaṃ yayau tūrṇaṃ śirasy ādhāya pāduke
2.107.13	tatas tu bharataḥ kṣipraṃ nandigrāmaṃ praviśya saḥ avatīrya rathāt tūrṇaṃ gurūn idam uvāca ha
2.107.14	etad rājyaṃ mama bhrātrā dattaṃ saṃnyāsavat svayam yogakṣemavahe ceme pāduke hemabhūṣite tam imaṃ pālayiṣyāmi rāghavāgamanaṃ prati
2.107.15	kṣipraṃ saṃyojayitvā tu rāghavasya punaḥ svayam caraṇau tau tu rāmasya drakṣyāmi sahapādukau
2.107.16	tato nikṣiptabhāro 'haṃ rāghaveṇa samāgataḥ nivedya gurave rājyaṃ bhajiṣye guruvṛttitām
2.107.17	rāghavāya ca saṃnyāsaṃ dattveme varapāduke rājyaṃ cedam ayodhyāṃ ca dhūtapāpo bhavāmi ca
2.107.18	abhiṣikte tu kākutsthe prahṛṣṭamudite jane prītir mama yaśaś caiva bhaved rājyāc caturguṇam
2.107.19	evaṃ tu vilapan dīno bharataḥ sa mahāyaśāḥ nandigrāme 'karod rājyaṃ duḥkhito mantribhiḥ saha
2.107.20	sa valkalajaṭādhārī muniveṣadharaḥ prabhuḥ nandigrāme 'vasad vīraḥ sasainyo bharatas tadā
2.107.21	rāmāgamanam ākāṅkṣan bharato bhrātṛvatsalaḥ bhrātur vacanakārī ca pratijñāpāragas tadā
2.107.22	pāduke tv abhiṣicyātha nandigrāme 'vasat tadā bharataḥ śāsanaṃ sarvaṃ pādukābhyāṃ nyavedayat
2.108.1	pratiprayāte bharate vasan rāmas tapovane lakṣayām āsa sodvegam athautsukyaṃ tapasvinām
2.108.2	ye tatra citrakūṭasya purastāt tāpasāśrame rāmam āśritya niratās tān alakṣayad utsukān
2.108.3	nayanair bhṛkuṭībhiś ca rāmaṃ nirdiśya śaṅkitāḥ anyonyam upajalpantaḥ śanaiś cakrur mithaḥ kathāḥ
2.108.4	teṣām autsukyam ālakṣya rāmas tv ātmani śaṅkitaḥ kṛtāñjalir uvācedam ṛṣiṃ kulapatiṃ tataḥ
2.108.5	na kaccid bhagavan kiṃ cit pūrvavṛttam idaṃ mayi dṛśyate vikṛtaṃ yena vikriyante tapasvinaḥ
2.108.6	pramādāc caritaṃ kaccit kiṃ cin nāvarajasya me lakṣmaṇasyarṣibhir dṛṣṭaṃ nānurūpam ivātmanaḥ
2.108.7	kaccic chuśrūṣamāṇā vaḥ śuśrūṣaṇaparā mayi pramadābhyucitāṃ vṛttiṃ sītā yuktaṃ na vartate
2.108.8	atharṣir jarayā vṛddhas tapasā ca jarāṃ gataḥ vepamāna ivovāca rāmaṃ bhūtadayāparam
2.108.9	kutaḥ kalyāṇasattvāyāḥ kalyāṇābhirates tathā calanaṃ tāta vaidehyās tapasviṣu viśeṣataḥ
2.108.10	tvannimittam idaṃ tāvat tāpasān prati vartate rakṣobhyas tena saṃvignāḥ kathayanti mithaḥ kathāḥ
2.108.11	rāvaṇāvarajaḥ kaś cit kharo nāmeha rākṣasaḥ utpāṭya tāpasān sarvāñ janasthānaniketanān
2.108.12	dhṛṣṭaś ca jitakāśī ca nṛśaṃsaḥ puruṣādakaḥ avaliptaś ca pāpaś ca tvāṃ ca tāta na mṛṣyate
2.108.13	tvaṃ yadā prabhṛti hy asminn āśrame tāta vartase tadā prabhṛti rakṣāṃsi viprakurvanti tāpasān
2.108.14	darśayanti hi bībhatsaiḥ krūrair bhīṣaṇakair api nānā rūpair virūpaiś ca rūpair asukhadarśanaiḥ
2.108.15	apraśastair aśucibhiḥ saṃprayojya ca tāpasān pratighnanty aparān kṣipram anāryāḥ purataḥ sthitaḥ
2.108.16	teṣu teṣv āśramasthāneṣv abuddham avalīya ca ramante tāpasāṃs tatra nāśayanto 'lpacetasaḥ
2.108.17	apakṣipanti srugbhāṇḍān agnīn siñcanti vāriṇā kalaśāṃś ca pramṛdnanti havane samupasthite
2.108.18	tair durātmabhir āviṣṭān āśramān prajihāsavaḥ gamanāyānyadeśasya codayanty ṛṣayo 'dya mām
2.108.19	tat purā rāma śārīrām upahiṃsāṃ tapasviṣu darśayati hi duṣṭās te tyakṣyāma imam āśramam
2.108.20	bahumūlaphalaṃ citram avidūrād ito vanam purāṇāśramam evāhaṃ śrayiṣye sagaṇaḥ punaḥ
2.108.21	kharas tvayy api cāyuktaṃ purā tāta pravartate sahāsmābhir ito gaccha yadi buddhiḥ pravartate
2.108.22	sakalatrasya saṃdeho nityaṃ yat tasya rāghava samarthasyāpi hi sato vāso duḥkha ihādya te
2.108.23	ity uktavantaṃ rāmas taṃ rājaputras tapasvinam na śaśākottarair vākyair avaroddhuṃ samutsukam
2.108.24	abhinandya samāpṛcchya samādhāya ca rāghavam sa jagāmāśramaṃ tyaktvā kulaiḥ kulapatiḥ saha
2.108.25	rāmaḥ saṃsādhya tv ṛṣigaṇam anugamanād deśāt tasmāccit kulapatim abhivādyarṣim samyakprītais tair anumata upadiṣṭārthaḥ puṇyaṃ vāsāya svanilayam upasaṃpede
2.108.26	āśramaṃ tv ṛṣivirahitaṃ prabhuḥ kṣaṇam api na jahau sa rāghavaḥ rāghavaṃ hi satatam anugatās tāpasāś carṣicaritadhṛtaguṇāḥ
2.109.1	rāghavas tv apayāteṣu tapasviṣu vicintayan na tatrārocayad vāsaṃ kāraṇair bahubhis tadā
2.109.2	iha me bharato dṛṣṭo mātaraś ca sanāgarāḥ sā ca me smṛtir anveti tān nityam anuśocataḥ
2.109.3	skandhāvāraniveśena tena tasya mahātmanaḥ hayahastikarīṣaiś ca upamardaḥ kṛto bhṛśam
2.109.4	tasmād anyatra gacchāma iti saṃcintya rāghavaḥ prātiṣṭhata sa vaidehyā lakṣmaṇena ca saṃgataḥ
2.109.5	so 'trer āśramam āsādya taṃ vavande mahāyaśāḥ taṃ cāpi bhagavān atriḥ putravat pratyapadyata
2.109.6	svayam ātithyam ādiśya sarvam asya susatkṛtam saumitriṃ ca mahābhāgāṃ sītāṃ ca samasāntvayat
2.109.7	patnīṃ ca tam anuprāptāṃ vṛddhām āmantrya satkṛtām sāntvayām āsa dharmajñaḥ sarvabhūtahite rataḥ
2.109.8	anasūyāṃ mahābhāgāṃ tāpasīṃ dharmacāriṇīm pratigṛhṇīṣva vaidehīm abravīd ṛṣisattamaḥ
2.109.9	rāmāya cācacakṣe tāṃ tāpasīṃ dharmacāriṇīm daśa varṣāṇy anāvṛṣṭyā dagdhe loke nirantaram
2.109.10	yayā mūlaphale sṛṣṭe jāhnavī ca pravartitā ugreṇa tapasā yuktā niyamaiś cāpy alaṃkṛtā
2.109.11	daśavarṣasahasrāṇi yayā taptaṃ mahat tapaḥ anasūyāvratais tāta pratyūhāś ca nibarhitāḥ
2.109.12	devakāryanimittaṃ ca yayā saṃtvaramāṇayā daśarātraṃ kṛtvā rātriḥ seyaṃ māteva te 'nagha
2.109.13	tām imāṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ namaskāryāṃ yaśasvinīm abhigacchatu vaidehī vṛddhām akrodhanāṃ sadā
2.109.14	evaṃ bruvāṇaṃ tam ṛṣiṃ tathety uktvā sa rāghavaḥ sītām uvāca dharmajñām idaṃ vacanam uttamam
2.109.15	rājaputri śrutaṃ tv etan muner asya samīritam śreyo 'rtham ātmanaḥ śīghram abhigaccha tapasvinīm
2.109.16	anasūyeti yā loke karmabhiḥ kyātim āgatā tāṃ śīghram abhigaccha tvam abhigamyāṃ tapasvinīm
2.109.17	sītā tv etad vacaḥ śrutvā rāghavasya hitaiṣiṇī tām atripatnīṃ dharmajñām abhicakrāma maithilī
2.109.18	śithilāṃ valitāṃ vṛddhāṃ jarāpāṇḍuramūrdhajām satataṃ vepamānāṅgīṃ pravāte kadalī yathā
2.109.19	tāṃ tu sītā mahābhāgām anasūyāṃ pativratām abhyavādayad avyagrā svaṃ nāma samudāharat
2.109.20	abhivādya ca vaidehī tāpasīṃ tām aninditām baddhāñjalipuṭā hṛṣṭā paryapṛcchad anāmayam
2.109.21	tataḥ sītāṃ mahābhāgāṃ dṛṣṭvā tāṃ dharmacāriṇīm sāntvayanty abravīd dhṛṣṭā diṣṭyā dharmam avekṣase
2.109.22	tyaktvā jñātijanaṃ sīte mānam ṛddhiṃ ca mānini avaruddhaṃ vane rāmaṃ diṣṭyā tvam anugacchasi
2.109.23	nagarastho vanastho vā pāpo vā yadi vāśubhaḥ yāsāṃ strīṇāṃ priyo bhartā tāsāṃ lokā mahodayāḥ
2.109.24	duḥśīlaḥ kāmavṛtto vā dhanair vā parivarjitaḥ strīṇām ārya svabhāvānāṃ paramaṃ daivataṃ patiḥ
2.109.25	nāto viśiṣṭaṃ paśyāmi bāndhavaṃ vimṛśanty aham sarvatra yogyaṃ vaidehi tapaḥ kṛtam ivāvyayam
2.109.26	na tv evam avagacchanti guṇa doṣam asat striyaḥ kāmavaktavyahṛdayā bhartṛnāthāś caranti yāḥ
2.109.27	prāpnuvanty ayaśaś caiva dharmabhraṃśaṃ ca maithili akārya vaśam āpannāḥ striyo yāḥ khalu tad vidhāḥ
2.109.28	tvadvidhās tu guṇair yuktā dṛṣṭalokaparāvarāḥ striyaḥ svarge cariṣyanti yathā puṇyakṛtas tathā
2.110.1	sā tv evam uktā vaidehī anasūyān asūyayā pratipūjya vaco mandaṃ pravaktum upacakrame
2.110.2	naitad āścaryam āryāyā yan māṃ tvam anubhāṣase viditaṃ tu mamāpy etad yathā nāryāḥ patir guruḥ
2.110.3	yady apy eṣa bhaved bhartā mamārye vṛttavarjitaḥ advaidham upavartavyas tathāpy eṣa mayā bhavet
2.110.4	kiṃ punar yo guṇaślāghyaḥ sānukrośo jitendriyaḥ sthirānurāgo dharmātmā mātṛvartī pitṛ priyaḥ
2.110.5	yāṃ vṛttiṃ vartate rāmaḥ kausalyāyāṃ mahābalaḥ tām eva nṛpanārīṇām anyāsām api vartate
2.110.6	sakṛd dṛṣṭāsv api strīṣu nṛpeṇa nṛpavatsalaḥ mātṛvad vartate vīro mānam utsṛjya dharmavit
2.110.7	āgacchantyāś ca vijanaṃ vanam evaṃ bhayāvaham samāhitaṃ hi me śvaśrvā hṛdaye yat sthitaṃ mama
2.110.8	prāṇipradānakāle ca yat purā tv agnisaṃnidhau anuśiṣṭā jananyāsmi vākyaṃ tad api me dhṛtam
2.110.9	navīkṛtaṃ tu tat sarvaṃ vākyais te dharmacāriṇi patiśuśrūṣaṇān nāryās tapo nānyad vidhīyate
2.110.10	sāvitrī patiśuśrūṣāṃ kṛtvā svarge mahīyate tathā vṛttiś ca yātā tvaṃ patiśuśrūṣayā divam
2.110.11	variṣṭhā sarvanārīṇām eṣā ca divi devatā rohiṇī ca vinā candraṃ muhūrtam api dṛśyate
2.110.12	evaṃvidhāś ca pravarāḥ striyo bhartṛdṛḍhavratāḥ devaloke mahīyante puṇyena svena karmaṇā
2.110.13	tato 'nasūyā saṃhṛṣṭā śrutvoktaṃ sītayā vacaḥ śirasy āghrāya covāca maithilīṃ harṣayanty uta
2.110.14	niyamair vividhair āptaṃ tapo hi mahad asti me tat saṃśritya balaṃ sīte chandaye tvāṃ śucivrate
2.110.15	upapannaṃ ca yuktaṃ ca vacanaṃ tava maithili prītā cāsmy ucitaṃ kiṃ te karavāṇi bravīhi me kṛtam ity abravīt sītā tapobalasamanvitām
2.110.16	sā tv evam uktā dharmajñā tayā prītatarābhavat saphalaṃ ca praharṣaṃ te hanta sīte karomy aham
2.110.17	idaṃ divyaṃ varaṃ mālyaṃ vastram ābharaṇāni ca aṅgarāgaṃ ca vaidehi mahārham anulepanam
2.110.18	mayā dattam idaṃ sīte tava gātrāṇi śobhayet anurūpam asaṃkliṣṭaṃ nityam eva bhaviṣyati
2.110.19	aṅgarāgeṇa divyena liptāṅgī janakātmaje śobhayiṣyāmi bhartāraṃ yathā śrīr viṣṇum avyayam
2.110.20	sā vastram aṅgarāgaṃ ca bhūṣaṇāni srajas tathā maithilī pratijagrāha prītidānam anuttamam
2.110.21	pratigṛhya ca tat sītā prītidānaṃ yaśasvinī śliṣṭāñjalipuṭā dhīrā samupāsta tapodhanām
2.110.22	tathā sītām upāsīnām anasūyā dṛḍhavratā vacanaṃ praṣṭum ārebhe kathāṃ kāṃ cid anupriyām
2.110.23	svayaṃvare kila prāptā tvam anena yaśasvinā rāghaveṇeti me sīte kathā śrutim upāgatā
2.110.24	tāṃ kathāṃ śrotum icchāmi vistareṇa ca maithili yathānubhūtaṃ kārtsnyena tan me tvaṃ vaktum arhasi
2.110.25	evam uktā tu sā sītā tāṃ tato dharmacāriṇīm śrūyatām iti coktvā vai kathayām āsa tāṃ kathām
2.110.26	mithilādhipatir vīro janako nāma dharmavit kṣatradharmaṇy abhirato nyāyataḥ śāsti medinīm
2.110.27	tasya lāṅgalahastasya karṣataḥ kṣetramaṇḍalam ahaṃ kilotthitā bhittvā jagatīṃ nṛpateḥ sutā
2.110.28	sa māṃ dṛṣṭvā narapatir muṣṭivikṣepatatparaḥ pāṃśu guṇṭhita sarvāṅgīṃ vismito janako 'bhavat
2.110.29	anapatyena ca snehād aṅkam āropya ca svayam mameyaṃ tanayety uktvā sneho mayi nipātitaḥ
2.110.30	antarikṣe ca vāg uktāpratimā mānuṣī kila evam etan narapate dharmeṇa tanayā tava
2.110.31	tataḥ prahṛṣṭo dharmātmā pitā me mithilādhipaḥ avāpto vipulām ṛddhiṃ mām avāpya narādhipaḥ
2.110.32	dattvā cāsmīṣṭavad devyai jyeṣṭhāyai puṇyakarmaṇā tayā saṃbhāvitā cāsmi snigdhayā mātṛsauhṛdāt
2.110.33	patisaṃyogasulabhaṃ vayo dṛṣṭvā tu me pitā cintām abhyagamad dīno vittanāśād ivādhanaḥ
2.110.34	sadṛśāc cāpakṛṣṭāc ca loke kanyāpitā janāt pradharṣaṇām avāpnoti śakreṇāpi samo bhuvi
2.110.35	tāṃ dharṣaṇām adūrasthāṃ saṃdṛśyātmani pārthivaḥ cinntārṇavagataḥ pāraṃ nāsasādāplavo yatha
2.110.36	ayonijāṃ hi māṃ jñātvā nādhyagacchat sa cintayan sadṛśaṃ cānurūpaṃ ca mahīpālaḥ patiṃ mama
2.110.37	tasya buddhir iyaṃ jātā cintayānasya saṃtatam svayaṃ varaṃ tanūjāyāḥ kariṣyāmīti dhīmataḥ
2.110.38	mahāyajñe tadā tasya varuṇena mahātmanā dattaṃ dhanurvaraṃ prītyā tūṇī cākṣayya sāyakau
2.110.39	asaṃcālyaṃ manuṣyaiś ca yatnenāpi ca gauravāt tan na śaktā namayituṃ svapneṣv api narādhipāḥ
2.110.40	tad dhanuḥ prāpya me pitrā vyāhṛtaṃ satyavādinā samavāye narendrāṇāṃ pūrvam āmantrya pārthivān
2.110.41	idaṃ ca dhanur udyamya sajyaṃ yaḥ kurute naraḥ tasya me duhitā bhāryā bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ
2.110.42	tac ca dṛṣṭvā dhanuḥśreṣṭhaṃ gauravād girisaṃnibham abhivādya nṛpā jagmur aśaktās tasya tolane
2.110.43	sudīrghasya tu kālasya rāghavo 'yaṃ mahādyutiḥ viśvāmitreṇa sahito yajñaṃ draṣṭuṃ samāgataḥ
2.110.44	lakṣmaṇena saha bhrātrā rāmaḥ satyaparākramaḥ viśvāmitras tu dharmātmā mama pitrā supūjitaḥ
2.110.45	provāca pitaraṃ tatra rāghavo rāmalakṣmaṇau sutau daśarathasyemau dhanurdarśanakāṅkṣiṇau ity uktas tena vipreṇa tad dhanuḥ samupānayat
2.110.46	nimeṣāntaramātreṇa tad ānamya sa vīryavān jyāṃ samāropya jhaṭiti pūrayām āsa vīryavān
2.110.47	tena pūrayatā vegān madhye bhagnaṃ dvidhā dhanuḥ tasya śabdo 'bhavad bhīmaḥ patitasyāśaner iva
2.110.48	tato 'haṃ tatra rāmāya pitrā satyābhisaṃdhinā udyatā dātum udyamya jalabhājanam uttamam
2.110.49	dīyamānāṃ na tu tadā pratijagrāha rāghavaḥ avijñāya pituś chandam ayodhyādhipateḥ prabhoḥ
2.110.50	tataḥ śvaśuram āmantrya vṛddhaṃ daśarathaṃ nṛpam mama pitrā ahaṃ dattā rāmāya viditātmane
2.110.51	mama caivānujā sādhvī ūrmilā priyadarśanā bhāryārthe lakṣmaṇasyāpi dattā pitrā mama svayam
2.110.52	evaṃ dattāsmi rāmāya tadā tasmin svayaṃ vare anuraktā ca dharmeṇa patiṃ vīryavatāṃ varam
2.111.1	anasūyā tu dharmajñā śrutvā tāṃ mahatīṃ kathām paryaṣvajata bāhubhyāṃ śirasy āghrāya maithilīm
2.111.2	vyaktākṣarapadaṃ citraṃ bhāṣitaṃ madhuraṃ tvayā yathā svayaṃvaraṃ vṛttaṃ tat sarvaṃ hi śrutaṃ mayā
2.111.3	rame 'haṃ kathayā te tu dṛḍhaṃ madhurabhāṣiṇi ravir astaṃ gataḥ śrīmān upohya rajanīṃ śivām
2.111.4	divasaṃ prati kīrṇānām āhārārthaṃ patatriṇām saṃdhyākāle nilīnānāṃ nidrārthaṃ śrūyate dhvaniḥ
2.111.5	ete cāpy abhiṣekārdrā munayaḥ phalaśodhanāḥ sahitā upavartante salilāplutavalkalāḥ
2.111.6	ṛṣīṇām agnihotreṣu huteṣu vidhipurvakam kapotāṅgāruṇo dhūmo dṛśyate pavanoddhataḥ
2.111.7	alpaparṇā hi taravo ghanībhūtāḥ samantataḥ viprakṛṣṭe 'pi ye deśe na prakāśanti vai diśaḥ
2.111.8	rajanī rasasattvāni pracaranti samantataḥ tapovanamṛgā hy ete veditīrtheṣu śerate
2.111.9	saṃpravṛttā niśā sīte nakṣatrasamalaṃkṛtā jyotsnā prāvaraṇaś candro dṛśyate 'bhyudito 'mbare
2.111.10	gamyatām anujānāmi rāmasyānucarī bhava kathayantyā hi madhuraṃ tvayāhaṃ paritoṣitā
2.111.11	alaṃkuru ca tāvat tvaṃ pratyakṣaṃ mama maithili prītiṃ janaya me vatsa divyālaṃkāraśobhinī
2.111.12	sā tadā samalaṃkṛtya sītā surasutopamā praṇamya śirasā tasyai rāmaṃ tv abhimukhī yayau
2.111.13	tathā tu bhūṣitāṃ sītāṃ dadarśa vadatāṃ varaḥ rāghavaḥ prītidānena tapasvinyā jaharṣa ca
2.111.14	nyavedayat tataḥ sarvaṃ sītā rāmāya maithilī prītidānaṃ tapasvinyā vasanābharaṇasrajām
2.111.15	prahṛṣṭas tv abhavad rāmo lakṣmaṇaś ca mahārathaḥ maithilyāḥ satkriyāṃ dṛṣṭvā mānuṣeṣu sudurlabhām
2.111.16	tatas tāṃ sarvarīṃ prītaḥ puṇyāṃ śaśinibhānanaḥ arcitas tāpasaiḥ siddhair uvāsa raghunandanaḥ
2.111.17	tasyāṃ rātryāṃ vyatītāyām abhiṣicya hutāgnikān āpṛcchetāṃ naravyāghrau tāpasān vanagocarān
2.111.18	tāv ūcus te vanacarās tāpasā dharmacāriṇaḥ vanasya tasya saṃcāraṃ rākṣasaiḥ samabhiplutam
2.111.19	eṣa panthā maharṣīṇāṃ phalāny āharatāṃ vane anena tu vanaṃ durgaṃ gantuṃ rāghava te kṣamam
2.111.20	itīva taiḥ prāñjalibhis tapasvibhir dvijaiḥ kṛtasvastyayanaḥ paraṃtapaḥ vanaṃ sabhāryaḥ praviveśa rāghavaḥ salakṣmaṇaḥ sūrya ivābhramaṇḍalam
